## system

### `archicad_status`

Checks the connection: lists running Archicad instances (port, version, build, language), which one is active, and whether the Claude Connector add-on (full create/modify power) and Tapir add-on are available. Call this first if anything fails.

### `select_archicad_instance`

When several Archicad instances run, switches all following tool calls to the instance listening on `port` (see archicad_status).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `port` | integer | yes |  |

### `get_connector_guide`

Returns the full usage guide for this connector: units, coordinate system, element references, workflows (building a model, documentation, schedules), gotchas (localized library names, top-linked walls, undo), and which tool to use for what. Read it before complex tasks.

### `list_addon_commands`

Lists every JSON command implemented by the Claude Connector add-on (namespace ClaudeConnector) with descriptions. Useful together with execute_addon_command for features without a dedicated tool.

### `execute_json_api_command`

Escape hatch: runs any official Archicad JSON API command verbatim, e.g. command 'API.GetNavigatorItemTree' with parameters {navigatorTreeId: {type: 'ProjectMap'}}. Prefer the dedicated tools; use this for commands without one. Reference: https://archicadapi.graphisoft.com/JSONInterfaceDocumentation/

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `command` | string | yes | Command name, e.g. 'API.GetElementsByType' (the 'API.' prefix is optional) |
| `parameters` | object |  | Command parameters object |

### `execute_addon_command`

Escape hatch: runs any add-on JSON command through API.ExecuteAddOnCommand. Default namespace is 'ClaudeConnector' (see list_addon_commands); other installed add-ons also work, e.g. namespace 'TapirCommand'.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `command` | string | yes | Add-on command name, e.g. 'GetElementDetails' |
| `parameters` | object |  | Command parameters object |
| `namespace` | string |  | Command namespace (default ClaudeConnector) |


## elements

### `create_elements`

Creates elements of ANY supported type in one undo step. Each item is {type: 'Wall'|'Column'|'Beam'|'Slab'|'Roof'|'Shell'|'Mesh'|'Zone'|'Window'|'Door'|'Skylight'|'Opening'|'Object'|'Lamp'|'Line'|'Arc'|'Circle'|'PolyLine'|'Spline'|'Hatch'|'Text'|'Label'|'Dimension'|'LevelDimension'|'Hotspot'|'Morph'|'CurtainWall'|..., ...fields}. The fields are the same as in the type-specific create_* tools (prefer those: they document every field). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | object[] | yes | Element specs, each with a 'type' |
| `elements[].type` | string | yes |  |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `get_element_details`

Returns everything known about elements: common fields (guid, type, storyIndex, layer, elementId, group/hotlink, renovation status, lock) plus type-specific details (geometry, heights, structure/materials, library part & key params, relations such as the openings of a wall). Works for all element types.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | string \\| object[] | yes | Element GUIDs |

### `modify_elements`

Changes fields of existing elements in one undo step. Each item is {guid, ...fields to change}; the field names are the same as the type's create_* tool (e.g. a wall: height, thickness, begin, end, composite; any element: layer, storyIndex, renovationStatus, elementId). Only the given fields change. Returns [{guid} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | object[] | yes | Patches: {guid, field: newValue, ...} |
| `elements[].guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `undoName` | string |  |  |

### `get_supported_element_types`

Lists every Archicad element type and whether this connector can create it, read its details, and modify it.


## walls

### `create_walls`

Creates straight, curved, trapezoid or slanted walls (one undo step). Coordinates in meters, angles in degrees, on the given story (default: current story). Unspecified settings come from the Wall tool defaults. Chain walls end-to-start so Archicad joins the corners. Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `walls` | object[] | yes |  |
| `walls[].begin` | object | yes | Start point of the wall reference line |
| `walls[].end` | object | yes | End point of the wall reference line |
| `walls[].height` | number |  | Height in m. Giving a height unlinks the wall top unless topLinkedStory is also given |
| `walls[].thickness` | number |  | Thickness in m (ignored for composite walls: the composite defines it) |
| `walls[].endThickness` | number |  | Thickness at the end point; makes a trapezoid wall |
| `walls[].bottomOffset` | number |  | Bottom elevation relative to the home story level (m); the wall then spans bottomOffset .. bottomOffset + height |
| `walls[].topLinkedStory` | integer |  | Link the top to the story N levels above the home story (0 = unlinked, use height) |
| `walls[].topOffset` | number |  | Offset from the linked top story (m, e.g. -0.3 under the slab) |
| `walls[].arcAngle` | number |  | Curved wall: central angle in degrees (0 = straight; sign sets the bulge side) |
| `walls[].referenceLine` | "Outside" \\| "Center" \\| "Inside" \\| "CoreOutside" \\| "CoreCenter" \\| "CoreInside" |  | Where the reference line (begin→end) sits in the wall section (verified live, with flipped false = the create default): 'Outside' = the reference line is the wall's outer face and the body lies to the RIGHT of begin→end — so draw exterior walls CLOCKWISE and the walls stay inside the outline; 'Inside' = body to the LEFT; 'Center' = centered. Core* variants refer to the core skin of composite walls |
| `walls[].flipped` | boolean |  | Mirror the wall to the other side of its reference line (default false on create, regardless of the Wall tool default): with 'Outside' the body then lies to the LEFT |
| `walls[].offset` | number |  | Offset of the wall body from the reference line (m) |
| `walls[].buildingMaterial` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Basic structure: building material name/index |
| `walls[].buildingMaterial.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `walls[].composite` | any |  | Composite structure: composite name/index (sets layered skins and total thickness) |
| `walls[].profile` | any |  | Complex profile name/index |
| `walls[].refSurface` | any \\| boolean |  | Override surface on the reference side (false = remove override) |
| `walls[].oppSurface` | any \\| boolean |  | Override surface on the opposite side |
| `walls[].sideSurface` | any \\| boolean |  | Override surface on the wall ends |
| `walls[].zoneRelation` | "Boundary" \\| "ReduceArea" \\| "None" |  | How the wall affects zone boundaries |
| `walls[].slantAlpha` | number |  | Slanted wall: angle of the first face in degrees (90 = vertical) |
| `walls[].slantBeta` | number |  | Trapezoid/double-slanted wall: angle of the second face in degrees |
| `walls[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `walls[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `walls[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `walls[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `walls[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |


## project

### `get_project_info`

Returns facts about the open project and Archicad: projectName, untitled (never saved), file {path, fileName, folder, fileType (SoloProject/Archive/Template...), exists, writable}, teamwork + teamworkInfo, current window/database (type, name) and current story (index, name, level), application {mainVersion, buildNumber, language, jsonApiPort...}, special folders (temporary, templates, userDocuments, application, projectTemporary...). Pass includeTemplates: true to also list installed .tpl templates (for new_project). Call this before save/open/new/close operations.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `includeTemplates` | boolean |  | Also scan the templates/defaults folders and return 'templates': [.tpl paths] (slower) |

### `get_project_info_fields`

Lists the Project Info fields (File > Info > Project Info), i.e. the project autotexts used in title blocks and layouts: [{name (localized UI label, e.g. Russian), key (database key such as 'PROJECTNAME', 'CLIENT', 'autotext-<GUID>' for custom fields), value, category}]. Categories: Fixed = built-in project fields, Custom = user-added fields, Other = values computed by Archicad (dates, layout/drawing data; usually not settable). Use the key with set_project_info_fields.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `category` | "Fixed" \\| "Custom" \\| "Other" \\| "All" |  | Filter by category (default All) |
| `search` | string |  | Case-insensitive substring matched against name, key and value |
| `nonEmptyOnly` | boolean |  | Only fields with a non-empty value |

### `set_project_info_fields`

Sets Project Info field values (shown by autotexts in title blocks/stamps). Identify each field by its database key (preferred; from get_project_info_fields) or by its exact localized name. createIfMissing: true adds a CUSTOM field for names that do not exist. Returns per-item results [{name, key, value, category, created?} | {error}]. Delete custom fields with delete_project_info_fields.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fields` | object[] | yes |  |
| `fields[].key` | string |  | Database key, e.g. 'PROJECTNAME', 'CLIENT', 'autotext-...' (see get_project_info_fields) |
| `fields[].name` | string |  | Localized field name as shown in Project Info (used when no key is given) |
| `fields[].value` | string\|number | yes | New value (text; numbers are converted to text). "" clears the field |
| `createIfMissing` | boolean |  | Create a custom Project Info field when 'name' matches no existing field (default false) |

### `delete_project_info_fields`

Deletes CUSTOM Project Info fields (category Custom) by database key or name. Built-in fields cannot be deleted (set their value to "" instead). Returns [{key, deleted} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fields` | string[] | yes | Keys ('autotext-<GUID>') or exact names of custom fields |

### `save_project`

Saves the open project to its own file (File > Save). Fails for untitled (never saved) projects — use save_project_as — and for read-only projects. Returns {saved, project}.

### `save_project_as`

Saves the project under a new file (File > Save As). Afterwards the open project refers to the NEW file. Formats: pln (solo project), pla (archive incl. library parts), tpl (template), pln25/pla25 (Archicad 25 = previous version). The format defaults to the path extension; a missing extension is added. For IFC/DWG/PDF/image exports use the export tools instead. Returns {saved, format, file, project}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Absolute target path on the Archicad machine, e.g. '/Users/me/Projects/House.pln' |
| `format` | "pln" \\| "pla" \\| "tpl" \\| "pln25" \\| "pla25" |  | File format (default: from the extension, else pln) |
| `overwrite` | boolean |  | Replace an existing file (default false: fails if the file exists) |
| `createFolders` | boolean |  | Create missing parent folders (default false) |
| `archive` | object |  | Options for pla/pla25 archives |
| `archive.includeTextures` | boolean |  | default true |
| `archive.includeBackgroundPictures` | boolean |  | default true |
| `archive.includePropertyObjects` | boolean |  | Include property library parts (default true) |
| `archive.includeAllLibraryParts` | boolean |  | Include the full loaded library, not only used parts (default false) |
| `archive.picturesInTIFF` | boolean |  | Store pictures as TIFF for cross-platform use (default false) |

### `open_project`

Opens a project file (.pln, .pla archive, .tpl template → untitled project, .bpn backup), CLOSING the current project. You must decide about unsaved changes: saveFirst: true or discardChanges: true. BIMcloud/Teamwork projects cannot be opened by path. Opening can take minutes and Archicad may show dialogs (e.g. missing libraries). Returns {opened, project}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Absolute path of the file to open |
| `saveFirst` | boolean |  | Save the current project in place before continuing (fails for untitled projects: use save_project_as first) |
| `discardChanges` | boolean |  | Continue WITHOUT saving: unsaved changes of the current project are lost. Exactly one of saveFirst / discardChanges must be true |
| `archiveLibraryFolder` | string |  | For .pla archives: folder where the embedded library is extracted (optional) |

### `new_project`

Creates a new untitled project, CLOSING the current one: from a template (.tpl path — list installed ones with get_project_info {includeTemplates: true}) or with File > New (reset: true = New & Reset, i.e. default settings). You must decide about unsaved changes: saveFirst: true or discardChanges: true. Returns {created, project}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `template` | string |  | Absolute path of a .tpl template to start from |
| `reset` | boolean |  | Without template: New & Reset (all settings back to defaults). Default false |
| `saveFirst` | boolean |  | Save the current project in place before continuing (fails for untitled projects: use save_project_as first) |
| `discardChanges` | boolean |  | Continue WITHOUT saving: unsaved changes of the current project are lost. Exactly one of saveFirst / discardChanges must be true |

### `close_project`

Closes the current project. WARNING: the Archicad JSON API only listens while a project is open, so after this NO tool works until a project is opened by hand in Archicad. Prefer open_project/new_project to switch projects. Requires confirm: true and saveFirst or discardChanges.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `confirm` | boolean | yes | Must be true |
| `saveFirst` | boolean |  | Save the current project in place before continuing (fails for untitled projects: use save_project_as first) |
| `discardChanges` | boolean |  | Continue WITHOUT saving: unsaved changes of the current project are lost. Exactly one of saveFirst / discardChanges must be true |

### `quit_archicad`

Quits Archicad. Without saveFirst: true, unsaved changes are DISCARDED. After quitting no tool works until Archicad is started again. Requires confirm: true.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `confirm` | boolean | yes | Must be true |
| `saveFirst` | boolean |  | Save the project in place first (fails — and does not quit — for untitled/read-only projects) |

### `get_preferences`

Reads Project Preferences: workingUnits, dimensions (display formats per dimension type), calculationUnits, calculationRules, referenceLevels, legacy, zones, imagingAndCalculation, floorPlanCutPlane, layouts, dataSafety (temporary folder) and environment switches (autoIntersect, autoGroup, suspendGroups, autoTextEnabled, exportTolerance). Units shown here only affect display; the connector always uses meters/degrees. Field names are the ones set_preferences accepts.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sections` | "workingUnits" \\| "dimensions" \\| "calculationUnits" \\| "calculationRules" \\| "referenceLevels" \\| "legacy" \\| "zones" \\| "imagingAndCalculation" \\| "floorPlanCutPlane" \\| "layouts" \\| "dataSafety" \\| "environment"[] |  | Sections to read (default: all) |

### `set_preferences`

Changes Project Preferences (only the given sections/fields; read the current values with get_preferences first). Examples: {workingUnits: {lengthUnit: 'Millimeter', lengthDecimals: 0}}, {dimensions: {linear: {unit: 'Centimeter', decimals: 1}}}, {floorPlanCutPlane: {cutHeight: 1.2}}, {environment: {autoIntersect: false}}. Returns per-section {ok, value} | {error}. dataSafety is read-only. In Teamwork, Project Preferences must be reserved.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `workingUnits` | object |  | Working units (display only — the API always works in meters/degrees) |
| `workingUnits.lengthUnit` | "Meter" \\| "Decimeter" \\| "Centimeter" \\| "Millimeter" \\| "FootFracInch" \\| "FootDecInch" \\| "DecFoot" \\| "FracInch" \\| "DecInch" \\| "Kilometer" \\| "Yard" |  | Model unit (Options > Project Preferences > Working Units) |
| `workingUnits.areaUnit` | "SquareMeter" \\| "SquareKilometer" \\| "SquareDecimeter" \\| "SquareCentimeter" \\| "SquareMillimeter" \\| "SquareFoot" \\| "SquareInch" \\| "SquareYard" |  | Area unit |
| `workingUnits.volumeUnit` | "CubicMeter" \\| "CubicKilometer" \\| "Liter" \\| "CubicCentimeter" \\| "CubicMillimeter" \\| "CubicFoot" \\| "CubicInch" \\| "CubicYard" \\| "Gallon" |  | Volume unit |
| `workingUnits.angleUnit` | "DecimalDegree" \\| "DegreeMinSec" \\| "Grad" \\| "Radian" \\| "Surveyors" |  | Angle unit |
| `workingUnits.lengthDecimals` | integer |  |  |
| `workingUnits.areaDecimals` | any |  |  |
| `workingUnits.volumeDecimals` | any |  |  |
| `workingUnits.angleDecimals` | any |  |  |
| `workingUnits.angleAccuracy` | any |  |  |
| `workingUnits.roundInch` | 1 \\| 2 \\| 4 \\| 8 \\| 16 \\| 32 \\| 64 |  | Fractional inch rounding denominator (1/N inch), only used by inch units |
| `dimensions` | object |  | Dimension display formats (Project Preferences > Dimensions) |
| `dimensions.standard` | string \\| integer \\| string \\| object |  | Select a Dimension Standard attribute (index or localized name, list with get_attributes {type: 'DimensionStandard'}) whose formats become active, or 'Custom'. While a standard is selected Archicad ignores changed formats, so format changes switch to Custom settings automatically (reported in 'note') |
| `dimensions.linear` | object |  | Linear dimensions |
| `dimensions.linear.unit` | any |  | Length unit |
| `dimensions.linear.decimals` | any |  | Number of decimals 0-4 |
| `dimensions.linear.roundInch` | any |  | Fractional inch rounding denominator (1/N inch), only used by inch units |
| `dimensions.linear.extraAccuracy` | "Off" \\| "Small5" \\| "Small25" \\| "Small1" \\| "Small01" \\| "Fractions" |  | Extra accuracy shown as small digits after the decimals (Off = none; Fractions only for inch units) |
| `dimensions.linear.hideZeroDecimals` | boolean |  | Hide trailing zero decimals |
| `dimensions.linear.showZeroWhole` | boolean |  | Show a leading zero for values below 1 (e.g. 0.5 instead of .5) |
