| `slabs[].coverFillFromSurface` | boolean |  | Use the vectorial 3D hatch of the top surface as cover fill |
| `slabs[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `slabs[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `slabs[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `slabs[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `slabs[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `modify_roofs`

Changes existing roofs (the class SinglePlane/MultiPlane cannot change). SinglePlane: polygon, pivotLine, slopeAngle, risesToLeft, edges. MultiPlane: pivotPolygon, slopeAngle, levels, eavesOverhang, pivotEdges (per-plane pitch / gable end / overhang; edge indices of the stored pivot polygon as returned by get_element_details). Both: level, thickness, structure, surfaces, edge trim, floor plan attributes. Fields as in create_roofs. Only the given fields change; the others keep their values. Each item must be an element of this type (checked first; other items still run). Returns [{guid} | {error}] in input order, one undo step.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `roofs` | object[] | yes | Patches {guid, ...fields to change} |
| `roofs[].guid` | string | yes | Roof GUID |
| `roofs[].polygon` | object[] \\| object |  | SinglePlane: new outline (replaces the shape; per-edge data is reset) |
| `roofs[].polygon.points` | any[] | yes | Vertices in order, do NOT repeat the first point at the end |
| `roofs[].polygon.arcs` | object[] |  | Curved edges |
| `roofs[].polygon.holes` | object[] |  | Inner holes, each {points, arcs?} |
| `roofs[].pivotPolygon` | any |  | MultiPlane: new pivot polygon |
| `roofs[].level` | number |  | Elevation of the pivot line / pivot polygon relative to the home story level (m), e.g. the wall top height |
| `roofs[].slopeAngle` | number |  | Roof pitch in degrees (0-90). Multi-plane: sets a single roof level with this pitch |
| `roofs[].pivotLine` | object |  | SinglePlane: horizontal line the plane pivots around (usually the eaves line), at elevation 'level' |
| `roofs[].pivotLine.begin` | object | yes | 2D point in meters (project coordinates) |
| `roofs[].pivotLine.end` | any | yes |  |
| `roofs[].risesToLeft` | boolean |  | SinglePlane: true = the plane rises on the left side of pivotLine begin→end. Default on create: towards the polygon |
| `roofs[].edges` | object[] |  | SinglePlane: per-edge trim / surface / edge type overrides of the roof outline |
| `roofs[].edges[].contour` | integer |  | 0 = outline (default), 1..N = holes in the order given |
| `roofs[].edges[].index` | integer | yes | Edge index: edge i runs from point i to point i+1 (the last edge closes the contour) |
| `roofs[].edges[].trim` | "Vertical" \\| "Perpendicular" \\| "Horizontal" \\| "CustomAngle" \\| "AlignWithCut" |  | Edge trim: Vertical, Perpendicular (to the element plane), Horizontal, CustomAngle (with an angle), AlignWithCut |
| `roofs[].edges[].angle` | number |  | Custom edge angle in degrees (0-180 exclusive, 90 = vertical edge); implies edgeTrim 'CustomAngle' |
| `roofs[].edges[].surface` | integer \\| string \\| object \\| boolean |  | Surface of this edge (false = structure's surface) |
| `roofs[].edges[].edgeType` | "Undefined" \\| "Ridge" \\| "Valley" \\| "Gable" \\| "Hip" \\| "Eaves" \\| "Peak" \\| "SideWall" \\| "EndWall" \\| "Dome" \\| "Hollow" |  | Roof edge type (used by roof accessories/schedules) |
| `roofs[].levels` | object[] |  | MultiPlane: roof levels from the pivot polygon upwards, e.g. mansard [{angle: 70, height: 2}, {angle: 25}] = 70° up to 2 m above the pivot polygon, then 25° to the ridge |
| `roofs[].levels[].angle` | number | yes | Slope of this roof level in degrees |
| `roofs[].levels[].height` | number |  | Height above the pivot polygon (m) where this level ENDS and the next level begins (the pitch break). Required for every level except the last; the top level always continues to the ridge. Strictly increasing |
| `roofs[].eavesOverhang` | number |  | MultiPlane: eaves overhang beyond the pivot polygon (m); the roof contour is the pivot polygon offset by this |
| `roofs[].arcSegments` | integer |  | MultiPlane: segments per curved pivot edge (resolution by arc) |
| `roofs[].circleSegments` | integer |  | MultiPlane: segments per full circle (resolution by circle) |
| `roofs[].fitSkylightsToCurve` | boolean |  | MultiPlane: place skylights tangentially on curved planes |
| `roofs[].pivotEdges` | object[] |  | MultiPlane: per-plane overrides (slope, gable end, overhang) by pivot polygon edge |
| `roofs[].pivotEdges[].contour` | integer |  | 0 = pivot polygon outline (default), 1..N = holes |
| `roofs[].pivotEdges[].index` | integer | yes | Pivot edge index: edge i runs from pivotPolygon point i to point i+1 |
| `roofs[].pivotEdges[].angle` | number |  | Slope of this roof plane in degrees (all levels) |
| `roofs[].pivotEdges[].angles` | number[] |  | Slope per roof level (index 0 = lowest level) |
| `roofs[].pivotEdges[].gable` | boolean |  | true = vertical gable end on this edge (no roof plane), false = sloped plane |
| `roofs[].pivotEdges[].eavesOverhang` | number |  | Eaves overhang of this plane beyond the pivot edge (m) |
| `roofs[].thickness` | number |  | Thickness in m (ignored for composite structures) |
| `roofs[].buildingMaterial` | any |  | Basic structure: building material name/index (localized — get_attributes type 'BuildingMaterial') |
| `roofs[].composite` | any |  | Composite structure: composite name/index (get_attributes type 'Composite'; its 'usage' must include this element type, e.g. usage.slabs for slabs); the composite defines the layers and total thickness |
| `roofs[].topSurface` | any |  | Top surface override (surface name/index, false = use the structure's surface) |
| `roofs[].bottomSurface` | any |  | Bottom surface override |
| `roofs[].sideSurface` | any |  | Edge/side surface override (applies to all edges; per-edge surfaces via 'edges') |
| `roofs[].surfacesChained` | boolean |  | Chain the surface overrides (Archicad UI chain icon) |
| `roofs[].edgeTrim` | any |  | Default trim of the edges |
| `roofs[].edgeAngle` | any |  | Custom edge angle in degrees (0-180 exclusive, 90 = vertical edge); implies edgeTrim 'CustomAngle' |
| `roofs[].connectionBody` | "Editable" \\| "ContoursDown" \\| "PivotLinesDown" \\| "UpwardsExtrusion" \\| "DownwardsExtrusion" |  | Body extension used for connections/trims (Archicad 'Extend body for connections') |
| `roofs[].floorPlanDisplay` | "Projected" \\| "ProjectedWithOverhead" \\| "CutOnly" \\| "OutlinesOnly" \\| "OverheadAll" \\| "CutAll" |  | Floor plan display option |
| `roofs[].viewDepth` | "FloorPlanRange" \\| "AbsoluteLimit" \\| "EntireElement" |  | Show projection: to floor plan range, to absolute limit, entire element |
| `roofs[].contourPen` | integer |  | Pen of the uncut contour on the floor plan |
| `roofs[].contourLineType` | any |  | Line type of the uncut contour (get_attributes type 'Line') |
| `roofs[].cutContourPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut contour |
| `roofs[].cutFillPen` | any \\| boolean |  | Override the cut fill foreground pen (1-255); false = use the structure's pen |
| `roofs[].cutFillBackgroundPen` | any |  | Override the cut fill background pen (1-255); false = use the structure's pen |
| `roofs[].showCoverFill` | boolean |  | Show the floor plan cover fill |
| `roofs[].coverFill` | any |  | Cover fill type name/index (get_attributes type 'Fill') |
| `roofs[].coverFillPen` | any |  | Cover fill foreground pen |
| `roofs[].coverFillBackgroundPen` | any |  | Cover fill background pen |
| `roofs[].storyVisibility` | object |  | Floor plan story visibility of the contour and cover fill (only the given flags change) |
| `roofs[].storyVisibility.onHomeStory` | boolean |  | Show on the home story |
| `roofs[].storyVisibility.allAbove` | boolean |  | Show on all stories above the home story |
| `roofs[].storyVisibility.allBelow` | boolean |  | Show on all stories below the home story |
| `roofs[].storyVisibility.storiesAbove` | integer |  | Show on this many stories above the home story |
| `roofs[].storyVisibility.storiesBelow` | integer |  | Show on this many stories below the home story |
| `roofs[].cutContourLineType` | any |  | Line type of the cut contour |
| `roofs[].overheadLinePen` | any |  | Pen of overhead lines |
| `roofs[].overheadLineType` | any |  | Line type of overhead lines |
| `roofs[].coverFillFromSurface` | boolean |  | Use the fill of the surface (vectorial 3D hatch) as cover fill |
| `roofs[].coverFillAlignedToPivot` | boolean |  | Align the cover fill with the pivot line (roofs) |
| `roofs[].coverFillDistorted` | boolean |  | Distort the cover fill with the slope |
| `roofs[].autoStoryVisibility` | boolean |  | Let Archicad compute the story visibility from the vertical extent |
| `roofs[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `roofs[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `roofs[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `roofs[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `roofs[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `modify_shells`

Changes existing shells (the class Extruded/Revolved/Ruled cannot change): profile / profile2 / closedProfile, placement (begin, extrusion, axisOrigin, profileRotation, basePlane, plane1/plane2), angles, thickness, flipped, structure, surfaces, floor plan attributes. Fields as in create_shells. Only the given fields change; the others keep their values. Each item must be an element of this type (checked first; other items still run). Returns [{guid} | {error}] in input order, one undo step.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `shells` | object[] | yes | Patches {guid, ...fields to change} |
| `shells[].guid` | string | yes | Shell GUID |
| `shells[].profile` | object[] \\| object |  | New profile (replaces the profile; clipping/hole contours are kept) |
| `shells[].profile.points` | any[] | yes |  |
| `shells[].profile.arcs` | object[] |  |  |
| `shells[].closedProfile` | boolean |  | true = closed profile (e.g. a tube); false (default) = open profile (arch, vault, dome) |
| `shells[].flipped` | boolean |  | Apply the thickness to the other side of the reference surface |
| `shells[].level` | number |  | Base level offset of the shell from its home story (m) |
| `shells[].basePlane` | object |  | Advanced: the shell's local coordinate system (read it from get_element_details of an existing shell) |
| `shells[].basePlane.matrix` | number[] | yes | Row-major 3x4 matrix; translation at indices 3, 7, 11 |
| `shells[].defaultEdgeType` | "Undefined" \\| "Ridge" \\| "Valley" \\| "Gable" \\| "Hip" \\| "Eaves" \\| "Peak" \\| "SideWall" \\| "EndWall" \\| "Dome" \\| "Hollow" |  | Default contour edge type |
| `shells[].begin` | any |  | Extruded: start point of the extrusion (m; z relative to the home story, default 0) |
| `shells[].extrusion` | any |  | Extruded: extrusion vector (direction and length, m), e.g. {x: 10, y: 0, z: 0} for a 10 m long vault along X |
| `shells[].profileDirection` | any |  | Extruded, advanced: 2D direction of the profile's x axis |
| `shells[].slantAngle` | number |  | Extruded/Revolved: slant of the extrusion / revolution in degrees |
| `shells[].profilePlaneTilt` | number |  | Extruded: tilt of the profile plane relative to the extrusion vector (degrees) |
| `shells[].beginPlaneTilt` | number |  | Extruded: tilt of the start cap plane (degrees, 90 = perpendicular) |
| `shells[].endPlaneTilt` | number |  | Extruded: tilt of the end cap plane (degrees) |
| `shells[].axisOrigin` | any |  | Revolved: point on the vertical revolution axis (m), default 0,0,0 |
| `shells[].profileRotation` | number |  | Revolved: direction (degrees from +X in plan) of the profile's x axis (radial direction) |
| `shells[].axisBase` | any |  | Revolved, advanced: frame of the revolution axis relative to basePlane (z axis = axis) |
| `shells[].revolutionAngle` | number |  | Revolved: revolution angle in degrees (360 = full dome/tower, default 360 on create) |
| `shells[].beginAngle` | number |  | Revolved: start angle of the revolution in degrees |
| `shells[].distortionAngle` | number |  | Revolved: shear angle in degrees (90 = none) |
| `shells[].segmentedSurfaces` | boolean |  | Revolved: build the surface from planar segments |
| `shells[].arcSegments` | integer |  | Revolved: segments of the revolution arc |
| `shells[].circleSegments` | integer |  | Revolved: segments per full circle |
| `shells[].profile2` | any |  | Ruled: second profile |
| `shells[].plane1` | any |  | Ruled: plane (frame) of the first profile |
| `shells[].plane2` | any |  | Ruled: plane (frame) of the second profile |
| `shells[].morphingRule` | "Paired" \\| "Smooth" |  | Ruled: how the two profiles are connected |
| `shells[].thickness` | number |  | Thickness in m (ignored for composite structures) |
| `shells[].buildingMaterial` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Basic structure: building material name/index (localized — get_attributes type 'BuildingMaterial') |
| `shells[].buildingMaterial.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `shells[].composite` | any |  | Composite structure: composite name/index (get_attributes type 'Composite'; its 'usage' must include this element type, e.g. usage.slabs for slabs); the composite defines the layers and total thickness |
| `shells[].topSurface` | any \\| boolean |  | Top surface override (surface name/index, false = use the structure's surface) |
| `shells[].bottomSurface` | any |  | Bottom surface override |
| `shells[].sideSurface` | any |  | Edge/side surface override (applies to all edges; per-edge surfaces via 'edges') |
| `shells[].surfacesChained` | boolean |  | Chain the surface overrides (Archicad UI chain icon) |
| `shells[].edgeTrim` | "Vertical" \\| "Perpendicular" \\| "Horizontal" \\| "CustomAngle" \\| "AlignWithCut" |  | Default trim of the edges |
| `shells[].edgeAngle` | number |  | Custom edge angle in degrees (0-180 exclusive, 90 = vertical edge); implies edgeTrim 'CustomAngle' |
| `shells[].connectionBody` | "Editable" \\| "ContoursDown" \\| "PivotLinesDown" \\| "UpwardsExtrusion" \\| "DownwardsExtrusion" |  | Body extension used for connections/trims (Archicad 'Extend body for connections') |
| `shells[].floorPlanDisplay` | "Projected" \\| "ProjectedWithOverhead" \\| "CutOnly" \\| "OutlinesOnly" \\| "OverheadAll" \\| "CutAll" |  | Floor plan display option |
| `shells[].viewDepth` | "FloorPlanRange" \\| "AbsoluteLimit" \\| "EntireElement" |  | Show projection: to floor plan range, to absolute limit, entire element |
| `shells[].contourPen` | integer |  | Pen of the uncut contour on the floor plan |
| `shells[].contourLineType` | any |  | Line type of the uncut contour (get_attributes type 'Line') |
| `shells[].cutContourPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut contour |
| `shells[].cutFillPen` | any \\| boolean |  | Override the cut fill foreground pen (1-255); false = use the structure's pen |
| `shells[].cutFillBackgroundPen` | any |  | Override the cut fill background pen (1-255); false = use the structure's pen |
| `shells[].showCoverFill` | boolean |  | Show the floor plan cover fill |
| `shells[].coverFill` | any |  | Cover fill type name/index (get_attributes type 'Fill') |
| `shells[].coverFillPen` | any |  | Cover fill foreground pen |
| `shells[].coverFillBackgroundPen` | any |  | Cover fill background pen |
| `shells[].storyVisibility` | object |  | Floor plan story visibility of the contour and cover fill (only the given flags change) |
| `shells[].storyVisibility.onHomeStory` | boolean |  | Show on the home story |
| `shells[].storyVisibility.allAbove` | boolean |  | Show on all stories above the home story |
| `shells[].storyVisibility.allBelow` | boolean |  | Show on all stories below the home story |
| `shells[].storyVisibility.storiesAbove` | integer |  | Show on this many stories above the home story |
| `shells[].storyVisibility.storiesBelow` | integer |  | Show on this many stories below the home story |
| `shells[].cutContourLineType` | any |  | Line type of the cut contour |
| `shells[].overheadLinePen` | any |  | Pen of overhead lines |
| `shells[].overheadLineType` | any |  | Line type of overhead lines |
| `shells[].coverFillFromSurface` | boolean |  | Use the fill of the surface (vectorial 3D hatch) as cover fill |
| `shells[].coverFillAlignedToPivot` | boolean |  | Align the cover fill with the pivot line (roofs) |
| `shells[].coverFillDistorted` | boolean |  | Distort the cover fill with the slope |
| `shells[].autoStoryVisibility` | boolean |  | Let Archicad compute the story visibility from the vertical extent |
| `shells[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `shells[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `shells[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `shells[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `shells[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `modify_meshes`

Changes existing meshes: polygon with point heights (replaces the outline incl. holes), levelLines ([] removes them), level, skirt, skirtLevel, ridges, building material, surfaces, floor plan attributes. Fields as in create_meshes. Only the given fields change; the others keep their values. Each item must be an element of this type (checked first; other items still run). Returns [{guid} | {error}] in input order, one undo step.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `meshes` | object[] | yes | Patches {guid, ...fields to change} |
| `meshes[].guid` | string | yes | Mesh GUID |
| `meshes[].polygon` | object[] \\| object |  | New outline with heights (replaces the whole polygon incl. holes) |
| `meshes[].polygon.points` | any[] | yes | Vertices in order, do NOT repeat the first point at the end |
| `meshes[].polygon.arcs` | object[] |  | Curved edges |
| `meshes[].polygon.holes` | object[] |  | Inner holes, each {points, arcs?} |
| `meshes[].level` | number |  | Elevation of the mesh base plane relative to the home story (m); point z values are relative to it |
| `meshes[].skirt` | "SolidBody" \\| "SkirtWithoutBottom" \\| "SurfaceOnly" |  | Body type: SolidBody (skirt + bottom), SkirtWithoutBottom, SurfaceOnly |
| `meshes[].skirtLevel` | number |  | Distance of the mesh bottom (skirt) from the base plane (m) |
| `meshes[].ridges` | "AllSharp" \\| "AllSmooth" \\| "UserDefined" |  | Ridge display: all sharp, all smooth, or only user-defined ridges sharp |
| `meshes[].showAllLines` | boolean |  | Show all (also secondary) mesh lines on the floor plan |
| `meshes[].levelLines` | object[] |  | Inner level lines (replace existing ones); [] removes all level lines |
| `meshes[].levelLines[].points` | object[] | yes | Points of the level line; z = height above the mesh level (m) |
| `meshes[].buildingMaterial` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Building material name/index (meshes have no composites) |
| `meshes[].buildingMaterial.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `meshes[].topSurface` | any \\| boolean |  | Top surface override (surface name/index, false = use the structure's surface) |
| `meshes[].bottomSurface` | any |  | Bottom surface override |
| `meshes[].sideSurface` | any |  | Edge/side surface override (applies to all edges; per-edge surfaces via 'edges') |
| `meshes[].surfacesChained` | boolean |  | Chain the surface overrides (Archicad UI chain icon) |
| `meshes[].contourPen` | integer |  | Pen of the uncut contour on the floor plan |
| `meshes[].contourLineType` | any |  | Line type of the uncut contour (get_attributes type 'Line') |
| `meshes[].cutContourPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut contour |
| `meshes[].cutFillPen` | any \\| boolean |  | Override the cut fill foreground pen (1-255); false = use the structure's pen |
| `meshes[].cutFillBackgroundPen` | any |  | Override the cut fill background pen (1-255); false = use the structure's pen |
| `meshes[].showCoverFill` | boolean |  | Show the floor plan cover fill |
| `meshes[].coverFill` | any |  | Cover fill type name/index (get_attributes type 'Fill') |
| `meshes[].coverFillPen` | any |  | Cover fill foreground pen |
| `meshes[].coverFillBackgroundPen` | any |  | Cover fill background pen |
| `meshes[].storyVisibility` | object |  | Floor plan story visibility of the contour and cover fill (only the given flags change) |
| `meshes[].storyVisibility.onHomeStory` | boolean |  | Show on the home story |
| `meshes[].storyVisibility.allAbove` | boolean |  | Show on all stories above the home story |
| `meshes[].storyVisibility.allBelow` | boolean |  | Show on all stories below the home story |
| `meshes[].storyVisibility.storiesAbove` | integer |  | Show on this many stories above the home story |
| `meshes[].storyVisibility.storiesBelow` | integer |  | Show on this many stories below the home story |
| `meshes[].levelLinePen` | any |  | Pen of the level lines |
| `meshes[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `meshes[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `meshes[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `meshes[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `meshes[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |


## openings

### `create_windows`

Places windows into walls (one undo step). Each window needs its host 'wall' GUID and either 'position' (m from the wall's begin point to the window centre along the reference line) or 'point' ({x,y} projected onto the wall). sillHeight = bottom of the window above the wall bottom. Unspecified settings (library part, size, sill, reveal, surfaces) come from the Window tool defaults. Library part names are LOCALIZED: find them with search_library_parts (type Window). Polygonal walls cannot host windows. Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; inspect results with get_element_details (position, location, sill/header height, library part, key GDL params).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `windows` | object[] | yes |  |
| `windows[].wall` | string \\| object | yes | GUID of the host wall (straight, curved or trapezoid; not polygonal). The window is always on the wall's story |
| `windows[].wall.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `windows[].position` | number |  | Distance in m along the wall reference line from the wall's BEGIN point to the window's CENTRE (see positionReference). Must lie within 0..wall length (get_element_details on the wall returns begin/end/length). Give position OR point |
| `windows[].point` | object |  | Plan point {x,y}; the window is centred on its perpendicular projection onto the wall reference line. Give position OR point |
| `windows[].positionReference` | "Center" \\| "Begin" \\| "End" |  | What position/point refers to: the window centre (default), or its edge nearer the wall's begin ('Begin') / end ('End'), e.g. position 0.5 + 'Begin' = 0.5 m gap from the wall start |
| `windows[].sillHeight` | number |  | Sill (parapet) height in m: bottom of the window above the wall bottom (default reference; see verticalAnchor). Doors usually 0 |
| `windows[].width` | number |  | Nominal window width in m (default: tool / library part default) |
| `windows[].height` | number |  | Nominal window height in m (default: tool / library part default) |
| `windows[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | Window library part: exact LOCALIZED name (find it with search_library_parts, type Window), index, or {guid}. Default: the Window tool's current part (with the tool's current parameters). When given, the part starts from its own default GDL parameters (run through its parameter script together with gdlParams, width and height) |
| `windows[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `windows[].emptyHole` | boolean |  | Create only an empty rectangular hole without a library part (create only; not combinable with libraryPart/gdlParams) |
| `windows[].gdlParams` | object |  | GDL parameter values {name: value} (lengths m, angles deg), applied through the part's parameter script. Parameter names are not localized; list them with get_gdl_parameters |
| `windows[].flipped` | boolean |  | Flip the window to the other side of the wall (opening direction / reveal side) |
| `windows[].referenceSideFlipped` | boolean |  | Reference (reveal) side flag; follows 'flipped' unless given explicitly |
| `windows[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror left/right (hinge / handle side of the window) |
| `windows[].anchor` | "Begin" \\| "Center" \\| "End" |  | Which point stays fixed when the width changes: the window edge towards the wall begin, the centre, or the edge towards the wall end |
| `windows[].verticalAnchor` | "SillToWallBottom" \\| "SillToStory" \\| "HeaderToWallBottom" \\| "HeaderToStory" \\| "SillToWallTop" \\| "HeaderToWallTop" |  | What stays fixed vertically when the wall / story changes: the sill or the header, relative to the wall bottom/top or a story |
| `windows[].verticalAnchorStory` | integer \\| string |  | Story index or name for verticalAnchor 'SillToStory' / 'HeaderToStory' (default: the wall's story) |
| `windows[].reveal` | boolean |  | Enable the reveal (opening set back into the wall) |
| `windows[].revealDepth` | number |  | Reveal depth in m: inset of the frame from the reference side of the wall (or of its core, see revealDepthLocation) |
| `windows[].revealDepthLocation` | "WallSide" \\| "Core" |  | revealDepth is measured from the wall side or from the core skin of a composite wall |
| `windows[].jambDepthHead` | number |  | Reveal inset on top (m) |
| `windows[].jambDepthSill` | number |  | Reveal inset at the bottom (m) |
| `windows[].jambDepthLeft` | number |  | Reveal inset on the left (m) |
| `windows[].jambDepthRight` | number |  | Reveal inset on the right (m) |
| `windows[].orientationInSlantedWall` | "FollowWall" \\| "Vertical" |  | In slanted walls: follow the wall plane or stay vertical |
| `windows[].inheritWallCut` | boolean |  | Inherit the wall's gables / trims (cut with the wall) |
| `windows[].subFloorThickness` | number |  | Sub-floor thickness (parapet correction) in m |
| `windows[].surface` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Surface (material) used for the whole object; also switches useObjectSurfaces off (list names with get_attributes type Surface) |
| `windows[].surface.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `windows[].useObjectSurfaces` | boolean |  | true = use the surfaces set in the library part's own GDL parameters |
| `windows[].pen` | integer |  | Contour pen; also switches useObjectPens off |
| `windows[].useObjectPens` | boolean |  | true = use the pens set in the library part's parameters |
| `windows[].lineType` | any |  | Line type; also switches useObjectLineTypes off |
| `windows[].useObjectLineTypes` | boolean |  | true = use the line types set in the library part's parameters |
| `windows[].cutFill` | any |  | Fill of cut (section) parts; also switches useObjectSectionAttributes off |
| `windows[].cutFillPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut fill |
| `windows[].cutFillBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Background pen of the cut fill (0 = transparent) |
| `windows[].cutContourPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut contour |
| `windows[].useObjectSectionAttributes` | boolean |  | true = use the section attributes set in the library part |
| `windows[].cutLineType` | any |  | Line type of cut lines |
| `windows[].overheadLinePen` | any |  | Pen of overhead lines (floor plan display 'OverheadAll') |
| `windows[].overheadLineType` | any |  | Line type of overhead lines |
| `windows[].uncutLinePen` | any |  | Pen of uncut lines |
| `windows[].uncutLineType` | any |  | Line type of uncut lines (display 'OutlinesOnly') |
| `windows[].displayOption` | "Projected" \\| "ProjectedWithOverhead" \\| "CutOnly" \\| "OutlinesOnly" \\| "OverheadAll" \\| "Symbolic" |  | Floor plan display of the opening |
| `windows[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `windows[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `windows[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `create_doors`

Places doors into walls (one undo step). Each door needs its host 'wall' GUID and either 'position' (m from the wall's begin point to the door centre along the reference line) or 'point' ({x,y} projected onto the wall). Use flipped (opening side) and mirrored (hinge side) to orient it; sillHeight is usually 0. Unspecified settings come from the Door tool defaults. Library part names are LOCALIZED: find them with search_library_parts (type Door). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `doors` | object[] | yes |  |
| `doors[].wall` | string \\| object | yes | GUID of the host wall (straight, curved or trapezoid; not polygonal). The door is always on the wall's story |
| `doors[].wall.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `doors[].position` | number |  | Distance in m along the wall reference line from the wall's BEGIN point to the door's CENTRE (see positionReference). Must lie within 0..wall length (get_element_details on the wall returns begin/end/length). Give position OR point |
| `doors[].point` | object |  | Plan point {x,y}; the door is centred on its perpendicular projection onto the wall reference line. Give position OR point |
| `doors[].positionReference` | "Center" \\| "Begin" \\| "End" |  | What position/point refers to: the door centre (default), or its edge nearer the wall's begin ('Begin') / end ('End'), e.g. position 0.5 + 'Begin' = 0.5 m gap from the wall start |
| `doors[].sillHeight` | number |  | Sill (parapet) height in m: bottom of the door above the wall bottom (default reference; see verticalAnchor). Doors usually 0 |
| `doors[].width` | number |  | Nominal door width in m (default: tool / library part default) |
| `doors[].height` | number |  | Nominal door height in m (default: tool / library part default) |
| `doors[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | Door library part: exact LOCALIZED name (find it with search_library_parts, type Door), index, or {guid}. Default: the Door tool's current part (with the tool's current parameters). When given, the part starts from its own default GDL parameters (run through its parameter script together with gdlParams, width and height) |
| `doors[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `doors[].emptyHole` | boolean |  | Create only an empty rectangular hole without a library part (create only; not combinable with libraryPart/gdlParams) |
| `doors[].gdlParams` | object |  | GDL parameter values {name: value} (lengths m, angles deg), applied through the part's parameter script. Parameter names are not localized; list them with get_gdl_parameters |
| `doors[].flipped` | boolean |  | Flip the door to the other side of the wall (opening direction / reveal side) |
| `doors[].referenceSideFlipped` | boolean |  | Reference (reveal) side flag; follows 'flipped' unless given explicitly |
| `doors[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror left/right (hinge / handle side of the door) |
| `doors[].anchor` | "Begin" \\| "Center" \\| "End" |  | Which point stays fixed when the width changes: the door edge towards the wall begin, the centre, or the edge towards the wall end |
| `doors[].verticalAnchor` | "SillToWallBottom" \\| "SillToStory" \\| "HeaderToWallBottom" \\| "HeaderToStory" \\| "SillToWallTop" \\| "HeaderToWallTop" |  | What stays fixed vertically when the wall / story changes: the sill or the header, relative to the wall bottom/top or a story |
| `doors[].verticalAnchorStory` | integer \\| string |  | Story index or name for verticalAnchor 'SillToStory' / 'HeaderToStory' (default: the wall's story) |
| `doors[].reveal` | boolean |  | Enable the reveal (opening set back into the wall) |
| `doors[].revealDepth` | number |  | Reveal depth in m: inset of the frame from the reference side of the wall (or of its core, see revealDepthLocation) |
| `doors[].revealDepthLocation` | "WallSide" \\| "Core" |  | revealDepth is measured from the wall side or from the core skin of a composite wall |
| `doors[].jambDepthHead` | number |  | Reveal inset on top (m) |
| `doors[].jambDepthSill` | number |  | Reveal inset at the bottom (m) |
| `doors[].jambDepthLeft` | number |  | Reveal inset on the left (m) |
| `doors[].jambDepthRight` | number |  | Reveal inset on the right (m) |
| `doors[].orientationInSlantedWall` | "FollowWall" \\| "Vertical" |  | In slanted walls: follow the wall plane or stay vertical |
| `doors[].inheritWallCut` | boolean |  | Inherit the wall's gables / trims (cut with the wall) |
| `doors[].subFloorThickness` | number |  | Sub-floor thickness (parapet correction) in m |
| `doors[].surface` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Surface (material) used for the whole object; also switches useObjectSurfaces off (list names with get_attributes type Surface) |
| `doors[].surface.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `doors[].useObjectSurfaces` | boolean |  | true = use the surfaces set in the library part's own GDL parameters |
| `doors[].pen` | integer |  | Contour pen; also switches useObjectPens off |
| `doors[].useObjectPens` | boolean |  | true = use the pens set in the library part's parameters |
| `doors[].lineType` | any |  | Line type; also switches useObjectLineTypes off |
| `doors[].useObjectLineTypes` | boolean |  | true = use the line types set in the library part's parameters |
| `doors[].cutFill` | any |  | Fill of cut (section) parts; also switches useObjectSectionAttributes off |
| `doors[].cutFillPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut fill |
| `doors[].cutFillBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Background pen of the cut fill (0 = transparent) |
| `doors[].cutContourPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut contour |
| `doors[].useObjectSectionAttributes` | boolean |  | true = use the section attributes set in the library part |
| `doors[].cutLineType` | any |  | Line type of cut lines |
| `doors[].overheadLinePen` | any |  | Pen of overhead lines (floor plan display 'OverheadAll') |
| `doors[].overheadLineType` | any |  | Line type of overhead lines |
| `doors[].uncutLinePen` | any |  | Pen of uncut lines |
| `doors[].uncutLineType` | any |  | Line type of uncut lines (display 'OutlinesOnly') |
| `doors[].displayOption` | "Projected" \\| "ProjectedWithOverhead" \\| "CutOnly" \\| "OutlinesOnly" \\| "OverheadAll" \\| "Symbolic" |  | Floor plan display of the opening |
| `doors[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `doors[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `doors[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `create_skylights`

Places skylights into roofs or shells (one undo step). Each skylight needs the host 'owner' (Roof or Shell GUID) and 'point' ({x,y}: plan position of its anchor on the roof). Size, library part and parameters default to the Skylight tool settings; library part names are LOCALIZED (search_library_parts, type Skylight). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `skylights` | object[] | yes |  |
| `skylights[].owner` | string \\| object | yes | GUID of the host Roof or Shell. The skylight is always on the host's story |
| `skylights[].owner.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `skylights[].point` | object | yes | Plan position {x,y} of the skylight anchor point; must lie on the roof/shell in plan |
| `skylights[].anchorLevel` | number |  | Absolute Z (from Project Zero) of the anchor point. Normally omit it: for single-plane roofs it is computed on the roof's pivot plane at 'point'; otherwise the Skylight tool default is kept (check the result with get_element_details) |
| `skylights[].anchor` | "BottomCenter" \\| "BottomLeft" \\| "BottomRight" \\| "TopCenter" \\| "TopLeft" \\| "TopRight" |  | Which point of the skylight is placed at 'point' (bottom = downslope edge) |
| `skylights[].fixMode` | "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" |  | What is kept when the roof changes: the plan (horizontal) position or the vertical position |
| `skylights[].azimuthAngle` | number |  | Rotation in the horizontal plane in degrees, measured clockwise |
| `skylights[].pivotVertexId` | integer |  | Multi-plane roofs only: pivot polygon vertex ID the skylight belongs to (advanced; normally omit) |
| `skylights[].width` | number |  | Skylight width in m |
| `skylights[].height` | number |  | Skylight length (in the slope direction) in m |
| `skylights[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | Skylight library part: exact LOCALIZED name (find it with search_library_parts, type Skylight), index, or {guid}. Default: the Skylight tool's current part (with the tool's current parameters). When given, the part starts from its own default GDL parameters (run through its parameter script together with gdlParams, width and height) |
| `skylights[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `skylights[].emptyHole` | boolean |  | Create only an empty hole without a library part (create only) |
| `skylights[].gdlParams` | object |  | GDL parameter values {name: value} (lengths m, angles deg); list names with get_gdl_parameters |
| `skylights[].flipped` | boolean |  | Flip the skylight (mirror about its x axis) |
| `skylights[].referenceSideFlipped` | boolean |  | Reference side flag; follows 'flipped' unless given |
| `skylights[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror left/right |
| `skylights[].verticalAnchor` | "ToRoofPivot" \\| "ToStory" \\| "ToShellBase" |  | Vertical reference of the skylight anchor: the roof pivot line, a story, or the shell base |
| `skylights[].verticalAnchorStory` | integer \\| string |  | Story index or name for verticalAnchor 'ToStory' (default: the host's story) |
| `skylights[].subFloorThickness` | number |  | Sub-floor thickness in m |
| `skylights[].surface` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Surface (material) used for the whole object; also switches useObjectSurfaces off (list names with get_attributes type Surface) |
| `skylights[].surface.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `skylights[].useObjectSurfaces` | boolean |  | true = use the surfaces set in the library part's own GDL parameters |
| `skylights[].pen` | integer |  | Contour pen; also switches useObjectPens off |
| `skylights[].useObjectPens` | boolean |  | true = use the pens set in the library part's parameters |
| `skylights[].lineType` | any |  | Line type; also switches useObjectLineTypes off |
| `skylights[].useObjectLineTypes` | boolean |  | true = use the line types set in the library part's parameters |
| `skylights[].cutFill` | any |  | Fill of cut (section) parts; also switches useObjectSectionAttributes off |
| `skylights[].cutFillPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut fill |
| `skylights[].cutFillBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Background pen of the cut fill (0 = transparent) |
| `skylights[].cutContourPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut contour |
| `skylights[].useObjectSectionAttributes` | boolean |  | true = use the section attributes set in the library part |
| `skylights[].cutLineType` | any |  | Line type of cut lines |
| `skylights[].overheadLinePen` | any |  | Pen of overhead lines (floor plan display 'OverheadAll') |
| `skylights[].overheadLineType` | any |  | Line type of overhead lines |
| `skylights[].uncutLinePen` | any |  | Pen of uncut lines |
| `skylights[].uncutLineType` | any |  | Line type of uncut lines (display 'OutlinesOnly') |
| `skylights[].displayOption` | "Projected" \\| "ProjectedWithOverhead" \\| "CutOnly" \\| "OutlinesOnly" \\| "OverheadAll" \\| "Symbolic" |  | Floor plan display of the opening |
| `skylights[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `skylights[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `skylights[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `create_openings`

Creates Opening-tool openings: rectangular or circular extrusion bodies that cut holes through their host (wall, slab, roof, shell, beam, column, mesh) — e.g. shafts through slabs, service holes in walls or beams. Each needs 'owner', 'width' (+ 'height' unless circular) and 'point' ({x,y,z?} plan anchor) or, for walls, 'position' along the wall. Wall openings extrude horizontally through the wall: set their height with bottomElevation (bottom edge above the home story). Slab/roof openings extrude vertically by default (limit 'Infinite' cuts through the whole host). Custom polygonal shapes are not supported by the Archicad 26 API. Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `openings` | object[] | yes |  |
| `openings[].owner` | string \\| object | yes | GUID of the element to cut: Wall, Slab, Roof, Shell, Beam, Column or Mesh. The opening is always on the host's story and follows the host |
| `openings[].owner.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `openings[].position` | number |  | Straight wall hosts only: distance in m along the wall reference line from the wall's begin point to the ANCHOR point |
| `openings[].point` | object |  | Anchor point {x, y, z?} in plan (projected onto the wall reference line for wall hosts); z = anchor elevation above the home story. Give position OR point |
| `openings[].bottomElevation` | number |  | Horizontal / aligned openings (e.g. in walls): elevation in m of the opening's bottom edge above the home story level |
| `openings[].anchorAltitude` | number |  | Alternative to bottomElevation: elevation in m of the anchor point above the home story |
| `openings[].shape` | "Rectangular" \\| "Circular" |  | Base shape (default: tool setting, usually Rectangular) |
| `openings[].width` | number |  | Width in m (circular: diameter). Required on create |
| `openings[].height` | number |  | Height in m (circular: defaults to width). Required on create for rectangular openings |
| `openings[].linkedSize` | boolean |  | Link width and height (keeps them equal) |
| `openings[].anchor` | "TopLeft" \\| "TopCenter" \\| "TopRight" \\| "CenterLeft" \\| "Center" \\| "CenterRight" \\| "BottomLeft" \\| "BottomCenter" \\| "BottomRight" |  | Which point of the base shape sits at the anchor point (default 'Center') |
| `openings[].constraint` | "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" \\| "Aligned" \\| "Free" |  | Extrusion orientation: 'Horizontal' (default for walls, beams, columns: perpendicular to the wall), 'Vertical' (default for slabs, roofs, shells, meshes: shafts), 'Aligned' (perpendicular to the host surface) or 'Free' (use extrusionDirection) |
| `openings[].extrusionDirection` | object |  | Explicit extrusion direction (default derived from constraint and host) |
| `openings[].xAxis` | any |  | Direction of the base shape's width axis (made perpendicular to the extrusion direction) |
| `openings[].rotation` | number |  | Rotation of the base shape in degrees about the extrusion direction (e.g. rotate a rectangular slab opening) |
| `openings[].limit` | "Infinite" \\| "Finite" \\| "HalfInfinite" |  | Extrusion extent: through everything along the axis, a finite body (startOffset + depth), or from startOffset to infinity |
| `openings[].startOffset` | number |  | Finite / HalfInfinite: start of the body from the anchor plane along the extrusion direction (m) |
| `openings[].depth` | number |  | Finite: length of the extrusion body (m) |
| `openings[].floorPlanDisplay` | "Symbolic" \\| "SymbolicCut" \\| "SymbolicOverhead" |  | Floor plan display mode |
| `openings[].connectionMode` | "Connected" \\| "Disconnected" |  | Floor plan connection mode |
| `openings[].outlinesStyle` | "HideBorder" \\| "ShowUncutBorder" \\| "ShowOverheadBorder" |  | Floor plan outline style |
| `openings[].showReferenceAxis` | boolean |  | Show the opening's reference axis on the floor plan |
| `openings[].layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `openings[].layer.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `openings[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `openings[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `modify_openings`

Changes existing windows, doors, skylights and Opening-tool openings in one undo step. Each item is {guid, ...fields to change} with the same field names as create_windows / create_doors / create_skylights / create_openings (e.g. move a window: position; resize: width/height; swap the library part: libraryPart; GDL: gdlParams; flip: flipped / mirrored). Openings keep their anchorAltitude when anchor/height change — pass bottomElevation to keep the bottom edge instead. The host (wall / roof / owner) cannot be changed — delete and recreate instead. Returns [{guid} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `openings` | object[] | yes |  |
| `openings[].guid` | string | yes | GUID of the window, door, skylight or opening to change |
| `openings[].position` | number |  | Windows/doors: new centre distance from the wall begin (m). Wall openings: anchor distance |
| `openings[].point` | object |  | Windows/doors: plan point projected onto the wall. Skylights: new anchor position {x,y}. Openings: new anchor point {x,y,z?} |
| `openings[].positionReference` | "Center" \\| "Begin" \\| "End" |  | Windows/doors: what position/point refers to (default Center) |
| `openings[].sillHeight` | number |  | Windows/doors: sill height (m) |
| `openings[].width` | number |  | Width in m |
| `openings[].height` | number |  | Height in m |
| `openings[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | Windows/doors/skylights: swap the library part (current size kept unless width/height given; GDL params reset to the new part's defaults) |
| `openings[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `openings[].gdlParams` | object |  | Windows/doors/skylights: GDL parameter values {name: value} (through the parameter script) |
| `openings[].flipped` | boolean |  | Windows/doors/skylights: flip to the other side |
| `openings[].referenceSideFlipped` | boolean |  |  |
| `openings[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Windows/doors/skylights: mirror left/right |
| `openings[].anchor` | string |  | Windows/doors: Begin\|Center\|End. Skylights: BottomCenter\|BottomLeft\|BottomRight\|TopCenter\|TopLeft\|TopRight. Openings: TopLeft..Center..BottomRight |
| `openings[].verticalAnchor` | "SillToWallBottom" \\| "SillToStory" \\| "HeaderToWallBottom" \\| "HeaderToStory" \\| "SillToWallTop" \\| "HeaderToWallTop" \\| "ToRoofPivot" \\| "ToStory" \\| "ToShellBase" |  | Windows/doors: SillToWallBottom\|SillToStory\|HeaderToWallBottom\|HeaderToStory\|SillToWallTop\|HeaderToWallTop. Skylights: ToRoofPivot\|ToStory\|ToShellBase |
| `openings[].verticalAnchorStory` | integer \\| string |  | Story for story-based verticalAnchor values |
| `openings[].reveal` | boolean |  |  |
| `openings[].revealDepth` | number |  |  |
| `openings[].revealDepthLocation` | "WallSide" \\| "Core" |  |  |
| `openings[].jambDepthHead` | number |  |  |
| `openings[].jambDepthSill` | number |  |  |
| `openings[].jambDepthLeft` | number |  |  |
| `openings[].jambDepthRight` | number |  |  |
| `openings[].orientationInSlantedWall` | "FollowWall" \\| "Vertical" |  |  |
| `openings[].inheritWallCut` | boolean |  |  |
| `openings[].subFloorThickness` | number |  |  |
| `openings[].anchorLevel` | number |  | Skylights: absolute Z of the anchor point |
| `openings[].fixMode` | "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" |  | Skylights |
| `openings[].azimuthAngle` | number |  | Skylights: rotation in degrees (clockwise) |
| `openings[].pivotVertexId` | integer |  | Skylights on multi-plane roofs |
| `openings[].bottomElevation` | number |  | Openings: bottom edge elevation above the home story (m) |
| `openings[].anchorAltitude` | number |  | Openings: anchor point elevation above the home story (m) |
| `openings[].shape` | "Rectangular" \\| "Circular" |  | Openings |
| `openings[].linkedSize` | boolean |  | Openings |
| `openings[].constraint` | "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" \\| "Aligned" \\| "Free" |  | Openings |
| `openings[].extrusionDirection` | object |  | Openings |
| `openings[].xAxis` | any |  | Openings |
| `openings[].rotation` | number |  | Openings: rotation about the extrusion direction (deg) |
| `openings[].limit` | "Infinite" \\| "Finite" \\| "HalfInfinite" |  | Openings |
| `openings[].startOffset` | number |  | Openings |
| `openings[].depth` | number |  | Openings |
| `openings[].floorPlanDisplay` | "Symbolic" \\| "SymbolicCut" \\| "SymbolicOverhead" |  | Openings |
| `openings[].connectionMode` | "Connected" \\| "Disconnected" |  | Openings |
| `openings[].outlinesStyle` | "HideBorder" \\| "ShowUncutBorder" \\| "ShowOverheadBorder" |  | Openings |
| `openings[].showReferenceAxis` | boolean |  | Openings |
| `openings[].surface` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Surface (material) used for the whole object; also switches useObjectSurfaces off (list names with get_attributes type Surface) |
| `openings[].surface.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `openings[].useObjectSurfaces` | boolean |  | true = use the surfaces set in the library part's own GDL parameters |
| `openings[].pen` | integer |  | Contour pen; also switches useObjectPens off |
| `openings[].useObjectPens` | boolean |  | true = use the pens set in the library part's parameters |
| `openings[].lineType` | any |  | Line type; also switches useObjectLineTypes off |
| `openings[].useObjectLineTypes` | boolean |  | true = use the line types set in the library part's parameters |
| `openings[].cutFill` | any |  | Fill of cut (section) parts; also switches useObjectSectionAttributes off |
| `openings[].cutFillPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut fill |
| `openings[].cutFillBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Background pen of the cut fill (0 = transparent) |
| `openings[].cutContourPen` | any |  | Pen of the cut contour |
| `openings[].useObjectSectionAttributes` | boolean |  | true = use the section attributes set in the library part |
| `openings[].cutLineType` | any |  | Line type of cut lines |
| `openings[].overheadLinePen` | any |  | Pen of overhead lines (floor plan display 'OverheadAll') |
| `openings[].overheadLineType` | any |  | Line type of overhead lines |
| `openings[].uncutLinePen` | any |  | Pen of uncut lines |
| `openings[].uncutLineType` | any |  | Line type of uncut lines (display 'OutlinesOnly') |
| `openings[].displayOption` | "Projected" \\| "ProjectedWithOverhead" \\| "CutOnly" \\| "OutlinesOnly" \\| "OverheadAll" \\| "Symbolic" |  | Floor plan display of the opening |
| `openings[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `openings[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `openings[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `get_host_openings`

Lists the windows, doors, skylights and Opening-tool openings placed in the given host elements (walls, roofs, shells, slabs, beams, ...). details=false (default) returns GUIDs only; details=true returns the full element details of every opening (position, size, library part...).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hosts` | string \\| object[] | yes | Host element GUIDs (walls, roofs, shells, slabs, beams, columns ...) |
| `details` | boolean |  | Include full element details of each opening (default false) |


## objects-library

### `create_objects`

Places GDL objects (furniture, equipment, fixtures, custom parts from create_library_part, ...) in one undo step. Each item needs a libraryPart (Object type — names are LOCALIZED, so find them first with search_library_parts {query, type: 'Object'}) and a position (meters). Optional: elevation above the home story, angle (degrees CCW), mirrored, sizes sizeA (X) / sizeB (Y) / height (ZZYZX), any GDL params by name (see get_library_part_details), pen/line/surface overrides, story visibility, layer, storyIndex. Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order. Lamps: create_lamps. Doors/windows: the opening tools. Later changes: modify_elements (same fields), set_gdl_parameters, change_library_part.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `objects` | object[] | yes | Objects to place |
| `objects[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object | yes | Library part to place: exact LOCALIZED name (this Archicad may be Russian), index, or {guid} — find it with search_library_parts, or use the guid returned by create_library_part |
| `objects[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `objects[].position` | object | yes | Insertion point = the part's local origin (for most parts a bottom-left corner of the A x B box), meters |
| `objects[].elevation` | number |  | Base elevation above the home story level in m (default: tool setting, usually 0) |
| `objects[].angle` | number |  | Rotation in degrees, counter-clockwise, around the insertion point (default 0) |
| `objects[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror the part (reflected about its local Y axis) |
| `objects[].sizeA` | number |  | GDL A = size along the part's local X axis (width) in m; default: the part's default |
| `objects[].sizeB` | number |  | GDL B = size along the part's local Y axis (depth) in m; default: the part's default |
| `objects[].height` | number |  | GDL ZZYZX = 3D height in m (only for parts having a ZZYZX parameter) |
| `objects[].params` | object |  | GDL parameter values by parameter NAME (the variable name like 'gs_cont_pen', not the localized description; list them with get_gdl_parameters / get_library_part_details). Length in meters, Angle in DEGREES, Boolean as true/false, Integer/RealNum as numbers, String as text. Surface / BuildingMaterial / LineType / Fill / Profile parameters accept the attribute NAME (localized) or index. Numeric parameters with a value list also accept the value's description text. Arrays: [..] (1-D) or [[..],[..]] (2-D, rows). 'A' and 'B' are the X/Y sizes. Changes run the part's parameter script, exactly like the settings dialog. |
| `objects[].pen` | integer |  | Override pen of all 2D/3D lines (sets useObjectPens=false unless given) |
| `objects[].useObjectPens` | boolean |  | true = use the part's own pens (no override) |
| `objects[].lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Override line type (name/index; sets useObjectLineTypes=false unless given) |
| `objects[].lineType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `objects[].useObjectLineTypes` | boolean |  | true = use the part's own line types |
| `objects[].overrideSurface` | any \\| boolean |  | One surface (name/index) for the whole 3D body, or false = use the part's own surfaces |
| `objects[].sectionFill` | any |  | Override cut fill (name/index) in sections |
| `objects[].sectionFillPen` | any |  | Override cut fill foreground pen |
| `objects[].sectionBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Override cut fill background pen (0 = transparent) |
| `objects[].sectionContourPen` | any |  | Override cut contour pen |
| `objects[].useObjectSectionAttributes` | boolean |  | true = the part's own section attributes (set automatically to false when a section override is given) |
| `objects[].showOnStories` | "HomeOnly" \\| "AllRelevant" \\| "AllStories" \\| object |  | Stories the element is shown on: 'HomeOnly', 'AllRelevant' (every story its 3D body intersects), 'AllStories', or a custom {home, allAbove, allBelow, above, below}. Default: tool setting |
| `objects[].showOnStories.home` | boolean |  | Show on the home story |
| `objects[].showOnStories.allAbove` | boolean |  | Show on all stories above |
| `objects[].showOnStories.allBelow` | boolean |  | Show on all stories below |
| `objects[].showOnStories.above` | integer |  | Show on this many stories above |
| `objects[].showOnStories.below` | integer |  | Show on this many stories below |
| `objects[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `objects[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `objects[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `objects[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `objects[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `create_lamps`

Places lamps (light-emitting GDL library parts of type 'Lamp': ceiling lights, spots, floor lamps, ...) in one undo step. Same fields as create_objects plus lightOn, lightColor {red, green, blue} (0..1) and lightIntensity. Find lamp names with search_library_parts {type: 'Lamp'} (names are localized). Typical ceiling light: elevation = ceiling height minus the lamp height. Returns [{guid, type} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lamps` | object[] | yes | Lamps to place |
| `lamps[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object | yes | Lamp library part (type 'Lamp'): exact LOCALIZED name, index or {guid} — find it with search_library_parts {type: 'Lamp'} |
| `lamps[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `lamps[].position` | object | yes | Insertion point = the part's local origin (for most parts a bottom-left corner of the A x B box), meters |
| `lamps[].elevation` | number |  | Base elevation above the home story level in m (default: tool setting, usually 0) |
| `lamps[].angle` | number |  | Rotation in degrees, counter-clockwise, around the insertion point (default 0) |
| `lamps[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror the part (reflected about its local Y axis) |
| `lamps[].sizeA` | number |  | GDL A = size along the part's local X axis (width) in m; default: the part's default |
| `lamps[].sizeB` | number |  | GDL B = size along the part's local Y axis (depth) in m; default: the part's default |
| `lamps[].height` | number |  | GDL ZZYZX = 3D height in m (only for parts having a ZZYZX parameter) |
| `lamps[].params` | object |  | GDL parameter values by parameter NAME (the variable name like 'gs_cont_pen', not the localized description; list them with get_gdl_parameters / get_library_part_details). Length in meters, Angle in DEGREES, Boolean as true/false, Integer/RealNum as numbers, String as text. Surface / BuildingMaterial / LineType / Fill / Profile parameters accept the attribute NAME (localized) or index. Numeric parameters with a value list also accept the value's description text. Arrays: [..] (1-D) or [[..],[..]] (2-D, rows). 'A' and 'B' are the X/Y sizes. Changes run the part's parameter script, exactly like the settings dialog. |
| `lamps[].pen` | integer |  | Override pen of all 2D/3D lines (sets useObjectPens=false unless given) |
| `lamps[].useObjectPens` | boolean |  | true = use the part's own pens (no override) |
| `lamps[].lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Override line type (name/index; sets useObjectLineTypes=false unless given) |
| `lamps[].lineType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `lamps[].useObjectLineTypes` | boolean |  | true = use the part's own line types |
| `lamps[].overrideSurface` | any \\| boolean |  | One surface (name/index) for the whole 3D body, or false = use the part's own surfaces |
| `lamps[].sectionFill` | any |  | Override cut fill (name/index) in sections |
| `lamps[].sectionFillPen` | any |  | Override cut fill foreground pen |
| `lamps[].sectionBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Override cut fill background pen (0 = transparent) |
| `lamps[].sectionContourPen` | any |  | Override cut contour pen |
| `lamps[].useObjectSectionAttributes` | boolean |  | true = the part's own section attributes (set automatically to false when a section override is given) |
| `lamps[].showOnStories` | "HomeOnly" \\| "AllRelevant" \\| "AllStories" \\| object |  | Stories the element is shown on: 'HomeOnly', 'AllRelevant' (every story its 3D body intersects), 'AllStories', or a custom {home, allAbove, allBelow, above, below}. Default: tool setting |
| `lamps[].showOnStories.home` | boolean |  | Show on the home story |
| `lamps[].showOnStories.allAbove` | boolean |  | Show on all stories above |
| `lamps[].showOnStories.allBelow` | boolean |  | Show on all stories below |
| `lamps[].showOnStories.above` | integer |  | Show on this many stories above |
| `lamps[].showOnStories.below` | integer |  | Show on this many stories below |
| `lamps[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `lamps[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `lamps[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `lamps[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `lamps[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `lamps[].lightOn` | boolean |  | Switch the light on/off (the part's Light Switch parameter) |
| `lamps[].lightColor` | object |  | Light color, components 0..1 (the part's RGB color parameters) |
| `lamps[].lightColor.red` | number | yes |  |
| `lamps[].lightColor.green` | number | yes |  |
| `lamps[].lightColor.blue` | number | yes |  |
| `lamps[].lightIntensity` | number |  | Light intensity (the part's Intensity parameter, usually 0-100 %) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `search_library_parts`

Finds library parts (objects, doors, windows, lamps, zone stamps, labels, skylights, macros ...) in the loaded libraries. Names are LOCALIZED — on a Russian Archicad search Russian words (e.g. 'стол' table, 'стул' chair, 'дверь' door, 'окно' window, 'светильник' lamp, 'кровать' bed, 'шкаф' cabinet, 'диван' sofa, 'унитаз' WC, 'раковина' sink, 'дерево' tree). The query is a case-insensitive substring; with several words all must match the name or file name. Exact and prefix matches come first. Filter by type, by subtype (category: a keyword like 'Furnishing', 'Plant', 'Light' or a template from get_library_part_subtypes), or embeddedOnly (parts created with create_library_part). Returns {libraryParts: [{index, name, guid, type, fileName, subtype}], total, hasMore}. Use the name or {guid} as libraryPart in create_objects / create_lamps / change_library_part.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `query` | string |  | Substring(s) of the name / file name; omit to list everything matching the filters |
| `type` | "Object" \\| "Door" \\| "Window" \\| "Lamp" \\| "Zone" \\| "Label" \\| "Skylight" \\| "Macro" \\| "Picture" \\| "PlanSign" \\| "ListScheme" \\| "Property" \\| "OpeningSymbol" \\| "Spec" \\| any[] |  | Only these library part types |
| `subtypeOf` | string \\| object |  | Only descendants of this subtype: a keyword (GeneralGDLObject, ModelElement, BuildingElement, Furnishing, Beds, Structure, Column, Beam, Slab, Wall, Roof, Stair, Railing, Ramp, Covering, Footing, Plant, People, Animal, Traffic, TransportElement, StreetFurniture, SportField, DistributionElement, ElectricalElement, FlowTerminal, FlowEquipment, SolarPVPanels, DrawingSymbol, DocumentationElement, Marker, PropertyObjects, Light, WindowWall, CornerWindow, DoorWall, WallOpening, WallEnd, Skylight, Label, ZoneStamp) or a template name/{guid} from get_library_part_subtypes |
| `subtypeOf.guid` | string | yes |  |
| `placeableOnly` | boolean |  | Only parts that can be placed (default true; false also lists macros and templates) |
| `embeddedOnly` | boolean |  | Only parts stored in the project's embedded library |
| `offset` | integer |  | Skip this many results (default 0) |
| `limit` | integer |  | Maximum results (default 100) |

### `get_library_part_subtypes`

Lists the library part subtype tree (categories such as Model Element > Building Element > Furnishing > Chair — names localized) with each subtype's path, parent, type and number of placeable parts directly under it. Use it to browse the library by category, then search_library_parts {subtypeOf: {guid}} to list the parts of a category, or pass a subtype to create_library_part.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `query` | string |  | Case-insensitive substring of the subtype name or its path |
| `type` | "Object" \\| "Door" \\| "Window" \\| "Lamp" \\| "Zone" \\| "Label" \\| "Skylight" \\| "Macro" \\| "Picture" \\| "PlanSign" \\| "ListScheme" \\| "Property" \\| "OpeningSymbol" \\| "Spec" \\| any[] |  | Only subtypes of these library part types |
| `offset` | integer |  | Skip this many results |
| `limit` | integer |  | Maximum results (default 300) |

### `get_library_part_details`

Everything about library parts before placing them: identity (name, guid, index, type, file), file location and containing library, subtype/ancestry, creator tool, sections, comment/keywords, default sizes (sizeA, sizeB, height) and the DEFAULT GDL parameters [{name, type, description, value, valueDescription?, hidden?, arrayDims?, valueList?}] — the parameter names are what create_objects params and set_gdl_parameters expect. includeValueLists adds the allowed values/ranges from the parameter script (slower: use with parameterNames).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `libraryParts` | string \\| integer \\| object[] | yes | Library parts (name, index or {guid}) |
| `includeParameters` | boolean |  | Include the default GDL parameters (default true) |
| `includeHidden` | boolean |  | Also include hidden parameters (default false) |
| `includeValueLists` | boolean |  | Add allowed values / ranges of each returned parameter (default false) |
| `parameterNames` | string[] |  | Only these parameters (exact names, case-insensitive) |

### `get_library_part_scripts`

Returns the GDL source of a library part: masterScript, script2D, script3D, parameterScript, interfaceScript, propertiesScript (default), and on request forwardMigrationScript, backwardMigrationScript, comment, keywords. Great for learning how a standard part works or as a starting point for create_library_part. Encrypted parts return errors. Long scripts are cut at maxLength characters.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object | yes | Library part name (localized! use search_library_parts), index, or {guid: '{MAIN}-{REV}'} |
| `libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `scripts` | "masterScript" \\| "script2D" \\| "script3D" \\| "parameterScript" \\| "interfaceScript" \\| "propertiesScript" \\| "forwardMigrationScript" \\| "backwardMigrationScript" \\| "comment" \\| "keywords"[] |  | Which scripts (default: the 6 main scripts) |
| `maxLength` | integer |  | Max characters per script (default 60000) |

### `create_library_part`

Creates a custom GDL library part (object, lamp, door, window, skylight, label, zone stamp) in the project's EMBEDDED library from GDL scripts and a parameter list, then returns its {libraryPart: {name, guid, index}} so create_objects / create_lamps can place it. Use it for anything the standard library lacks (custom furniture, built-ins, fixtures, signage, parametric equipment). overwrite: true replaces an existing embedded part of the same name (placed instances keep their link and update). Defaults: type 'Object' with subtype ModelElement; a ZZYZX (height) Length parameter is added for objects/lamps; when script2D is omitted for objects/lamps it is 'PROJECT2 3, 270, 2' (plan symbol = top view of the 3D model). Verify with get_library_part_scripts, then place it and look at it (3D view / get_element_details); GDL errors show up as missing geometry.

GDL QUICK REFERENCE (units: meters, angles in DEGREES):
- Local system: origin = insertion point, X along A (width), Y along B (depth), Z up, ZZYZX = height. Every parameter is a variable in all scripts; A, B (and ZZYZX for objects) always exist.
- masterScript runs before the 2D, 3D and parameter scripts: compute shared variables there. One statement per line; '!' starts a comment; strings in "..." or '...'.
- 3D transformations (a stack): ADDX dx / ADDY dy / ADDZ dz / ADD dx, dy, dz; ROTX a / ROTY a / ROTZ a; MULX f / MUL fx, fy, fz; DEL n (undo the last n), DEL TOP (undo all).
- 3D bodies at the current origin: BLOCK a, b, c (box along +X +Y +Z); PRISM_ n, h, x1, y1, s1, ..., xn, yn, sn (vertical extrusion of a polygon; s = 15 for visible edges); CPRISM_ topMat, botMat, sideMat, n, h, x1, y1, s1, ...; CYLIND h, r (along Z); SPHERE r; ELLIPS h, r; CONE h, r1, r2, 90, 90; REVOLVE n, alpha, mask, x1, y1, s1, ... (profile in the XY plane revolved around X); EXTRUDE n, dx, dy, dz, mask, x1, y1, s1, ...; TUBE; RULED; SLAB_.
- Attributes: MATERIAL m (surface name, index or a Surface parameter) before the bodies; PEN p; RESOL n (segments of curved surfaces); HOTSPOT x, y, z (3D editing point).
- 2D script (plan symbol): PROJECT2 3, 270, 2 (top view of the 3D model), or draw: LINE2 x1, y1, x2, y2; RECT2 x1, y1, x2, y2; POLY2_ n, frameFill, x1, y1, s1, ... (frameFill 1 = contour, 2 = fill, 4 = close; 7 = all); CIRCLE2 x, y, r; ARC2 x, y, r, a1, a2; FILL f before a filled POLY2_; HOTSPOT2 x, y; TEXT2 x, y, "text"; ADD2 dx, dy / ROT2 a / MUL2 fx, fy / DEL n.
- parameterScript: VALUES "len" 0.6, 0.8, 1.2 | VALUES "len" RANGE [0.3, 2.4] | VALUES "style" "Modern", "Classic"; LOCK "param"; HIDEPARAMETER "param"; PARAMETERS param = expression (store a computed value).
- Flow: IF c THEN ... ELSE ... ENDIF; FOR i = 1 TO n ... NEXT i; WHILE c DO ... ENDWHILE; GOSUB "sub" ... END ... "sub": ... RETURN. Operators + - * / ^ MOD = <> < > <= >= AND OR NOT; functions SIN COS TAN ATN (degrees), SQR (square root), ABS, MIN, MAX, INT, FRA, STR, PI.
- Example: table with parameters [{name:'topThk', type:'Length', value:0.04}, {name:'legW', type:'Length', value:0.05}, {name:'mat', type:'Surface', value:<surface name>}], a: 1.6, b: 0.8, height: 0.75, script3D:
  MATERIAL mat
  ADDZ ZZYZX - topThk
  BLOCK A, B, topThk
  DEL 1
  FOR i = 0 TO 1
    FOR j = 0 TO 1
      ADD i * (A - legW), j * (B - legW), 0
      BLOCK legW, legW, ZZYZX - topThk
      DEL 1
    NEXT j
  NEXT i
  (script2D omitted = PROJECT2 3, 270, 2)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | Library part name (becomes the .gsm file name; no / \ : * ? " < > \|). Must be unique in the loaded libraries |
| `type` | "Object" \\| "Lamp" \\| "Window" \\| "Door" \\| "Skylight" \\| "Label" \\| "Zone" |  | Library part type (default Object, or implied by subtype) |
| `subtype` | string \\| object |  | Parent subtype: a keyword (GeneralGDLObject, ModelElement, BuildingElement, Furnishing, Beds, Structure, Column, Beam, Slab, Wall, Roof, Stair, Railing, Ramp, Covering, Footing, Plant, People, Animal, Traffic, TransportElement, StreetFurniture, SportField, DistributionElement, ElectricalElement, FlowTerminal, FlowEquipment, SolarPVPanels, DrawingSymbol, DocumentationElement, Marker, PropertyObjects, Light, WindowWall, CornerWindow, DoorWall, WallOpening, WallEnd, Skylight, Label, ZoneStamp) or a template library part name/{guid} from get_library_part_subtypes. Default by type: Object → ModelElement, Lamp → Light, Window → WindowWall, Door → DoorWall, Skylight, Label, Zone → ZoneStamp |
| `subtype.guid` | string | yes |  |
| `scripts` | object |  | GDL scripts (plain text, newline separated) |
| `scripts.masterScript` | string |  | Runs before every other script: shared calculations |
| `scripts.script2D` | string |  | Floor plan symbol (default for objects: PROJECT2 3, 270, 2) |
| `scripts.script3D` | string |  | 3D model |
| `scripts.parameterScript` | string |  | VALUES / RANGE / LOCK / PARAMETERS logic |
| `scripts.interfaceScript` | string |  | Custom settings-dialog page (UI_ commands) |
| `scripts.propertiesScript` | string |  | Listing / component properties |
| `scripts.forwardMigrationScript` | string |  |  |
| `scripts.backwardMigrationScript` | string |  |  |
| `parameters` | object[] |  | Parameter list in dialog order (A, B are implicit — use a / b) |
| `parameters[].name` | string |  | Variable name used in the scripts (ASCII, max 31 chars). Required except for Separator/Title. Do not declare A or B |
| `parameters[].type` | "Length" \\| "Angle" \\| "RealNum" \\| "Integer" \\| "Boolean" \\| "String" \\| "Surface" \\| "BuildingMaterial" \\| "Pen" \\| "LineType" \\| "Fill" \\| "Profile" \\| "LightSwitch" \\| "ColorRGB" \\| "Intensity" \\| "Separator" \\| "Title" | yes | Parameter type |
| `parameters[].description` | string |  | Label shown in the settings dialog (any language; default: the name) |
| `parameters[].value` | number \\| string \\| boolean \\| number[] \\| string[] \\| number[][] \\| string[][] |  | Default value: Length in m, Angle in degrees, Boolean true/false, String text, Surface/BuildingMaterial/LineType/Fill/Profile as attribute name or index, Pen index. An array value makes an array parameter ([..] 1-D, [[..],[..]] 2-D) |
| `parameters[].hidden` | boolean |  | Hide from the settings dialog |
| `parameters[].bold` | boolean |  | Show the name in bold |
| `parameters[].child` | boolean |  | Indent under the previous parameter |
| `parameters[].unique` | boolean |  | Value is not transferred when swapping library parts |
| `parameters[].arrayDims` | object |  | Array size when no array value is given (dim2 = 0 for a 1-D array); cells default to 0 / '' |
| `parameters[].arrayDims.dim1` | integer | yes |  |
| `parameters[].arrayDims.dim2` | integer | yes |  |
| `a` | number |  | Default A (X size) in m (default 1) |
| `b` | number |  | Default B (Y size) in m (default 1) |
| `height` | number |  | Default ZZYZX (height) in m for the automatic height parameter (default 1) |
| `addHeightParameter` | boolean |  | Add a ZZYZX Length parameter when not declared (default true for Object/Lamp) |
| `autoHotspots` | boolean |  | Automatic bounding-box hotspots (default true; set false when the 2D script places HOTSPOT2s) |
| `fixSize` | boolean |  | Size cannot be stretched (default false) |
| `placeable` | boolean |  | Can be placed (default true; false = macro for CALL) |
| `template` | boolean |  | Can be used as a subtype of other parts (default false) |
| `comment` | string |  | Description shown in the library browser |
| `keywords` | string |  | Search keywords |
| `author` | string |  | Author / copyright |
| `folder` | string |  | Sub-folder in the embedded library (default 'Claude Objects'; '' = root) |
| `overwrite` | boolean |  | Replace an existing embedded library part with the same name (default false) |

### `get_libraries`

Lists the libraries loaded in the project (Library Manager): name, path, type (Local, Embedded, BuiltIn, Server, Url ...), available, readOnly, plus the total number of library parts.

### `add_libraries`

Adds local libraries to the project and loads them: absolute folder paths (or .lcf library container files) on this Mac; '~/' is expanded. Already loaded paths are reported as alreadyLoaded. NOT undoable. Returns per-path results and the new library list.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `paths` | string[] | yes | Absolute folder or .lcf paths, e.g. '/Users/me/Documents/My Objects' |

### `remove_libraries`

Removes libraries from the project by path or name (see get_libraries). The embedded and built-in libraries cannot be removed. Placed elements using parts of a removed library become 'missing objects'. NOT undoable.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `libraries` | string[] | yes | Library paths or names exactly as listed by get_libraries |

### `reload_libraries`

Reloads all loaded libraries (picks up library part files changed on disk, fixes stale 'missing' parts).

### `get_gdl_parameters`

Returns the GDL parameters of placed library-part based elements (objects, lamps, doors, windows, skylights, zones, symbol labels, ...): per element {guid, type, libraryPart, parameters: [{name, type, description, value, valueDescription?, hidden?, disabled?, arrayDims?, valueList?}]}. Lengths in m, angles in degrees. Filter with names (exact) or search (substring of name or localized description). includeValueLists adds allowed values/ranges and 'locked' state from the parameter script. Hidden parameters are skipped unless includeHidden or named explicitly.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | string \\| object[] | yes | Element GUIDs |
| `names` | string[] |  | Only these parameters (exact variable names, case-insensitive) |
| `search` | string |  | Only parameters whose name or description contains this text (case-insensitive) |
| `includeHidden` | boolean |  | Include hidden parameters (default false) |
| `includeValueLists` | boolean |  | Add value lists / ranges / locked flags (default false; slower) |

### `set_gdl_parameters`

Changes GDL parameters of placed objects, lamps, doors, windows, skylights, zones or labels in one undo step, running each part's parameter script like the settings dialog (dependent parameters update, invalid values are corrected or rejected). Give per-element params, and/or top-level params applied to every listed element (per-element values win). Changing A/B also resizes the element. Returns [{guid, parameters: resulting values} | {error}]. Parameter names: get_gdl_parameters.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | object[] | yes | Elements to change: {guid, params?} |
| `elements[].guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `elements[].params` | object |  | GDL parameter values by parameter NAME (the variable name like 'gs_cont_pen', not the localized description; list them with get_gdl_parameters / get_library_part_details). Length in meters, Angle in DEGREES, Boolean as true/false, Integer/RealNum as numbers, String as text. Surface / BuildingMaterial / LineType / Fill / Profile parameters accept the attribute NAME (localized) or index. Numeric parameters with a value list also accept the value's description text. Arrays: [..] (1-D) or [[..],[..]] (2-D, rows). 'A' and 'B' are the X/Y sizes. Changes run the part's parameter script, exactly like the settings dialog. |
| `params` | any |  | Values applied to every element (merged under each element's own params) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `change_library_part`

Swaps the library part of placed objects, lamps, doors, windows, skylights, zones or symbol labels (e.g. replace a chair model, change a door type) in one undo step, keeping position/orientation. The new part must have the same type (Object for objects, Door for doors ...). keepParameters (default true) copies values of same-named, same-typed, visible, non-unique GDL parameters like Archicad does; keepSize (default true for doors/windows/skylights, false otherwise) keeps A/B. Extra params / sizeA / sizeB / height are applied afterwards. A top-level libraryPart applies to every element without its own. Returns [{guid, type, libraryPart, carriedOverParameters} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | object[] | yes |  |
