| `elements[].guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `elements[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | New library part for this element (default: the top-level libraryPart) |
| `elements[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `elements[].keepParameters` | boolean |  |  |
| `elements[].keepSize` | boolean |  |  |
| `elements[].params` | object |  | GDL parameter values by parameter NAME (the variable name like 'gs_cont_pen', not the localized description; list them with get_gdl_parameters / get_library_part_details). Length in meters, Angle in DEGREES, Boolean as true/false, Integer/RealNum as numbers, String as text. Surface / BuildingMaterial / LineType / Fill / Profile parameters accept the attribute NAME (localized) or index. Numeric parameters with a value list also accept the value's description text. Arrays: [..] (1-D) or [[..],[..]] (2-D, rows). 'A' and 'B' are the X/Y sizes. Changes run the part's parameter script, exactly like the settings dialog. |
| `elements[].sizeA` | number |  | Objects/lamps: new A in m |
| `elements[].sizeB` | number |  | Objects/lamps: new B in m |
| `elements[].height` | number |  | Objects/lamps: new ZZYZX in m |
| `libraryPart` | any |  | New library part for all listed elements (localized name, index or {guid}) |
| `keepParameters` | boolean |  | Default for all elements: copy matching parameter values (default true) |
| `keepSize` | boolean |  | Default for all elements: keep A/B sizes |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |


## zones

### `create_zones`

Creates zones (rooms/spaces) in one undo step. Two modes per item: (1) MANUAL: 'polygon' = the outline in meters; (2) AUTOMATIC: 'referencePoint' = a point inside a room enclosed by walls/columns/room separators on that story — Archicad detects the outline (boundary 'InnerEdge' or 'ReferenceLine'); it fails with a clear error if the point is not enclosed. Create the walls first. Set name, number, category (localized ZoneCategory attribute, see get_attributes), height (m), bottomOffset, stamp position/angle, stamp library part + GDL parameters, fill/contour. Unspecified settings come from the Zone tool defaults. Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order. Read areas/volumes back with get_zones.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `zones` | object[] | yes | Zones to create |
| `zones[].polygon` | object[] \\| object |  | MANUAL zone outline in m: array of points or {points, arcs?, holes?}. 2D only (no z). Exactly one of polygon / referencePoint is required |
| `zones[].polygon.points` | any[] | yes | Vertices in order, do NOT repeat the first point at the end |
| `zones[].polygon.arcs` | object[] |  | Curved edges |
| `zones[].polygon.holes` | object[] |  | Inner holes, each {points, arcs?} |
| `zones[].referencePoint` | object |  | AUTOMATIC zone: a point INSIDE a room fully enclosed by walls (zoneRelation 'Boundary'), columns, curtain walls or room-separator lines on the zone's story; Archicad detects the boundary. Exactly one of polygon / referencePoint is required |
| `zones[].name` | string |  | Zone name shown in the stamp, e.g. 'Кухня', 'Living room' |
| `zones[].number` | string \\| number |  | Zone number shown in the stamp (roomNoStr, max 31 characters), e.g. '101', '1.05', 'B-12' |
| `zones[].category` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Zone category attribute (sets the stamp color / schedule category). Names are LOCALIZED (Russian Archicad) — list them with get_attributes (type ZoneCategory) |
| `zones[].category.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `zones[].boundary` | "InnerEdge" \\| "ReferenceLine" |  | Automatic zones only (default: the Zone tool setting): 'InnerEdge' = outline follows the inner faces of the surrounding walls (net room), 'ReferenceLine' = gross room measured to the wall reference lines (read back as referenceLinePolygon; 'polygon' stays the inner-edge outline) |
| `zones[].height` | number |  | Zone (room) height in m. Giving a height unlinks the zone top from a story unless topLinkedStory is also given |
| `zones[].topLinkedStory` | integer |  | Link the zone top to the story N levels above the home story (0 = not linked: 'height' is used) |
| `zones[].topOffset` | number |  | Offset of the top from the linked story (m), only with topLinkedStory > 0 |
| `zones[].bottomOffset` | number |  | Zone bottom relative to the home story level (m, default from the Zone tool, usually 0) |
| `zones[].floorThickness` | number |  | Sub-floor thickness (m) used by Archicad's zone calculations |
| `zones[].areaReduction` | number |  | Reduce the calculated zone area by this percentage (0-100) |
| `zones[].showFoundPolygon` | boolean |  | Advanced (ReferenceLine zones only): Archicad's show_found_poly flag — draw the reference-line polygon instead of the inner-edge outline |
| `zones[].stampPosition` | any |  | Zone stamp (label) position in m. Default on create: an interior point of the polygon (manual zones) or the referencePoint (automatic zones) |
| `zones[].stampAngle` | number |  | Zone stamp rotation in degrees |
| `zones[].fixedStampAngle` | boolean |  | Keep the stamp angle fixed when the zone is rotated |
| `zones[].stampPen` | integer |  | Pen (1-255) of the zone stamp |
| `zones[].useStampPens` | boolean |  | Archicad's useStampPens flag (single stamp pen vs. the stamp library part's own pens) |
| `zones[].stamp` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | Zone stamp library part (a library part of type Zone; names are localized — find them with search_library_parts). Changing the stamp resets its parameters to the new part's defaults (plus stampParameters) |
| `zones[].stamp.name` | string | yes |  |
| `zones[].stampParameters` | object |  | GDL parameters of the zone stamp {name: value} (lengths in m, angles in degrees). Read the names from an existing zone with get_gdl_parameters |
| `zones[].surface` | any |  | Surface (material) of the zone's 3D body |
| `zones[].useSurfaceForAllFaces` | boolean |  | Use 'surface' for all faces of the 3D zone body |
| `zones[].showFill` | boolean |  | Show the floor plan cover fill of the zone |
| `zones[].fill` | any |  | Cover fill type (Fill attribute name/index); also turns showFill on unless showFill is given |
| `zones[].fillPen` | any |  | Foreground pen of the cover fill |
| `zones[].fillBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Background pen of the cover fill (0-255) |
| `zones[].fillFromSurface` | boolean |  | Use the fill of the zone's surface (3D hatching) instead of 'fill' |
| `zones[].showContour` | boolean |  | Draw the zone contour line on the floor plan |
| `zones[].contourPen` | any |  | Pen of the zone contour line |
| `zones[].contourLineType` | any |  | Line type (attribute name/index) of the contour; also turns showContour on unless given |
| `zones[].fillOrigin` | any |  | Local origin of the cover fill pattern (m); switches the fill to local orientation |
| `zones[].fillAngle` | number |  | Angle of the cover fill pattern in degrees (local orientation) |
| `zones[].localFillOrientation` | boolean |  | true = fill pattern uses fillOrigin/fillAngle, false = project origin |
| `zones[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `zones[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `zones[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `zones[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `zones[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `modify_zones`

Changes existing zones in one undo step: name, number, category, height/topLinkedStory/bottomOffset, area reduction, stamp (position, angle, library part, GDL parameters), fill/contour/surface, layer/story/elementId. Geometry: 'polygon' sets a new outline and makes the zone manual; 'referencePoint' makes it automatic around a new point; automatic:false freezes the current outline. Only the given fields change. Non-zone GUIDs are rejected per item. Returns {results: [{guid} | {guid?, error}]} in input order. Use get_zones / get_element_details to read current values.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `zones` | object[] | yes | Patches: {guid, field: newValue, ...} |
| `zones[].guid` | string | yes | GUID of the zone to change |
| `zones[].polygon` | object[] \\| object |  | New outline (m); makes the zone MANUAL |
| `zones[].polygon.points` | any[] | yes | Vertices in order, do NOT repeat the first point at the end |
| `zones[].polygon.arcs` | object[] |  | Curved edges |
| `zones[].polygon.holes` | object[] |  | Inner holes, each {points, arcs?} |
| `zones[].referencePoint` | object |  | New reference point inside a closed room; makes the zone AUTOMATIC. Archicad cannot move a zone's reference point in place, so the zone is RE-CREATED at the new point (settings, stamp parameters, Element ID, classifications and custom properties are kept; the stamp moves along) and gets a NEW GUID: the result item then contains {guid: old, newGuid} |
| `zones[].automatic` | boolean |  | false = freeze an automatic zone as a manual zone keeping its current polygon; true requires referencePoint for manual zones |
| `zones[].name` | string |  | Zone name shown in the stamp, e.g. 'Кухня', 'Living room' |
| `zones[].number` | string \\| number |  | Zone number shown in the stamp (roomNoStr, max 31 characters), e.g. '101', '1.05', 'B-12' |
| `zones[].category` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Zone category attribute (sets the stamp color / schedule category). Names are LOCALIZED (Russian Archicad) — list them with get_attributes (type ZoneCategory) |
| `zones[].category.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `zones[].boundary` | "InnerEdge" \\| "ReferenceLine" |  | Automatic zones only (default: the Zone tool setting): 'InnerEdge' = outline follows the inner faces of the surrounding walls (net room), 'ReferenceLine' = gross room measured to the wall reference lines (read back as referenceLinePolygon; 'polygon' stays the inner-edge outline) |
| `zones[].height` | number |  | Zone (room) height in m. Giving a height unlinks the zone top from a story unless topLinkedStory is also given |
| `zones[].topLinkedStory` | integer |  | Link the zone top to the story N levels above the home story (0 = not linked: 'height' is used) |
| `zones[].topOffset` | number |  | Offset of the top from the linked story (m), only with topLinkedStory > 0 |
| `zones[].bottomOffset` | number |  | Zone bottom relative to the home story level (m, default from the Zone tool, usually 0) |
| `zones[].floorThickness` | number |  | Sub-floor thickness (m) used by Archicad's zone calculations |
| `zones[].areaReduction` | number |  | Reduce the calculated zone area by this percentage (0-100) |
| `zones[].showFoundPolygon` | boolean |  | Advanced (ReferenceLine zones only): Archicad's show_found_poly flag — draw the reference-line polygon instead of the inner-edge outline |
| `zones[].stampPosition` | any |  | Zone stamp (label) position in m. Default on create: an interior point of the polygon (manual zones) or the referencePoint (automatic zones) |
| `zones[].stampAngle` | number |  | Zone stamp rotation in degrees |
| `zones[].fixedStampAngle` | boolean |  | Keep the stamp angle fixed when the zone is rotated |
| `zones[].stampPen` | integer |  | Pen (1-255) of the zone stamp |
| `zones[].useStampPens` | boolean |  | Archicad's useStampPens flag (single stamp pen vs. the stamp library part's own pens) |
| `zones[].stamp` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | Zone stamp library part (a library part of type Zone; names are localized — find them with search_library_parts). Changing the stamp resets its parameters to the new part's defaults (plus stampParameters) |
| `zones[].stamp.name` | string | yes |  |
| `zones[].stampParameters` | object |  | GDL parameters of the zone stamp {name: value} (lengths in m, angles in degrees). Read the names from an existing zone with get_gdl_parameters |
| `zones[].surface` | any |  | Surface (material) of the zone's 3D body |
| `zones[].useSurfaceForAllFaces` | boolean |  | Use 'surface' for all faces of the 3D zone body |
| `zones[].showFill` | boolean |  | Show the floor plan cover fill of the zone |
| `zones[].fill` | any |  | Cover fill type (Fill attribute name/index); also turns showFill on unless showFill is given |
| `zones[].fillPen` | any |  | Foreground pen of the cover fill |
| `zones[].fillBackgroundPen` | integer |  | Background pen of the cover fill (0-255) |
| `zones[].fillFromSurface` | boolean |  | Use the fill of the zone's surface (3D hatching) instead of 'fill' |
| `zones[].showContour` | boolean |  | Draw the zone contour line on the floor plan |
| `zones[].contourPen` | any |  | Pen of the zone contour line |
| `zones[].contourLineType` | any |  | Line type (attribute name/index) of the contour; also turns showContour on unless given |
| `zones[].fillOrigin` | any |  | Local origin of the cover fill pattern (m); switches the fill to local orientation |
| `zones[].fillAngle` | number |  | Angle of the cover fill pattern in degrees (local orientation) |
| `zones[].localFillOrientation` | boolean |  | true = fill pattern uses fillOrigin/fillAngle, false = project origin |
| `zones[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `zones[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `zones[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `zones[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `zones[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `update_zones`

Equivalent of Archicad's Design > Update Zones for AUTOMATIC zones: after walls/columns/room separators were moved, added or deleted, re-detects each zone's boundary from its reference point (a temporary zone is placed there and deleted again) and applies it. Manual zones are skipped (change them with modify_zones). Default: all automatic zones of the project (narrow with 'zones' or 'stories'). method 'copyBoundary' (default) keeps the zone GUID and writes the new polygon into it; 'recreate' replaces each zone by a new one with the same settings, stamp parameters, element ID, classifications and custom property values (NEW GUIDs, associative labels are lost) — use it only if copyBoundary reports an error or verified:false. dryRun:true only reports which zones are 'outdated'. Returns {results: [{guid, status: updated|upToDate|outdated|recreated|skipped, before/after areas, verified, newGuid?} | {guid, error}], summary}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `zones` | string \\| object[] |  | Zones to update (default: every automatic zone) |
| `stories` | integer[] |  | Only zones on these story indices (0 = ground floor, negative = basements; see get_stories) |
| `dryRun` | boolean |  | Only check: report outdated zones (with the re-detected polygon) without changing them |
| `method` | "copyBoundary" \\| "recreate" |  | 'copyBoundary' (default, keeps GUIDs) or 'recreate' (fallback: new zones, new GUIDs) |

### `get_zones`

Lists zones (rooms) like a room schedule, sorted by story then number: guid, name, number, category, story, construction method (Manual / InnerEdge / ReferenceLine), area, netArea, calculatedArea (after reductions, as in the stamp) in m², perimeter (m), volume (m³), height, bottomElevation, stamp position, plus totals over ALL matching zones. Filter by guids, stories, category or a search text (name or number). Optional: includePolygon, includeQuantities (walls/doors/windows surfaces, corners, extracted areas...), includeRelations (boundary walls/beams, contained elements by type), includeReductions (area reductions with type/percent/area/polygon). For every setting of one zone use get_element_details.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `zones` | string \\| object[] |  | Only these zones |
| `stories` | integer[] |  | Only zones on these story indices (0 = ground floor, negative = basements; see get_stories) |
| `category` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Only zones of this zone category (localized name or index) |
| `category.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `search` | string |  | Case-insensitive text contained in the zone name or number |
| `includePolygon` | boolean |  | Add each zone's outline 'polygon' (inner edges); ReferenceLine zones also get 'referenceLinePolygon' (the measured gross outline) |
| `includeQuantities` | boolean |  | Add the full Archicad zone quantity set |
| `includeRelations` | boolean |  | Add boundary walls/beams/curtain wall segments and contained elements by type |
| `includeReductions` | boolean |  | Add the area reduction polygons (walls, columns, fills, low-height parts) |
| `offset` | integer |  | Skip this many zones |
| `limit` | integer |  | Return at most this many zones (default 500) |


## drafting

### `create_lines`

Draws straight 2D lines (one undo step). Each line: begin/end points in meters plus optional pen, colorOverridePen, lineType, lineWeight (mm), category, zoneBoundary and arrows. Unspecified settings come from the Line tool defaults. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). For connected segments prefer create_polylines. Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lines` | object[] | yes | Lines to draw |
| `lines[].begin` | object | yes | Start point (m) |
| `lines[].end` | object | yes | End point (m), different from begin |
| `lines[].pen` | integer |  | Pen index 1-255 (colour + weight). Default: tool setting |
| `lines[].colorOverridePen` | integer |  | Draw with the COLOUR of this pen while keeping the weight of 'pen' (0 = no override) |
| `lines[].lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Line type attribute: exact LOCALIZED name or index (1 is usually solid) — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Line'} |
| `lines[].lineType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `lines[].lineWeight` | number |  | Line weight override in mm (e.g. 0.35); -1 = use the pen's own weight (default) |
| `lines[].category` | "Drafting" \\| "Cut" \\| "SkinSeparator" |  | Line category: 'Drafting' (default drafting line), 'Cut' (cut/contour line), 'SkinSeparator' (inner skin line) |
| `lines[].zoneBoundary` | boolean |  | true = the line acts as a zone (room) boundary for automatic zones |
| `lines[].arrows` | object |  | Arrowheads (omitted fields keep the tool defaults) |
| `lines[].arrows.begin` | boolean |  | Arrow at the start point |
| `lines[].arrows.end` | boolean |  | Arrow at the end point |
| `lines[].arrows.type` | "EmptyCircle" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "OpenArrow15" \\| "ClosedArrow15" \\| "FullArrow15" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "OpenArrow45" \\| "ClosedArrow45" \\| "FullArrow45" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "SlashLine75" \\| "SlashLine90" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "HalfArrowCcw15" \\| "HalfArrowCw15" \\| "HalfArrowCcw30" \\| "HalfArrowCw30" \\| "HalfArrowCcw45" \\| "HalfArrowCw45" |  | Arrowhead shape (number = opening angle in degrees) |
| `lines[].arrows.size` | number |  | Arrowhead size in mm (paper) |
| `lines[].arrows.pen` | any |  | Pen of the arrowhead (1-255) |
| `lines[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `lines[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `lines[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `lines[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_arcs`

Draws 2D circular or elliptical arcs. Three ways to define each arc: (A) center + radius + beginAngle + endAngle (degrees, CCW from +X; add minorRadius/axisAngle for elliptical arcs); (B) begin + end + arcAngle (signed sweep, + = counter-clockwise); (C) begin + through + end (three points). Archicad stores arcs counter-clockwise, so a clockwise arc (negative arcAngle) is returned with begin/end swapped. Style fields as create_lines. Full circles: create_circles. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `arcs` | object[] | yes | Arcs to draw |
| `arcs[].center` | object |  | Form A: centre point (m) |
| `arcs[].radius` | number |  | Form A: radius in m (for ellipses: the semi-axis along axisAngle) |
| `arcs[].beginAngle` | number |  | Form A: start angle in degrees, counter-clockwise from +X; the arc runs CCW to endAngle |
| `arcs[].endAngle` | number |  | Form A: end angle in degrees |
| `arcs[].minorRadius` | number |  | Form A, elliptical arc: the other semi-axis in m (default = radius) |
| `arcs[].ratio` | number |  | Form A, elliptical arc: radius / minorRadius (alternative to minorRadius) |
| `arcs[].axisAngle` | number |  | Form A, elliptical arc: direction of the main axis in degrees |
| `arcs[].begin` | any |  | Forms B/C: start point of the arc (m) |
| `arcs[].end` | any |  | Forms B/C: end point of the arc (m) |
| `arcs[].arcAngle` | number |  | Form B: signed sweep in degrees (+ = counter-clockwise from begin to end, - = clockwise), e.g. 90 or -180 |
| `arcs[].through` | any |  | Form C: a third point the arc passes through between begin and end |
| `arcs[].reflected` | boolean |  | Archicad 'reflected' flag (mirrored elliptical arc) |
| `arcs[].pen` | integer |  | Pen index 1-255 (colour + weight). Default: tool setting |
| `arcs[].colorOverridePen` | integer |  | Draw with the COLOUR of this pen while keeping the weight of 'pen' (0 = no override) |
| `arcs[].lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Line type attribute: exact LOCALIZED name or index (1 is usually solid) — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Line'} |
| `arcs[].lineType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `arcs[].lineWeight` | number |  | Line weight override in mm (e.g. 0.35); -1 = use the pen's own weight (default) |
| `arcs[].category` | "Drafting" \\| "Cut" \\| "SkinSeparator" |  | Line category: 'Drafting' (default drafting line), 'Cut' (cut/contour line), 'SkinSeparator' (inner skin line) |
| `arcs[].zoneBoundary` | boolean |  | true = the line acts as a zone (room) boundary for automatic zones |
| `arcs[].arrows` | object |  | Arrowheads (omitted fields keep the tool defaults) |
| `arcs[].arrows.begin` | boolean |  | Arrow at the start point |
| `arcs[].arrows.end` | boolean |  | Arrow at the end point |
| `arcs[].arrows.type` | "EmptyCircle" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "OpenArrow15" \\| "ClosedArrow15" \\| "FullArrow15" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "OpenArrow45" \\| "ClosedArrow45" \\| "FullArrow45" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "SlashLine75" \\| "SlashLine90" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "HalfArrowCcw15" \\| "HalfArrowCw15" \\| "HalfArrowCcw30" \\| "HalfArrowCw30" \\| "HalfArrowCcw45" \\| "HalfArrowCw45" |  | Arrowhead shape (number = opening angle in degrees) |
| `arcs[].arrows.size` | number |  | Arrowhead size in mm (paper) |
| `arcs[].arrows.pen` | any |  | Pen of the arrowhead (1-255) |
| `arcs[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `arcs[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `arcs[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `arcs[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_circles`

Draws full 2D circles (center + radius in m) or ellipses (+ minorRadius and axisAngle in degrees). Style fields: pen, colorOverridePen, lineType, lineWeight, category, zoneBoundary. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `circles` | object[] | yes | Circles/ellipses to draw |
| `circles[].center` | object | yes | Centre point (m) |
| `circles[].radius` | number | yes | Radius in m (for ellipses: the semi-axis along axisAngle) |
| `circles[].minorRadius` | number |  | Ellipse: the other semi-axis in m (default = radius, i.e. a circle) |
| `circles[].ratio` | number |  | Ellipse: radius / minorRadius (alternative to minorRadius) |
| `circles[].axisAngle` | number |  | Ellipse: direction of the main axis in degrees |
| `circles[].pen` | integer |  | Pen index 1-255 (colour + weight). Default: tool setting |
| `circles[].colorOverridePen` | integer |  | Draw with the COLOUR of this pen while keeping the weight of 'pen' (0 = no override) |
| `circles[].lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Line type attribute: exact LOCALIZED name or index (1 is usually solid) — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Line'} |
| `circles[].lineType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `circles[].lineWeight` | number |  | Line weight override in mm (e.g. 0.35); -1 = use the pen's own weight (default) |
| `circles[].category` | "Drafting" \\| "Cut" \\| "SkinSeparator" |  | Line category: 'Drafting' (default drafting line), 'Cut' (cut/contour line), 'SkinSeparator' (inner skin line) |
| `circles[].zoneBoundary` | boolean |  | true = the line acts as a zone (room) boundary for automatic zones |
| `circles[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `circles[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `circles[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `circles[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_polylines`

Draws 2D polylines: connected straight and curved segments (points + optional arcs {index, angle}), open or closed. Ideal for outlines, symbols and room-separator chains (zoneBoundary: true). Style fields as create_lines plus continuousPattern. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details. Modifying 'arcs' or 'closed' requires sending 'points' too (the geometry is replaced).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `polylines` | object[] | yes | Polylines to draw |
| `polylines[].points` | object[] | yes | Vertices in order (m). For a closed shape set closed: true instead of repeating the first point |
| `polylines[].arcs` | object[] |  | Curved segments: {index, angle} makes the edge points[index] -> points[index+1] an arc (+ = CCW) |
| `polylines[].arcs[].index` | integer | yes | Edge from points[index] to points[index+1] (wrapping) is curved |
| `polylines[].arcs[].angle` | number | yes | Central angle in degrees; positive = counter-clockwise arc |
| `polylines[].closed` | boolean |  | Close the polyline back to the first point (the closing edge has index points.length-1) |
| `polylines[].continuousPattern` | boolean |  | true = the line type pattern runs continuously around corners; false = it restarts on every segment |
| `polylines[].pen` | integer |  | Pen index 1-255 (colour + weight). Default: tool setting |
| `polylines[].colorOverridePen` | integer |  | Draw with the COLOUR of this pen while keeping the weight of 'pen' (0 = no override) |
| `polylines[].lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Line type attribute: exact LOCALIZED name or index (1 is usually solid) — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Line'} |
| `polylines[].lineType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `polylines[].lineWeight` | number |  | Line weight override in mm (e.g. 0.35); -1 = use the pen's own weight (default) |
| `polylines[].category` | "Drafting" \\| "Cut" \\| "SkinSeparator" |  | Line category: 'Drafting' (default drafting line), 'Cut' (cut/contour line), 'SkinSeparator' (inner skin line) |
| `polylines[].zoneBoundary` | boolean |  | true = the line acts as a zone (room) boundary for automatic zones |
| `polylines[].arrows` | object |  | Arrowheads (omitted fields keep the tool defaults) |
| `polylines[].arrows.begin` | boolean |  | Arrow at the start point |
| `polylines[].arrows.end` | boolean |  | Arrow at the end point |
| `polylines[].arrows.type` | "EmptyCircle" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "OpenArrow15" \\| "ClosedArrow15" \\| "FullArrow15" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "OpenArrow45" \\| "ClosedArrow45" \\| "FullArrow45" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "SlashLine75" \\| "SlashLine90" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "HalfArrowCcw15" \\| "HalfArrowCw15" \\| "HalfArrowCcw30" \\| "HalfArrowCw30" \\| "HalfArrowCcw45" \\| "HalfArrowCw45" |  | Arrowhead shape (number = opening angle in degrees) |
| `polylines[].arrows.size` | number |  | Arrowhead size in mm (paper) |
| `polylines[].arrows.pen` | any |  | Pen of the arrowhead (1-255) |
| `polylines[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `polylines[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `polylines[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `polylines[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_splines`

Draws smooth 2D Bezier splines through the given points (automatic natural spline), or with explicit Bezier handles (directions: one {angle, lengthPrev, lengthNext} per point). Open or closed. Style fields as create_lines. NOTE: Archicad cannot change the geometry of an existing spline — to reshape one, create a new spline and delete the old one (delete_elements); modify_elements can still change its pen, lineType, arrows etc. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `splines` | object[] | yes | Splines to draw |
| `splines[].points` | object[] | yes | Points the spline passes through, in order (m); closed splines need >= 3 |
| `splines[].closed` | boolean |  | Closed curve (do not repeat the first point) |
| `splines[].directions` | object[] |  | Explicit Bezier handles, one per point. Omit for a smooth automatic (natural) spline |
| `splines[].directions[].angle` | number | yes | Tangent direction at the point in degrees |
| `splines[].directions[].lengthPrev` | number |  | Length of the incoming Bezier handle in m (default 0) |
| `splines[].directions[].lengthNext` | number |  | Length of the outgoing Bezier handle in m (default 0) |
| `splines[].pen` | integer |  | Pen index 1-255 (colour + weight). Default: tool setting |
| `splines[].colorOverridePen` | integer |  | Draw with the COLOUR of this pen while keeping the weight of 'pen' (0 = no override) |
| `splines[].lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Line type attribute: exact LOCALIZED name or index (1 is usually solid) — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Line'} |
| `splines[].lineType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `splines[].lineWeight` | number |  | Line weight override in mm (e.g. 0.35); -1 = use the pen's own weight (default) |
| `splines[].category` | "Drafting" \\| "Cut" \\| "SkinSeparator" |  | Line category: 'Drafting' (default drafting line), 'Cut' (cut/contour line), 'SkinSeparator' (inner skin line) |
| `splines[].zoneBoundary` | boolean |  | true = the line acts as a zone (room) boundary for automatic zones |
| `splines[].arrows` | object |  | Arrowheads (omitted fields keep the tool defaults) |
| `splines[].arrows.begin` | boolean |  | Arrow at the start point |
| `splines[].arrows.end` | boolean |  | Arrow at the end point |
| `splines[].arrows.type` | "EmptyCircle" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "OpenArrow15" \\| "ClosedArrow15" \\| "FullArrow15" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "OpenArrow45" \\| "ClosedArrow45" \\| "FullArrow45" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "SlashLine75" \\| "SlashLine90" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "HalfArrowCcw15" \\| "HalfArrowCw15" \\| "HalfArrowCcw30" \\| "HalfArrowCw30" \\| "HalfArrowCcw45" \\| "HalfArrowCw45" |  | Arrowhead shape (number = opening angle in degrees) |
| `splines[].arrows.size` | number |  | Arrowhead size in mm (paper) |
| `splines[].arrows.pen` | any |  | Pen of the arrowhead (1-255) |
| `splines[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `splines[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `splines[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `splines[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_hatches`

Creates 2D fill polygons (Archicad 'Fill' tool): a polygon in meters with optional arcs and holes, filled with a Fill pattern (fillType) or a building material's cut fill (buildingMaterial), with pens, RGB colour overrides, contour (on/off, pen, line type, weight), pattern orientation (rotated / distorted / radial, local origin), fill category and an optional area text. Attribute names are LOCALIZED — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Fill' | 'BuildingMaterial' | 'Line'}. get_element_details returns the polygon plus area and perimeter. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hatches` | object[] | yes | Fills to create |
| `hatches[].polygon` | object[] \\| object | yes | Fill outline (m): array of points or {points, arcs?, holes?: [{points, arcs?}]}. Do not repeat the first point; orientation is normalized |
| `hatches[].polygon.points` | any[] | yes | Vertices in order, do NOT repeat the first point at the end |
| `hatches[].polygon.arcs` | object[] |  | Curved edges |
| `hatches[].polygon.holes` | object[] |  | Inner holes, each {points, arcs?} |
| `hatches[].fillType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Fill attribute (pattern): exact LOCALIZED name or index — list with get_attributes {type: 'Fill'}. Makes it a plain fill hatch (with buildingMaterial: overrides the material's fill) |
| `hatches[].fillType.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `hatches[].buildingMaterial` | any |  | Building material: the hatch shows the material's cut fill and pens (get_attributes {type: 'BuildingMaterial'}) |
| `hatches[].fillPen` | integer |  | Foreground (pattern) pen; on a building-material hatch this overrides the material's pen |
| `hatches[].fillColorOverridePen` | integer |  | Draw the pattern with this pen's colour (0 = none) |
| `hatches[].backgroundPen` | any |  | Background pen (0 = transparent); overrides the material's on building-material hatches |
| `hatches[].overrideBuildingMaterialPens` | boolean |  | Building-material hatch: true = use fillPen/backgroundPen, false = back to the material's pens |
| `hatches[].foregroundColor` | string \\| object \\| boolean |  | RGB override of the pattern colour ('#RRGGBB' \| {r,g,b} \| false) |
| `hatches[].foregroundColor.r` | number | yes |  |
| `hatches[].foregroundColor.g` | number | yes |  |
| `hatches[].foregroundColor.b` | number | yes |  |
| `hatches[].backgroundColor` | any |  | RGB override of the background colour ('#RRGGBB' \| {r,g,b} \| false) |
| `hatches[].fillCategory` | "Drafting" \\| "Cut" \\| "Cover" |  | Fill category (affects graphic overrides/model view options) |
| `hatches[].contour` | boolean |  | Draw the contour line (false = no contour) |
| `hatches[].contourPen` | any |  | Contour pen (implies contour: true) |
| `hatches[].contourColorOverridePen` | any |  | Draw the contour with this pen's colour (0 = none) |
| `hatches[].contourLineType` | any |  | Contour line type (name/index, get_attributes {type: 'Line'}) |
| `hatches[].contourLineWeight` | number |  | Line weight override in mm (e.g. 0.35); -1 = use the pen's own weight (default) |
| `hatches[].orientation` | object |  | Fill pattern placement |
| `hatches[].orientation.type` | "Global" \\| "Rotated" \\| "Distorted" \\| "Radial" |  | Pattern orientation: 'Global' (aligned to the project origin), 'Rotated' (angle), 'Distorted' (xAxis/yAxis), 'Radial' (centered) |
| `hatches[].orientation.origin` | object |  | Local origin of the fill pattern (m); makes the origin local |
| `hatches[].orientation.angle` | number |  | Pattern rotation in degrees (sets type 'Rotated' unless type is given) |
| `hatches[].orientation.xAxis` | any |  | Distortion: pattern X direction vector (sets type 'Distorted'); give together with yAxis |
| `hatches[].orientation.yAxis` | any |  | Distortion: pattern Y direction vector |
| `hatches[].orientation.innerRadius` | number |  | Radial fills: inner radius in m (0 = none) |
| `hatches[].orientation.fitX` | boolean |  | Symbol fills: stretch the pattern width to the xAxis length |
| `hatches[].orientation.fitY` | boolean |  | Symbol fills: stretch the pattern height to the yAxis length |
| `hatches[].orientation.keepProportion` | boolean |  | Symbol fills: keep the pattern proportion when fitting |
| `hatches[].orientation.globalOrigin` | boolean |  | true = pattern origin at the project origin |
| `hatches[].showArea` | boolean |  | Show the area text (placed in the middle of the polygon unless areaText.position is given) |
| `hatches[].areaText` | object |  | Area text settings (with showArea: true) |
| `hatches[].areaText.position` | any |  | Area text position (m) |
| `hatches[].areaText.pen` | any |  |  |
| `hatches[].areaText.font` | any |  | Font name or index |
| `hatches[].areaText.size` | number |  | Text height in mm |
| `hatches[].areaText.angle` | number |  | Rotation in degrees |
| `hatches[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `hatches[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `hatches[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `hatches[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `hatches[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |

### `create_texts`

Places text blocks (Archicad 'Text' tool): position (m) + text ('\n' = new line, any language incl. Cyrillic) or rich-text runs with per-run pen/font/size/bold/italic/underline/strikeout/superscript/subscript; plus anchor, justification, angle, widthFactor, charSpacing, wrapWidth (mm), frame, background, alwaysReadable. Text size is in MILLIMETERS on paper (it scales with the drawing scale unless fixedSize). Fonts by name ('Arial') — get_attributes {type: 'Font'}. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details. Restyling via modify_elements (e.g. {guid, size: 5, bold: true}) keeps the content.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `texts` | object[] | yes | Texts to place |
| `texts[].position` | object | yes | Anchor point of the text (m) |
| `texts[].text` | string |  | Text content (UTF-8, any language). Use '\n' for new lines. Give either text or runs |
| `texts[].runs` | object[] |  | Rich text: runs concatenated in order, each with its own style (e.g. a bold title run + a normal run). Give either text or runs |
| `texts[].runs[].text` | string | yes | Text of this run; may contain '\n' line breaks |
| `texts[].runs[].pen` | integer |  | Text pen (colour) |
| `texts[].runs[].font` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Font by name (e.g. 'Arial') or Font attribute index — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Font'} |
| `texts[].runs[].size` | number |  | Character height in mm (paper), e.g. 2.5 |
| `texts[].runs[].bold` | boolean |  |  |
| `texts[].runs[].italic` | boolean |  |  |
| `texts[].runs[].underline` | boolean |  |  |
| `texts[].runs[].strikeout` | boolean |  |  |
| `texts[].runs[].superscript` | boolean |  |  |
| `texts[].runs[].subscript` | boolean |  |  |
| `texts[].pen` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].font` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].size` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].bold` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].italic` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].underline` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].strikeout` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].superscript` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].subscript` | any |  |  |
| `texts[].justification` | "Left" \\| "Center" \\| "Right" \\| "Full" |  | Paragraph alignment |
| `texts[].lineSpacing` | number |  | Archicad line-spacing value of the paragraphs (advanced; keep the tool default unless you know the convention) |
| `texts[].anchor` | "TopLeft" \\| "TopCenter" \\| "TopRight" \\| "MiddleLeft" \\| "Center" \\| "MiddleRight" \\| "BottomLeft" \\| "BottomCenter" \\| "BottomRight" |  | Which point of the text block sits at 'position' (default: tool setting) |
| `texts[].angle` | number |  | Rotation in degrees (counter-clockwise) |
| `texts[].widthFactor` | number |  | Character width factor (1 = normal, 0.8 = condensed) |
| `texts[].charSpacing` | number |  | Character spacing factor (1 = normal) |
| `texts[].wrapWidth` | number |  | Wrap the text inside a box of this width in mm (paper); 0 = no wrapping (lines break only at '\n') |
| `texts[].fixedSize` | boolean |  | true = the text size does not scale with the output scale |
| `texts[].framePen` | any |  | Pen of the text frame |
| `texts[].background` | boolean |  | Opaque background behind the text |
| `texts[].backgroundPen` | any |  | Pen (colour) of the background |
| `texts[].alwaysReadable` | boolean |  | Keep the text readable (flip it) when rotated upside down |
| `texts[].frame` | boolean |  | Draw a frame around the text |
| `texts[].frameOffset` | number |  | Gap between text and frame (mm) |
| `texts[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `texts[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `texts[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `texts[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_labels`

Places labels with a leader line. ASSOCIATIVE labels (parent = element GUID) stick to the element and can show its data via the Label tool's autotext default content; INDEPENDENT labels need 'begin'. Leader: begin (arrow point) -> middle -> end (text). Text labels take text/runs and the same text style fields as create_texts; symbol labels (labelClass 'Symbol') use a Label library part (libraryPart, gdlParameters — find parts with search_library_parts {type: 'Label'}). For associative labels without 'end' Archicad uses its default label position. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details. The parent and the class of an existing label cannot be changed (delete and recreate).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `labels` | object[] | yes | Labels to place |
| `labels[].parent` | string \\| object |  | ASSOCIATIVE label: GUID of the element to label (wall, slab, object, door, zone, hatch ...). The label follows the element and its autotext shows the element's data. Omit for an independent label |
| `labels[].parent.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `labels[].labelClass` | "Text" \\| "Symbol" |  | 'Text' = text label (text/runs); 'Symbol' = GDL label library part (libraryPart). Default: Label tool setting |
| `labels[].begin` | object |  | Leader start = arrow point on the element (m). Required for independent labels; default for associative labels: a point of the parent |
| `labels[].middle` | any |  | Leader break point (m); needs end. Default: halfway between begin and end |
| `labels[].end` | any |  | Leader end where the text sits (m). Omit on associative labels to let Archicad use its default label position |
| `labels[].leader` | object |  | Leader line settings |
| `labels[].leader.show` | boolean |  | Show the leader line |
| `labels[].leader.shape` | "Segmented" \\| "Spline" \\| "SquareRoot" |  | Leader shape |
| `labels[].leader.pen` | integer |  | Leader pen |
| `labels[].leader.lineType` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Leader line type (name/index) |
| `labels[].leader.arrows` | object |  | Arrowhead of the leader (the arrow point is 'begin') |
| `labels[].leader.anchor` | "Middle" \\| "Top" \\| "Bottom" \\| "Underlined" |  | Where the leader attaches to the text |
| `labels[].leader.squareRootAngle` | number |  | Angle of the 'SquareRoot' leader shape in degrees |
| `labels[].frame` | boolean |  | Frame around the label text |
| `labels[].frameOffset` | number |  | Gap between the text and its frame |
| `labels[].textOrientation` | "Parallel" \\| "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" \\| "General" |  | Text direction: 'Horizontal' (default for new text labels), 'Parallel' (to the labelled element), 'Vertical', or 'General' (uses angle) |
| `labels[].alwaysReadable` | boolean |  | Flip the text so it is never upside down |
| `labels[].angle` | number |  | Text/symbol rotation in degrees (with textOrientation 'General') |
| `labels[].text` | string |  | Text label content; '\n' = new line. Omit to keep the Label tool's default content (e.g. autotext of an associative label) |
| `labels[].runs` | object[] |  | Rich text: runs concatenated in order, each with its own style (e.g. a bold title run + a normal run). Give either text or runs |
| `labels[].runs[].text` | string | yes | Text of this run; may contain '\n' line breaks |
| `labels[].runs[].pen` | any |  | Text pen (colour) |
| `labels[].runs[].font` | any |  | Font by name (e.g. 'Arial') or Font attribute index — list them with get_attributes {type: 'Font'} |
| `labels[].runs[].size` | number |  | Character height in mm (paper), e.g. 2.5 |
| `labels[].runs[].bold` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].runs[].italic` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].runs[].underline` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].runs[].strikeout` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].runs[].superscript` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].runs[].subscript` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].pen` | any |  | Text pen (text labels) / symbol pen (symbol labels) |
| `labels[].font` | any |  |  |
| `labels[].size` | any |  |  |
| `labels[].bold` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].italic` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].underline` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].strikeout` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].superscript` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].subscript` | boolean |  |  |
| `labels[].justification` | "Left" \\| "Center" \\| "Right" \\| "Full" |  | Paragraph alignment |
| `labels[].lineSpacing` | number |  | Archicad line-spacing value of the paragraphs (advanced; keep the tool default unless you know the convention) |
| `labels[].widthFactor` | number |  | Character width factor (1 = normal, 0.8 = condensed) |
| `labels[].charSpacing` | number |  | Character spacing factor (1 = normal) |
| `labels[].wrapWidth` | number |  | Wrap the label text inside a box of this width in mm (paper); new text labels do not wrap unless given (lines break only at '\n') |
| `labels[].fixedSize` | boolean |  | true = the text size does not scale with the output scale |
| `labels[].framePen` | any |  | Pen of the text frame |
| `labels[].background` | boolean |  | Opaque background behind the label text |
| `labels[].backgroundPen` | any |  | Background pen (colour) |
| `labels[].libraryPart` | string \\| integer \\| object |  | Symbol labels: a Label library part (LOCALIZED name — find it with search_library_parts {type: 'Label'}) |
| `labels[].libraryPart.name` | string | yes |  |
| `labels[].gdlParameters` | object |  | Symbol labels: GDL parameter values {name: value} of the label library part |
| `labels[].wrapText` | boolean |  | Symbol labels: wrap the text |
| `labels[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `labels[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `labels[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `labels[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_hotspots`

Places hotspots (snap points) at 2D positions (m) with an optional elevation (height, m) and pen — useful as reference/snap points for later drawing. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Change them later with modify_elements using the same field names; read them back with get_element_details.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hotspots` | object[] | yes | Hotspots to place |
| `hotspots[].position` | object | yes | Hotspot position (m) |
| `hotspots[].height` | number |  | Elevation of the hotspot (m, relative to the home story) — snaps in 3D/sections |
| `hotspots[].pen` | integer |  |  |
| `hotspots[].layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `hotspots[].layer.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `hotspots[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `hotspots[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `hotspots[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_pictures`

Places raster images (PNG, JPEG, GIF, TIFF, BMP) from ABSOLUTE file paths on this Mac, e.g. a logo on a layout or a site photo on a worksheet. Size in meters via width and/or height (aspect ratio kept when only one is given); without a size the picture is placed at its pixel size with its bottom-left corner at position. Optional anchor, angle, mirrored, transparent, name. Elements go into the database of the ACTIVE window: on a floor plan the given storyIndex (default: current story), otherwise the open section / elevation / detail / worksheet / layout (a 3D window cannot hold 2D elements). Returns [{guid, type} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others. Position/size/angle can be changed later with modify_elements; the image itself cannot be replaced.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pictures` | object[] | yes | Pictures to place |
| `pictures[].file` | string | yes | ABSOLUTE path of an image file on this Mac (PNG, JPEG, GIF, TIFF or BMP; max 32767 px per side, 200 MB) |
| `pictures[].position` | object | yes | Where the picture's anchor point goes (m) |
| `pictures[].width` | number |  | Width in m; with only width or height the other follows the image aspect ratio |
| `pictures[].height` | number |  | Height in m |
| `pictures[].anchor` | "TopLeft" \\| "TopCenter" \\| "TopRight" \\| "MiddleLeft" \\| "Center" \\| "MiddleRight" \\| "BottomLeft" \\| "BottomCenter" \\| "BottomRight" |  | Which point of the picture sits at 'position' (default BottomLeft; other anchors need width or height) |
| `pictures[].angle` | number |  | Rotation in degrees |
| `pictures[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror horizontally |
| `pictures[].transparent` | boolean |  | Transparent background (white/alpha) |
| `pictures[].name` | string |  | Picture name (default: the file name) |
| `pictures[].layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `pictures[].layer.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `pictures[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `pictures[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `pictures[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |


## dimensions

### `create_dimensions`

Creates linear dimension chains (one undo step). Each dimension = ordered 'points' + a dimension line (direction + linePoint/offset). Points: {x, y} static, or linked to elements (associative, the dimension follows edits): {element, at: 'begin'|'end'} for wall/beam/line reference-line ends, {element, x, y} for the element anchor (hotspot, corner, opening point) nearest to x,y — list anchors with get_dimension_anchors. Distances are measured along 'direction' (e.g. 'Horizontal' = X distances). Units: m; text/marker sizes in paper mm. Style defaults come from the Dimension tool settings. Dimensions linked to model elements are placed on the Floor Plan (story: storyIndex, default current); static ones go into the active window (plan, section, detail, worksheet, layout). Linked points that Archicad cannot resolve fall back to static (see 'warning'). For walls prefer dimension_walls (mode 'Thickness' for wall widths). Returns [{guid, type, pointCount, associativePoints, segments (measured lengths), total, linePoint} | {error}] in input order; one failing item does not stop the others.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `dimensions` | object[] | yes |  |
| `dimensions[].points` | object[] | yes | The measured points IN ORDER along the chain (at least 2). Each consecutive pair gives one segment |
| `dimensions[].points[].x` | number |  | X in m. Alone with y: a static point. With 'element': a point NEAR the element point to link (snapped within snapTolerance) |
| `dimensions[].points[].y` | number |  | Y in m (see x) |
| `dimensions[].points[].element` | string \\| object |  | Link this point to an element (associative: the dimension follows when the element moves) |
| `dimensions[].points[].at` | "begin" \\| "end" |  | With a Wall / Beam / Line 'element': link to the begin or end of its reference line (no x,y needed) |
| `dimensions[].points[].inIndex` | integer |  | EXPERT: explicit Archicad reference (API_Base) — copy element/inIndex/line/special from an existing dimension's details or get_dimension_anchors; needs x,y too (approximate location). Wall thickness (DevKit convention): {element: wall, inIndex: 11, line: true, x, y} and {element: wall, inIndex: 21, line: true, x, y} — the two wall faces — with a direction perpendicular to the wall |
| `dimensions[].points[].line` | boolean |  | EXPERT (with inIndex): the reference is a line/edge of the element, not a point |
| `dimensions[].points[].special` | boolean |  | EXPERT (with inIndex): Archicad's 'special' reference flag (wall corners, opening holes) |
| `dimensions[].points[].nodeId` | integer |  | EXPERT (with inIndex): polygon vertex id |
| `dimensions[].points[].witnessForm` | "None" \\| "Small" \\| "Large" \\| "Fixed" |  | Witness line form of this point only |
| `dimensions[].points[].witnessVal` | number |  | Witness line value: the gap between the dimensioned point and the witness line (form 'Large') or the witness line length (form 'Fixed'), in the unit Archicad stores — read witnessVal of an existing dimension (get_element_details) or of the tool defaults first |
| `dimensions[].points[].text` | string \\| boolean |  | Custom text shown at this point: in a 'Normal' chain the text of the segment ENDING here (e.g. 'EQ'; meaningless on the first point), in 'Cumulative'/'Elevation' chains the point's own value |
| `dimensions[].direction` | "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" \\| number \\| object |  | Dimension line direction (default: from the first point towards the next distinct point). Distances are measured PROJECTED on this direction, e.g. 'Horizontal' gives X distances even for diagonal points |
| `dimensions[].linePoint` | object |  | A point the dimension line passes through (m). Alternative to offset |
| `dimensions[].offset` | number |  | Distance (m) of the dimension line from the FIRST point, perpendicular to the direction: + = left of the direction (above a left-to-right chain), - = right/below. Default 1 |
| `dimensions[].associative` | boolean |  | Link points that reference elements (default true). false = every point is static |
| `dimensions[].snapTolerance` | number |  | Max distance (m, default 0.02) between an {element, x, y} point and the element anchor it links to; farther points stay static |
| `dimensions[].appearance` | "Normal" \\| "Cumulative" \\| "CumulativeSV" \\| "Elevation" |  | Chain type: 'Normal' (segment lengths, default), 'Cumulative' (running distances from the first point), 'CumulativeSV' (Archicad's other cumulative variant), 'Elevation' (elevation-type values) |
| `dimensions[].textPosition` | "Above" \\| "In" \\| "Below" |  | Where the value text sits relative to the dimension line: 'Above' (default of most standards), 'In' (breaks the line), 'Below' |
| `dimensions[].textDirection` | "Parallel" \\| "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" |  | Text orientation: 'Parallel' to the dimension line (default), always 'Horizontal', or 'Vertical' |
| `dimensions[].linePen` | integer |  | Pen of the dimension and witness lines |
| `dimensions[].witnessForm` | any |  | Default witness line form of every point |
| `dimensions[].witnessVal` | any |  | Witness line value: the gap between the dimensioned point and the witness line (form 'Large') or the witness line length (form 'Fixed'), in the unit Archicad stores — read witnessVal of an existing dimension (get_element_details) or of the tool defaults first |
| `dimensions[].horizontalText` | boolean |  | Keep the texts horizontal |
| `dimensions[].onlyDimensionText` | boolean |  | Show only the texts (no lines/markers) |
| `dimensions[].layout` | "Legacy" \\| "Flexible" \\| "Centered" \\| "Off" |  | Text layout algorithm for crowded chains ('Flexible' moves colliding texts) |
| `dimensions[].clipOtherSide` | boolean |  | Do not draw the witness line part beyond the dimensioned point |
| `dimensions[].markerType` | "CrossLine" \\| "EmptyCircle" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "OpenArrow90" \\| "ClosedArrow90" \\| "FullArrow90" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "OpenArrow60" \\| "ClosedArrow60" \\| "FullArrow60" \\| "SlashLine75" |  | Dimension line end marker: ticks ('SlashLine45', 'SlashLine60', 'SlashLine75', 'CrossLine'), arrows ('OpenArrow30', 'FullArrow30', ...), dots ('FullCircle', 'EmptyCircle', 'PepitaCircle', 'CrossCircle'), 'BandArrow' |
| `dimensions[].markerPen` | any |  | Pen of the markers |
| `dimensions[].markerSize` | number |  | Marker size in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2) |
| `dimensions[].textPen` | any |  | Pen (color) of the dimension text, 1-255 |
| `dimensions[].textSize` | number |  | Text height in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2.5), independent of the drawing scale |
| `dimensions[].textFont` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Font by name (e.g. 'Arial') or font attribute index |
| `dimensions[].textFont.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `dimensions[].textBold` | boolean |  |  |
| `dimensions[].textItalic` | boolean |  |  |
| `dimensions[].textUnderline` | boolean |  |  |
| `dimensions[].textFrame` | boolean |  | Draw a frame around the text |
| `dimensions[].textOpaque` | boolean |  | Opaque (filled) text background |
| `dimensions[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `dimensions[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `dimensions[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `dimensions[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_level_dimensions`

Places level (elevation) dimension markers (one undo step). Each marker shows the story level at 'position', or the level of an 'element' (slab/mesh/roof/stair top), or a static 'level' value. Markers on an element are placed on the Floor Plan (story: storyIndex, default current); the others go into the active window (normally the Floor Plan). Units: m, degrees, marker/text sizes in paper mm. Unspecified settings come from the Level Dimension tool. Returns [{guid, type, position, level, static, element?, storyLevel} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `levelDimensions` | object[] | yes |  |
| `levelDimensions[].position` | object | yes | Where the level marker is placed (m) |
| `levelDimensions[].element` | string \\| object |  | Show the level of this element at the marker (Slab, Mesh, Roof, Shell, Stair, Object...: typically its top surface). Omit = the story level |
| `levelDimensions[].element.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `levelDimensions[].level` | number |  | Static level value in m (makes the marker static: it no longer follows the story/element) |
| `levelDimensions[].static` | boolean |  | Static marker (keeps its value); set false to follow the story/element again |
| `levelDimensions[].elevationReference` | "ProjectZero" \\| "ReferenceLevel1" \\| "ReferenceLevel2" \\| "StoredOrigin" \\| "SeaLevel" |  | What the shown elevation is measured from: 'ProjectZero', 'ReferenceLevel1'/'ReferenceLevel2' (the project's reference levels), 'StoredOrigin', 'SeaLevel' (altitude). Default from the Level Dimension tool (usually ProjectZero) |
| `levelDimensions[].markerStyle` | integer |  | Marker form 0..9 (Archicad's level dimension marker types) |
| `levelDimensions[].markerSize` | number |  | Marker size in PAPER millimeters |
| `levelDimensions[].pen` | integer |  | Pen of the marker |
| `levelDimensions[].markerAngle` | number |  | Rotation of the marker in degrees |
| `levelDimensions[].showPlusSign` | boolean |  | Show '+' before positive values |
| `levelDimensions[].text` | string \\| boolean |  | Custom text replacing the measured value (e.g. 'EQ', 'Ø 600'); "" or false = show the measured value again |
| `levelDimensions[].secondText` | any |  | Second note (marker styles 8 and 9 only) |
| `levelDimensions[].textPen` | any |  | Pen (color) of the dimension text, 1-255 |
| `levelDimensions[].textSize` | number |  | Text height in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2.5), independent of the drawing scale |
| `levelDimensions[].textFont` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Font by name (e.g. 'Arial') or font attribute index |
| `levelDimensions[].textFont.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `levelDimensions[].textBold` | boolean |  |  |
| `levelDimensions[].textItalic` | boolean |  |  |
| `levelDimensions[].textUnderline` | boolean |  |  |
| `levelDimensions[].textFrame` | boolean |  | Draw a frame around the text |
| `levelDimensions[].textOpaque` | boolean |  | Opaque (filled) text background |
| `levelDimensions[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `levelDimensions[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `levelDimensions[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `levelDimensions[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_radial_dimensions`

Dimensions the radius of arcs, circles, curved walls or curved beams (one undo step). The dimension is linked to the element and verified (the measured radius must match the element). 'at' picks where it touches the arc, 'lineEnd' where the radial line ends. Walls/beams must be on the Floor Plan; arcs/circles in the active window's drawing. Returns [{guid, type, radius, associative, element, arcPoint, lineEnd} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `radialDimensions` | object[] | yes |  |
| `radialDimensions[].element` | string \\| object | yes | The Arc, Circle, curved Wall (arcAngle != 0) or curved Beam to dimension |
| `radialDimensions[].element.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `radialDimensions[].at` | object |  | A point near the arc where the dimension touches it (projected onto the arc). Default: the middle of the arc |
| `radialDimensions[].lineEnd` | any |  | End point of the radial dimension line (default: the arc point). Place it outside the arc to pull the text out |
| `radialDimensions[].inIndex` | integer |  | EXPERT: explicit reference index of the element (copy from an existing radial dimension's details) |
| `radialDimensions[].line` | boolean |  | EXPERT (with inIndex): reference is the element's line (default true) |
| `radialDimensions[].special` | boolean |  | EXPERT (with inIndex) |
| `radialDimensions[].showCenter` | boolean |  | Mark the arc center |
| `radialDimensions[].prefix` | string |  | Text before the value, e.g. 'R' or 'R=' |
| `radialDimensions[].textPosition` | "Above" \\| "In" \\| "Below" |  | Where the value text sits relative to the dimension line: 'Above' (default of most standards), 'In' (breaks the line), 'Below' |
| `radialDimensions[].textDirection` | "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" \\| "Radial" |  | Text orientation: 'Radial' follows the radius line |
| `radialDimensions[].linePen` | integer |  | Pen of the dimension line |
| `radialDimensions[].onlyDimensionText` | boolean |  | Show only the text |
| `radialDimensions[].text` | string \\| boolean |  | Custom text replacing the measured value (e.g. 'EQ', 'Ø 600'); "" or false = show the measured value again |
| `radialDimensions[].markerType` | "CrossLine" \\| "EmptyCircle" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "OpenArrow90" \\| "ClosedArrow90" \\| "FullArrow90" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "OpenArrow60" \\| "ClosedArrow60" \\| "FullArrow60" \\| "SlashLine75" |  | Dimension line end marker: ticks ('SlashLine45', 'SlashLine60', 'SlashLine75', 'CrossLine'), arrows ('OpenArrow30', 'FullArrow30', ...), dots ('FullCircle', 'EmptyCircle', 'PepitaCircle', 'CrossCircle'), 'BandArrow' |
| `radialDimensions[].markerPen` | any |  | Pen of the markers |
| `radialDimensions[].markerSize` | number |  | Marker size in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2) |
| `radialDimensions[].textPen` | any |  | Pen (color) of the dimension text, 1-255 |
| `radialDimensions[].textSize` | number |  | Text height in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2.5), independent of the drawing scale |
| `radialDimensions[].textFont` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Font by name (e.g. 'Arial') or font attribute index |
| `radialDimensions[].textFont.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `radialDimensions[].textBold` | boolean |  |  |
| `radialDimensions[].textItalic` | boolean |  |  |
| `radialDimensions[].textUnderline` | boolean |  |  |
| `radialDimensions[].textFrame` | boolean |  | Draw a frame around the text |
| `radialDimensions[].textOpaque` | boolean |  | Opaque (filled) text background |
| `radialDimensions[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `radialDimensions[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `radialDimensions[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `radialDimensions[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `create_angle_dimensions`

Dimensions the angle between two non-parallel lines (one undo step): static 'line1'/'line2' {begin, end}, or two straight walls/beams/lines ('elements', linked to their end points when possible, verified, else static). The arc is centered on the lines' intersection; 'arcPoint' (a point on the arc) or 'radius' (m, default 1) places it; by default it sits between the far ends of both lines. Returns [{guid, type, angle (degrees), radius, center, arcPoint, associative} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `angleDimensions` | object[] | yes |  |
| `angleDimensions[].line1` | object |  | First line (static) |
| `angleDimensions[].line1.begin` | object | yes | 2D point in meters (project coordinates) |
| `angleDimensions[].line1.end` | any | yes |  |
| `angleDimensions[].line2` | any |  | Second line (static) |
| `angleDimensions[].elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Instead of line1/line2: two straight Walls / Beams / Lines (their reference lines; linked to their end points when possible) |
| `angleDimensions[].associative` | boolean |  | With elements: link to the element end points (default true) |
| `angleDimensions[].arcPoint` | any |  | A point the dimension arc passes through: picks the measured sector and the arc radius |
| `angleDimensions[].radius` | number |  | Arc radius in m (default 1; with arcPoint: keeps its direction, sets the distance) |
| `angleDimensions[].smallArc` | boolean |  | Measure the smaller angle between the lines (default true) |
| `angleDimensions[].textPosition` | "Above" \\| "In" \\| "Below" |  | Where the value text sits relative to the dimension line: 'Above' (default of most standards), 'In' (breaks the line), 'Below' |
| `angleDimensions[].textDirection` | "Parallel" \\| "Horizontal" \\| "Perpendicular" |  | Text orientation relative to the arc |
| `angleDimensions[].witnessForm` | "None" \\| "Small" \\| "Large" \\| "Fixed" |  | Witness (extension) line form: 'None', 'Small' (short tick), 'Large' (from the dimension line to near the element), 'Fixed' (fixed length) |
| `angleDimensions[].witnessVal` | number |  | Witness line value: the gap between the dimensioned point and the witness line (form 'Large') or the witness line length (form 'Fixed'), in the unit Archicad stores — read witnessVal of an existing dimension (get_element_details) or of the tool defaults first |
| `angleDimensions[].linePen` | integer |  | Pen of the arc and witness lines |
| `angleDimensions[].onlyDimensionText` | boolean |  |  |
| `angleDimensions[].text` | string \\| boolean |  | Custom text replacing the measured value (e.g. 'EQ', 'Ø 600'); "" or false = show the measured value again |
| `angleDimensions[].markerType` | "CrossLine" \\| "EmptyCircle" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "OpenArrow90" \\| "ClosedArrow90" \\| "FullArrow90" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "OpenArrow60" \\| "ClosedArrow60" \\| "FullArrow60" \\| "SlashLine75" |  | Dimension line end marker: ticks ('SlashLine45', 'SlashLine60', 'SlashLine75', 'CrossLine'), arrows ('OpenArrow30', 'FullArrow30', ...), dots ('FullCircle', 'EmptyCircle', 'PepitaCircle', 'CrossCircle'), 'BandArrow' |
| `angleDimensions[].markerPen` | any |  | Pen of the markers |
| `angleDimensions[].markerSize` | number |  | Marker size in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2) |
| `angleDimensions[].textPen` | any |  | Pen (color) of the dimension text, 1-255 |
| `angleDimensions[].textSize` | number |  | Text height in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2.5), independent of the drawing scale |
| `angleDimensions[].textFont` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Font by name (e.g. 'Arial') or font attribute index |
| `angleDimensions[].textFont.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `angleDimensions[].textBold` | boolean |  |  |
| `angleDimensions[].textItalic` | boolean |  |  |
| `angleDimensions[].textUnderline` | boolean |  |  |
| `angleDimensions[].textFrame` | boolean |  | Draw a frame around the text |
| `angleDimensions[].textOpaque` | boolean |  | Opaque (filled) text background |
| `angleDimensions[].layer` | any |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `angleDimensions[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `angleDimensions[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `angleDimensions[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |

### `dimension_walls`

Automatically dimensions walls on the Floor Plan (one undo step): wall end points plus window/door jambs (or centers), linked to the walls/openings so the dimensions follow later edits. mode 'Chain' (default) = ONE chain through all given walls, measured along 'direction' (default: first wall's begin→end) — e.g. pass all walls of a facade, including the perpendicular ones whose ends should appear; mode 'EachWall' = one dimension parallel to each wall; mode 'Thickness' = one dimension ACROSS each straight wall, linked to both wall faces (shows the wall width, follows thickness changes), crossing the wall at 'thicknessPosition'. For Chain/EachWall the line is placed 'distance' m beyond the outermost wall face on 'side' ('Auto' = away from the first wall's body); 'overall' adds a second line with the total length. Get wall GUIDs first (e.g. list_elements with types ['Wall'], find_elements or get_selection). Returns {results: [{guid, role: 'chain'|'overall'|'thickness', walls, pointCount, associativePoints, segments, total, linePoint, warnings?} | {error, wall?}]}; in EachWall/Thickness mode one failing wall does not stop the others.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `walls` | string \\| object[] | yes | Wall GUIDs |
| `mode` | "Chain" \\| "EachWall" \\| "Thickness" |  | 'Chain' (default): one chain for all walls; 'EachWall': one dimension per wall; 'Thickness': one width dimension across each wall |
| `direction` | "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" \\| number \\| object |  | Chain direction (default: the first wall's begin→end direction). Not with mode 'Thickness' |
| `side` | "Auto" \\| "Left" \\| "Right" |  | Side of the dimension line relative to the first wall's begin→end (or 'direction'): 'Auto' (default) = the side without the wall body |
| `distance` | number |  | Distance (m) from the outermost wall face to the dimension line (default 1) |
| `includeOpenings` | boolean |  | Dimension windows/doors of walls parallel to the chain (default true) |
| `openingPoints` | "Jambs" \\| "Centers" |  | 'Jambs' (default: both sides of each opening) or 'Centers' |
| `overall` | boolean |  | Also create an overall dimension (first to last point) further out |
| `overallSpacing` | number |  | Distance (m) between the chain and the overall line (default 0.8) |
| `thicknessPosition` | number |  | Mode 'Thickness': where the dimension crosses the wall, in m from the wall's begin point along its reference line (default: the middle). Values < 0 or > the wall length put the dimension beyond the wall ends |
| `associative` | boolean |  | Link points to walls/openings/wall faces (default true); false = static points |
| `storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Story of the dimensions (default: the wall's story — in 'Chain' mode the first wall's) |
| `storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `layer.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `appearance` | "Normal" \\| "Cumulative" \\| "CumulativeSV" \\| "Elevation" |  | Chain type: 'Normal' (segment lengths, default), 'Cumulative' (running distances from the first point), 'CumulativeSV' (Archicad's other cumulative variant), 'Elevation' (elevation-type values) |
| `textPosition` | "Above" \\| "In" \\| "Below" |  | Where the value text sits relative to the dimension line: 'Above' (default of most standards), 'In' (breaks the line), 'Below' |
| `textDirection` | "Parallel" \\| "Horizontal" \\| "Vertical" |  | Text orientation: 'Parallel' to the dimension line (default), always 'Horizontal', or 'Vertical' |
| `linePen` | integer |  | Pen of the dimension and witness lines |
| `witnessForm` | "None" \\| "Small" \\| "Large" \\| "Fixed" |  | Default witness line form of every point |
| `witnessVal` | number |  | Witness line value: the gap between the dimensioned point and the witness line (form 'Large') or the witness line length (form 'Fixed'), in the unit Archicad stores — read witnessVal of an existing dimension (get_element_details) or of the tool defaults first |
| `horizontalText` | boolean |  | Keep the texts horizontal |
| `onlyDimensionText` | boolean |  | Show only the texts (no lines/markers) |
| `layout` | "Legacy" \\| "Flexible" \\| "Centered" \\| "Off" |  | Text layout algorithm for crowded chains ('Flexible' moves colliding texts) |
| `clipOtherSide` | boolean |  | Do not draw the witness line part beyond the dimensioned point |
| `markerType` | "CrossLine" \\| "EmptyCircle" \\| "SlashLine60" \\| "OpenArrow30" \\| "ClosedArrow30" \\| "FullArrow30" \\| "SlashLine45" \\| "CrossCircle" \\| "OpenArrow90" \\| "ClosedArrow90" \\| "FullArrow90" \\| "FullCircle" \\| "PepitaCircle" \\| "BandArrow" \\| "OpenArrow60" \\| "ClosedArrow60" \\| "FullArrow60" \\| "SlashLine75" |  | Dimension line end marker: ticks ('SlashLine45', 'SlashLine60', 'SlashLine75', 'CrossLine'), arrows ('OpenArrow30', 'FullArrow30', ...), dots ('FullCircle', 'EmptyCircle', 'PepitaCircle', 'CrossCircle'), 'BandArrow' |
| `markerPen` | any |  | Pen of the markers |
| `markerSize` | number |  | Marker size in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2) |
| `textPen` | any |  | Pen (color) of the dimension text, 1-255 |
| `textSize` | number |  | Text height in PAPER millimeters (e.g. 2.5), independent of the drawing scale |
| `textFont` | any |  | Font by name (e.g. 'Arial') or font attribute index |
| `textBold` | boolean |  |  |
| `textItalic` | boolean |  |  |
| `textUnderline` | boolean |  |  |
| `textFrame` | boolean |  | Draw a frame around the text |
| `textOpaque` | boolean |  | Opaque (filled) text background |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `modify_dimensions`

Changes existing dimensions in one undo step. Each item is {guid, ...fields}; only the given fields change and only fields of the dimension's type apply. Linear: move the dimension line (linePoint, or offset from the first point), change direction, replace points, pointTexts (custom segment texts), makeStatic, style (pens, markers, texts, witness lines). Level: position, element, level/static, elevationReference, marker, text. Radial: at, lineEnd, prefix, showCenter. Angle: arcPoint/radius, smallArc, line1/line2. Common: layer, storyIndex.
