| `interiorElevations[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `interiorElevations[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `interiorElevations[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `interiorElevations[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  |  |

### `create_details`

Creates detail markers (one undo step) in the current floor plan/section/elevation (or the floor plan when another window is active): the boundary polygon or box (m) defines the region copied into a new detail viewpoint; the marker is placed at markerPosition. Returns [{guid, name, referenceId, database, markerPosition, polygon}]. Open the detail with open_view {element: guid}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `details` | object[] | yes |  |
| `details[].polygon` | object[] \\| object |  | Boundary of the detail/worksheet region (m). Give polygon or box |
| `details[].polygon.points` | any[] | yes | Vertices in order, do NOT repeat the first point at the end |
| `details[].polygon.arcs` | object[] |  | Curved edges |
| `details[].polygon.holes` | object[] |  | Inner holes, each {points, arcs?} |
| `details[].box` | object |  | Rectangular boundary instead of polygon |
| `details[].box.xMin` | number | yes | Left edge (m, project coordinates; paper mm-coordinates in layouts) |
| `details[].box.yMin` | number | yes | Bottom edge |
| `details[].box.xMax` | number | yes | Right edge |
| `details[].box.yMax` | number | yes | Top edge |
| `details[].markerPosition` | object |  | Where the marker is placed (default: 0.5 m beyond the top-right corner of the boundary) |
| `details[].markerAngle` | number |  | Marker rotation in degrees |
| `details[].horizontalMarker` | boolean |  | Keep the marker text horizontal |
| `details[].name` | string |  | Viewpoint name shown in the Navigator and in the marker (e.g. 'Section A-A') |
| `details[].referenceId` | string |  | Reference ID shown in the marker (e.g. 'A'); Archicad may auto-number when omitted |
| `details[].layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `details[].layer.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `details[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `details[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `details[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `details[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  |  |

### `create_worksheets`

Creates worksheet markers (one undo step) like create_details: boundary polygon or box (m) + marker position; each gets a new worksheet viewpoint (a 2D drafting sheet seeded with the region's drawing). Returns [{guid, name, database, ...}]. Open with open_view {element: guid}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `worksheets` | object[] | yes |  |
| `worksheets[].polygon` | object[] \\| object |  | Boundary of the detail/worksheet region (m). Give polygon or box |
| `worksheets[].polygon.points` | any[] | yes | Vertices in order, do NOT repeat the first point at the end |
| `worksheets[].polygon.arcs` | object[] |  | Curved edges |
| `worksheets[].polygon.holes` | object[] |  | Inner holes, each {points, arcs?} |
| `worksheets[].box` | object |  | Rectangular boundary instead of polygon |
| `worksheets[].box.xMin` | number | yes | Left edge (m, project coordinates; paper mm-coordinates in layouts) |
| `worksheets[].box.yMin` | number | yes | Bottom edge |
| `worksheets[].box.xMax` | number | yes | Right edge |
| `worksheets[].box.yMax` | number | yes | Top edge |
| `worksheets[].markerPosition` | object |  | Where the marker is placed (default: 0.5 m beyond the top-right corner of the boundary) |
| `worksheets[].markerAngle` | number |  | Marker rotation in degrees |
| `worksheets[].horizontalMarker` | boolean |  | Keep the marker text horizontal |
| `worksheets[].name` | string |  | Viewpoint name shown in the Navigator and in the marker (e.g. 'Section A-A') |
| `worksheets[].referenceId` | string |  | Reference ID shown in the marker (e.g. 'A'); Archicad may auto-number when omitted |
| `worksheets[].layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer name or index (defaults to the tool's default layer) |
| `worksheets[].layer.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `worksheets[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story (defaults to the current story) |
| `worksheets[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `worksheets[].renovationStatus` | "Existing" \\| "New" \\| "Demolished" \\| "Default" |  | Renovation status |
| `worksheets[].elementId` | string |  | Element ID text shown in the Info Box / schedules |
| `undoName` | string |  |  |

### `get_view_settings`

Without 'view': settings of the ACTIVE window — drawingScale (N of 1:N), layerCombination, penSet, structureDisplay, renovationFilter (+ available filters) and the model view option combinations matching the current options. With 'view' (View Map item GUID/name): the settings stored in that saved view (layer combination, model view options, pen set, dimension style, scale, structure display, zoom, renovation filter, graphic overrides, 3D style, rendering scene; storedInView tells which ones the view stores).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `view` | string \\| object |  | Saved view (View Map). Omit for the current window |
| `view.guid` | string | yes | Navigator item GUID (from list_views) |

### `set_view_settings`

Changes view settings of the ACTIVE window (drawingScale, layerCombination, modelViewOptions, structureDisplay, renovationFilter, style3D) or — with 'view' — of a saved View Map view (additionally penSet, dimensionStyle, graphicOverrides, zoom, ignoreSavedZoom, renderingScene; the view then stores these settings). Attribute names are localized: list them with get_attributes (types LayerCombination, ModelViewOption, PenTable, DimensionStandard). Returns {changed, settings}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `view` | string \\| object |  | Saved view to change (View Map). Omit to change the current window |
| `view.guid` | string | yes | Navigator item GUID (from list_views) |
| `drawingScale` | number |  | Scale denominator N of 1:N, e.g. 50 or 100 |
| `layerCombination` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer combination (name/index) |
| `layerCombination.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `modelViewOptions` | any |  | Model view options combination (name/index) |
| `penSet` | any |  | Saved views only: pen set (PenTable attribute) |
| `dimensionStyle` | any |  | Saved views only: dimension standard |
| `graphicOverrides` | string |  | Saved views only: graphic override combination name |
| `renovationFilter` | string |  | Renovation filter name or GUID (see get_view_settings availableRenovationFilters) |
| `structureDisplay` | "EntireStructure" \\| "CoreOnly" \\| "WithoutFinishes" \\| "StructureOnly" |  | Partial structure display |
| `style3D` | string |  | 3D style name (current 3D window, or stored in a saved 3D view) |
| `renderingScene` | string |  | Saved views only: rendering scene name |
| `zoom` | object |  | Saved views only: stored zoom area (m) |
| `zoom.xMin` | number | yes | Left edge (m, project coordinates; paper mm-coordinates in layouts) |
| `zoom.yMin` | number | yes | Bottom edge |
| `zoom.xMax` | number | yes | Right edge |
| `zoom.yMax` | number | yes | Top edge |
| `ignoreSavedZoom` | boolean |  | Saved views only: open the view without its stored zoom |


## documentation

### `get_databases`

Lists Archicad databases — the floor plan, sections, elevations, interior elevations, details, worksheets, 3D documents, layouts and master layouts — with databaseRef ("FloorPlan" or a guid), type, name, reference ID, title and the linked marker element. Layouts also get their Layout Book navigatorItemGuid, layoutId, master layout and sheet {width, height, margins} in meters. The databaseRef / navigatorItemGuid values are what place_drawing, get_layout_drawings, update_drawings and the export tools take. includeViews adds, per database, the View Map views that show it (navigatorItemGuid, scale) — the best sources for place_drawing. Also returns the current database/window. Names are localized (Russian Archicad).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `types` | "FloorPlan" \\| "Section" \\| "Elevation" \\| "InteriorElevation" \\| "Detail" \\| "Worksheet" \\| "DocumentFrom3D" \\| "Layout" \\| "MasterLayout"[] |  | Only these database types (default: all) |
| `search` | string |  | Only databases whose name, reference ID or title contains this text (case-insensitive) |
| `includeViews` | boolean |  | Add the View Map views of each database (default false) |
| `includeLayoutInfo` | boolean |  | Add sheet size/margins/numbering of layouts (default true) |
| `navigatorItems` | string[] |  | Also resolve these navigator item guids to their database and scale |

### `get_layout_drawings`

Lists the drawings placed on layouts: guid, name, number, source view (navigatorItemGuid, name, type, link type, viewDeleted), status (UpToDate | Modified), position and bounds in PAPER meters, anchor, angle, ratio, viewScale and effective scale (denominator, e.g. 100), crop frame polygon, title, border, pen table / color mode, update mode. Default: every layout. Use it before modify_drawings / update_drawings, and to check what a layout contains.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `layouts` | string[] |  | Only these layouts (default: all layouts) |
| `includeMasterLayouts` | boolean |  | With no 'layouts': also scan master layouts (default false) |
| `includeFrame` | boolean |  | Include the crop/bounding polygon of each drawing (default true) |
| `includeLinkInfo` | boolean |  | Include name/number and source link details (default true) |

### `place_drawing`

Places views as drawings on layouts (one undo step for the batch). Each item: layout + source ('view' navigator guid — preferably a View Map view from get_databases {includeViews: true} — or 'database', e.g. a section databaseRef, or 'FloorPlan' + storyIndex), then optional position (paper meters from the sheet's bottom-left corner, default the sheet center), anchor, scale (100 = 1:100) or ratio, angle, name / number, crop frame, title, border, pen set, update mode, layer. The first target layout is brought to the front unless restoreWindow. Returns [{guid, layout, source, drawing: {position, bounds, scale, status ...}} | {error}] in input order. Create layouts with create_layout.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `drawings` | object[] | yes | Drawings to place |
| `drawings[].layout` | string | yes | Target layout (get_databases {types: ['Layout']}; create one with create_layout) |
| `drawings[].view` | string |  | Source view/viewpoint navigator guid (preferred: a View Map view carries layers/scale/zoom) |
| `drawings[].database` | string |  | Alternative source: a database (section, elevation, detail, worksheet, 'FloorPlan' + storyIndex); its View Map view is used, else its Project Map viewpoint |
| `drawings[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | With database 'FloorPlan': which story (default: the current story) |
| `drawings[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `drawings[].position` | object |  | Placement point on the sheet in PAPER meters from the sheet's bottom-left corner (A3 landscape is 0.42 x 0.297, its center 0.21, 0.1485). Default: the sheet center. Sheet sizes: get_databases (sheet.width/height) |
| `drawings[].anchor` | "LeftTop" \\| "CenterTop" \\| "RightTop" \\| "LeftCenter" \\| "Center" \\| "RightCenter" \\| "LeftBottom" \\| "CenterBottom" \\| "RightBottom" |  | Which point of the drawing's bounding box sits at 'position' (default Center) |
| `drawings[].useViewOrigin` | boolean |  | Place by the view's own origin (project 0,0) instead of the bounding-box anchor |
| `drawings[].scale` | number |  | Scale denominator on paper: 100 = 1:100, 50 = 1:50. Default: the view's own scale. Use scale OR ratio |
| `drawings[].ratio` | number |  | Magnification relative to the view's own scale (1 = as the view, 2 = twice as large) |
| `drawings[].angle` | number |  | Rotation on the sheet in degrees (Archicad's drawing angle, which the API defines clockwise) |
| `drawings[].name` | string |  | Custom drawing name (sets nameType 'Custom' unless nameType is given) |
| `drawings[].nameType` | "ViewOrSourceFileName" \\| "ViewIdAndName" \\| "Custom" |  | Where the drawing name comes from: the view name, the view ID + name, or the custom 'name' |
| `drawings[].number` | string |  | Custom drawing number (sets numbering 'Custom' unless numbering is given) |
| `drawings[].numbering` | "ByLayout" \\| "ByViewId" \\| "Custom" |  | Drawing numbering: by layout sequence, by the view ID, or the custom 'number' |
| `drawings[].includeInNumbering` | boolean |  | Take part in the layout's drawing numbering sequence |
| `drawings[].includeInAutoTexts` | boolean |  | Include in drawing-scale autotexts and in drawing/layout indexes |
| `drawings[].manualUpdate` | boolean |  | true = the drawing is only refreshed by update_drawings; false = automatic update (default of the tool) |
| `drawings[].colorMode` | "OriginalColors" \\| "BlackAndWhite" \\| "GrayScale" |  | Pen color mode of the placed drawing |
| `drawings[].penTable` | string \\| integer \\| string \\| object |  | Pen set used for the drawing: 'Own' (the view's), 'Model' (the model's) or a pen set name/index |
| `drawings[].transparentBackground` | boolean |  | Transparent drawing background |
| `drawings[].border` | object |  | Border line around the drawing |
| `drawings[].border.show` | boolean |  | Draw a border (default true when 'border' is given on placement) |
| `drawings[].border.pen` | integer |  | Pen index 1-255 |
| `drawings[].border.lineType` | any |  | Line type name/index of the border |
| `drawings[].border.size` | number |  | Border line thickness in m |
| `drawings[].title` | boolean \\| string \\| integer \\| object |  | Drawing title: false = no title, or a drawing-title library part (localized name, e.g. from search_library_parts) |
| `drawings[].frame` | boolean \\| object[] \\| object |  | Crop the drawing with this polygon (PAPER meters on the sheet); false = remove the crop |
| `drawings[].layer` | any |  | Layer of the drawing element |
| `restoreWindow` | boolean |  | Return to the previous front window afterwards (default false: the layout stays in front) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `modify_drawings`

Changes drawings already placed on layouts (one undo step): position (paper m), anchor, angle, scale / ratio, name, number, numbering, crop frame (polygon or false), title (false or a title library part), border, pen set, color mode, update mode, layer. The source view of a drawing cannot be changed in Archicad 26 — place a new drawing and delete the old one instead. Returns [{guid, drawing} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `drawings` | object[] | yes | Drawings to change: guid + the fields to set |
| `drawings[].guid` | string | yes | Drawing element guid (get_layout_drawings) |
| `drawings[].position` | object |  | Placement point on the sheet in PAPER meters from the sheet's bottom-left corner (A3 landscape is 0.42 x 0.297, its center 0.21, 0.1485). Default: the sheet center. Sheet sizes: get_databases (sheet.width/height) |
| `drawings[].anchor` | "LeftTop" \\| "CenterTop" \\| "RightTop" \\| "LeftCenter" \\| "Center" \\| "RightCenter" \\| "LeftBottom" \\| "CenterBottom" \\| "RightBottom" |  | Which point of the drawing's bounding box sits at 'position' (default Center) |
| `drawings[].useViewOrigin` | boolean |  | Place by the view's own origin (project 0,0) instead of the bounding-box anchor |
| `drawings[].scale` | number |  | Scale denominator on paper: 100 = 1:100, 50 = 1:50. Default: the view's own scale. Use scale OR ratio |
| `drawings[].ratio` | number |  | Magnification relative to the view's own scale (1 = as the view, 2 = twice as large) |
| `drawings[].angle` | number |  | Rotation on the sheet in degrees (Archicad's drawing angle, which the API defines clockwise) |
| `drawings[].name` | string |  | Custom drawing name (sets nameType 'Custom' unless nameType is given) |
| `drawings[].nameType` | "ViewOrSourceFileName" \\| "ViewIdAndName" \\| "Custom" |  | Where the drawing name comes from: the view name, the view ID + name, or the custom 'name' |
| `drawings[].number` | string |  | Custom drawing number (sets numbering 'Custom' unless numbering is given) |
| `drawings[].numbering` | "ByLayout" \\| "ByViewId" \\| "Custom" |  | Drawing numbering: by layout sequence, by the view ID, or the custom 'number' |
| `drawings[].includeInNumbering` | boolean |  | Take part in the layout's drawing numbering sequence |
| `drawings[].includeInAutoTexts` | boolean |  | Include in drawing-scale autotexts and in drawing/layout indexes |
| `drawings[].manualUpdate` | boolean |  | true = the drawing is only refreshed by update_drawings; false = automatic update (default of the tool) |
| `drawings[].colorMode` | "OriginalColors" \\| "BlackAndWhite" \\| "GrayScale" |  | Pen color mode of the placed drawing |
| `drawings[].penTable` | string \\| integer \\| string \\| object |  | Pen set used for the drawing: 'Own' (the view's), 'Model' (the model's) or a pen set name/index |
| `drawings[].transparentBackground` | boolean |  | Transparent drawing background |
| `drawings[].border` | object |  | Border line around the drawing |
| `drawings[].border.show` | boolean |  | Draw a border (default true when 'border' is given on placement) |
| `drawings[].border.pen` | integer |  | Pen index 1-255 |
| `drawings[].border.lineType` | any |  | Line type name/index of the border |
| `drawings[].border.size` | number |  | Border line thickness in m |
| `drawings[].title` | boolean \\| string \\| integer \\| object |  | Drawing title: false = no title, or a drawing-title library part (localized name, e.g. from search_library_parts) |
| `drawings[].frame` | boolean \\| object[] \\| object |  | Crop the drawing with this polygon (PAPER meters on the sheet); false = remove the crop |
| `drawings[].layer` | any |  | Layer of the drawing element |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `delete_drawings`

Deletes drawing elements from layouts (one undo step). The source views are not touched. Returns [{guid, deleted} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `drawings` | string \\| object[] | yes | Drawing element guids (get_layout_drawings) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `update_drawings`

Refreshes placed drawings from their source views: pass drawing guids, layouts, or all: true. Archicad 26 has no direct update call, so the connector opens each affected layout (Archicad refreshes outdated auto-update drawings when a layout is shown), temporarily switching manual-update drawings to automatic (includeManual, default true). Returns per drawing statusBefore / statusAfter (UpToDate | Modified | Unknown) and counts. Publishing a set (publish_publisher_set) also updates the drawings it contains.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `drawings` | string \\| object[] |  | Drawing element guids (get_layout_drawings) |
| `layouts` | string[] |  | Update every drawing on these layouts |
| `all` | boolean |  | Update the drawings of every layout |
| `includeManual` | boolean |  | Also refresh drawings set to manual update (default true) |
| `restoreWindow` | boolean |  | Bring back the window that was in front before the export (default true) |

### `publish_publisher_set`

Runs File > Publish for one publisher set (list them with get_publisher_sets; names are localized, e.g. '2 - Макеты'). The set's own formats apply (PDF, DWG, DXF, IFC, images, BIMx ... as configured in Archicad's Publisher). outputPath overrides the set's folder. Pass 'items' (publisher item navigator guids from get_publisher_sets {name}) to publish only those. Returns {published, outputFolder, files: [{path, sizeBytes}] written during the run, durationSeconds}. Can take minutes; Archicad is busy meanwhile.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string |  | Publisher set name (exact, then case-insensitive) |
| `index` | integer |  | Alternatively the 0-based index of the set |
| `outputPath` | string |  | Absolute output FOLDER (default: the set's own path), e.g. /private/tmp/claude-connector-tests/publish |
| `createFolders` | boolean |  | Create the output folder when missing (default false) |
| `items` | string[] |  | Publish only these items of the set (navigator item guids) |

### `get_ifc_translators`

Lists the IFC export translators of the project (localized names; the first one is Archicad's default). Use a name in export_ifc.

### `export_ifc`

Saves the model as IFC (.ifc) or ifcXML (.ifcxml) with an IFC translator (default: the first/default one; see get_ifc_translators). Scope: the entire project (default), visible elements, the current story, the selection, or an explicit element list. Returns {file: {path, sizeBytes}, translator, scope, durationSeconds}. Big models can take minutes.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Absolute output path, e.g. /private/tmp/claude-connector-tests/model.ifc |
| `translator` | string |  | Translator name (exact, case-insensitive, or a unique part of it) |
| `scope` | "EntireProject" \\| "VisibleOnAllStories" \\| "AllOnCurrentStory" \\| "VisibleOnCurrentStory" \\| "Selection" \\| "Elements" |  | What to export: EntireProject (default), VisibleOnAllStories (respecting layer visibility), AllOnCurrentStory / VisibleOnCurrentStory (use storyIndex), Selection (current selection), Elements (the 'elements' list; default when elements is given) |
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Elements to export (scope 'Elements') |
| `storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Story for the *CurrentStory scopes (switches the floor plan to it) |
| `storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `format` | "ifc" \\| "ifcxml" |  | File format (default: from the path extension, else ifc) |
| `includeBoundingBoxGeometry` | boolean |  | Also write bounding-box representations (default false) |
| `overwrite` | boolean |  | Replace an existing file (default false: existing files are an error) |
| `createFolders` | boolean |  | Create missing parent folders (default false) |

### `export_dwg`

Exports a 2D window — floor plan story, section, elevation, detail, worksheet, 3D document or a whole layout (with its drawings and master layout) — as DXF, or DWG. The file is written from the primitives Archicad draws for every visible element, so it matches the view: lines, arcs, circles, polylines (with arcs), texts and fill pattern lines; layers = Archicad layers, colors = pen numbers, units mm by default. Line types and solid fills are not transferred (fills: fillBoundaries). A section/elevation window holds only its own 2D drafting — to get the model cut, place it on a layout and export the layout. format 'dwg' converts with the ODA File Converter (must be installed); for Archicad's own DWG translator publish a Publisher Set configured for DWG. Returns {file: {path, sizeBytes}, entityCount, counts, layerCount}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Absolute output path ending in .dxf or .dwg, e.g. /private/tmp/claude-connector-tests/plan.dxf |
| `format` | "dxf" \\| "dwg" |  | Output format (default: from the path extension) |
| `view` | string |  | Open this navigator view/viewpoint first (a View Map view applies its saved story, layer combination, scale, zoom), then export |
| `layout` | string |  | Export this layout (sheet) |
| `database` | string |  | Export this database (section, elevation, detail, worksheet, 3D document, layout, 'FloorPlan') |
| `storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Export the floor plan of this story (index or localized name) |
| `storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Export only these elements (they must be in the exported window's database) |
| `units` | "mm" \\| "cm" \\| "m" \\| "in" \\| "ft" |  | Drawing units of the file (default mm) |
| `fillPatterns` | boolean |  | Include the pattern lines of fills (default true) |
| `fillBoundaries` | boolean |  | Also write fill areas as closed polylines, solid triangles as SOLIDs (default false) |
| `includeMasterLayout` | boolean |  | Layouts: include the master layout content (title block) (default true) |
| `maxEntities` | integer |  | Safety limit of written entities (default 3,000,000) |
| `dwgVersion` | "ACAD2018" \\| "ACAD2013" \\| "ACAD2010" \\| "ACAD2007" \\| "ACAD2004" \\| "ACAD2000" \\| "ACAD14" \\| "ACAD12" |  | DWG version for format 'dwg' (default ACAD2018) |
| `restoreWindow` | boolean |  | Bring back the window that was in front before the export (default true) |
| `overwrite` | boolean |  | Replace an existing file (default false: existing files are an error) |
| `createFolders` | boolean |  | Create missing parent folders (default false) |

### `export_pdf`

Saves a window as PDF (Archicad's Save As PDF): a layout (sheet size from the layout), a floor plan story, section, elevation, detail, worksheet, 3D document, a View Map view, or the current front window. Batch with 'exports' (one PDF per item; common options apply to all). Page size/margins via 'paper' (meters; default: layout sheet, else A3 landscape). For many layouts in one go prefer publish_publisher_set. Returns {file: {path, sizeBytes}, exported, paper} (or results[] for a batch).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string |  | Absolute output path of a single PDF, e.g. /private/tmp/claude-connector-tests/layout.pdf |
| `view` | string |  | Open this navigator view/viewpoint first (a View Map view applies its saved story, layer combination, scale, zoom), then export |
| `layout` | string |  | Export this layout (sheet) |
| `database` | string |  | Export this database (section, elevation, detail, worksheet, 3D document, layout, 'FloorPlan') |
| `storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Export the floor plan of this story (index or localized name) |
| `storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `paper` | object |  | PDF page. Default: the layout's own sheet size for layouts, otherwise A3 landscape with 10 mm margins |
| `paper.width` | number |  | Page width in m (A4 portrait 0.21, A3 landscape 0.42) |
| `paper.height` | number |  | Page height in m (A4 portrait 0.297, A3 landscape 0.297) |
| `paper.margin` | number |  | All four margins in m |
| `paper.margins` | object |  | Individual margins in m |
| `paper.margins.left` | number |  |  |
| `paper.margins.top` | number |  |  |
| `paper.margins.right` | number |  |  |
| `paper.margins.bottom` | number |  |  |
| `exports` | object[] |  | Batch: several PDFs, each {path, view \| layout \| database \| storyIndex, paper?} |
| `exports[].path` | string | yes | Absolute output path of the PDF, e.g. /private/tmp/claude-connector-tests/plan.pdf |
| `exports[].view` | any |  |  |
| `exports[].layout` | any |  |  |
| `exports[].database` | any |  |  |
| `exports[].storyIndex` | any |  |  |
| `exports[].paper` | any |  | PDF page. Default: the layout's own sheet size for layouts, otherwise A3 landscape with 10 mm margins |
| `exports[].restoreWindow` | boolean |  | Bring back the window that was in front before the export (default true) |
| `exports[].overwrite` | boolean |  | Replace an existing file (default false: existing files are an error) |
| `exports[].createFolders` | boolean |  | Create missing parent folders (default false) |
| `restoreWindow` | any |  |  |
| `overwrite` | any |  |  |
| `createFolders` | any |  |  |

### `export_3d_model`

Exports 3D geometry: .obj (Wavefront, with a .mtl of surface colors/transparency next to it), .stl (binary by default, triangulated, for 3D printing / analysis) or .gsm (Archicad GDL object of the 3D window via Save as Object). Source: what the 3D window shows (default — set it up first with the views tools, e.g. show selected elements in 3D, 3D cutaway), every 3D element of the project (source 'AllElements'), explicit 'elements', or the current selection. Coordinates are project coordinates; OBJ defaults to Y-up (Blender/ three.js), STL to Z-up; units m by default. Returns {file, materialFile?, bodyCount, elementCount, vertexCount, faceCount|triangleCount, boundingBox (m, Z-up)}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Absolute output path ending in .obj, .stl or .gsm, e.g. /private/tmp/claude-connector-tests/model.obj |
| `format` | "obj" \\| "stl" \\| "gsm" |  | Output format (default: from the extension) |
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Export only these 3D elements (curtain walls/stairs/railings include their parts) |
| `useSelection` | boolean |  | Export the currently selected elements |
| `source` | "3DWindow" \\| "AllElements" |  | Without elements/useSelection: '3DWindow' (default: what the 3D window currently shows) or 'AllElements' (every 3D element on every story) |
| `includeSubelements` | boolean |  | With elements: include curtain wall / stair / railing / segmented beam-column parts (default true) |
| `units` | "m" \\| "mm" \\| "cm" \\| "in" \\| "ft" |  | File units (default m) |
| `upAxis` | "Y" \\| "Z" |  | Up axis in the file (default Y for obj, Z for stl) |
| `materials` | boolean |  | obj: write the .mtl material file (default true) |
| `binary` | boolean |  | stl: binary (default true) or ASCII |
| `includeInvisible` | boolean |  | Also export polygons Archicad marks invisible (default false) |
| `gdlMode` | "Binary" \\| "Text" |  | gsm: binary 3D data (default) or editable GDL text |
| `placeable` | boolean |  | gsm: make the object placeable (default true) |
| `restoreWindow` | boolean |  | Bring back the window that was in front before the export (default true) |
| `overwrite` | boolean |  | Replace an existing file (default false: existing files are an error) |
| `createFolders` | boolean |  | Create missing parent folders (default false) |

### `export_module`

Saves elements (or the current selection) as an Archicad module file (.mod) — the file format for hotlinked modules (place_hotlink) and for merging into other projects (merge_file). All elements must be in one database (floor plan, or one section/detail/worksheet). Returns {file: {path, sizeBytes}, elementCount}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Absolute output path ending in .mod, e.g. /private/tmp/claude-connector-tests/core.mod |
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Elements to save |
| `useSelection` | boolean |  | Save the current selection instead |
| `overwrite` | boolean |  | Replace an existing file (default false: existing files are an error) |
| `createFolders` | boolean |  | Create missing parent folders (default false) |

### `merge_file`

Merges another Archicad project (.pln), archive (.pla) or module (.mod) into this project as own, editable elements (Archicad 26 has no merge API: the file is placed as a hotlinked module and the hotlink is broken). Optional placement (position m, angle deg, mirrored, home story) and story range. keepHotlink: true leaves it as a live hotlink instead. IFC / DWG / DXF cannot be merged through the API (use Archicad's File > Interoperability > Merge). Refuses files that are already hotlinked (breaking would convert those too). Returns {merged, elementCount, countsByType, elements: [guid]}. Undo: two steps (place + break).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | string | yes | Absolute path of an Archicad project (.pln), archive (.pla) or module (.mod, see export_module) file |
| `name` | string |  | Name of the temporary hotlink (default 'Merge - <file name>') |
| `keepHotlink` | boolean |  | Keep it as a hotlinked module instead of breaking it into own elements (default false) |
| `maxGuids` | integer |  | Return at most this many new element guids (default 500; counts are always complete) |
| `storyRange` | "AllStories" \\| "SingleStory" |  | Hotlink all stories of the source (default) or a single one (then give sourceStory) |
| `sourceStory` | integer |  | Story index IN THE SOURCE FILE for a single-story hotlink (implies storyRange 'SingleStory') |
| `position` | object |  | Where the source file's origin (0,0,0) lands in this project, meters (default 0,0,0; z = vertical offset) |
| `angle` | number |  | Rotation around the insertion point in degrees, counter-clockwise (default 0) |
| `mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror the module (about its local Y axis) before rotating |
| `storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story of the placed module in this project (default: the current story) |
| `storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `floorDifference` | integer |  | Advanced: story offset between the source's and this project's stories (default 0 / tool default) |
| `skipNested` | boolean |  | Skip modules nested inside the source file |
| `ignoreTopFloorLinks` | boolean |  | Ignore top links of walls/columns on the top story of the module |
| `relinkWallOpenings` | boolean |  | Re-link wall openings of the module |
| `adjustLevelDiffs` | boolean |  | Adjust elevations of elements with relative level linking |
| `suspendFixAngle` | boolean |  | Adjust fixed-angle elements (e.g. texts) to the module rotation |
| `layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer of the hotlink instance |
| `layer.index` | integer | yes |  |

### `get_hotlinks`

Lists hotlink nodes — hotlinked modules (.pln/.pla/.mod) and XRefs (DWG/DXF) — as a tree: nodeGuid, name, type, source path, sourceStatus (Available | Missing | NotAccessible | ...), story range, last update, nested children, and the placed instances (guid, home story, position {x,y,z} m, angle deg, mirrored, options, optional elementCount). Use nodeGuid with place_hotlink {node}, update_hotlinks and delete_hotlinks.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `types` | "Module" \\| "XRef"[] |  | Only these hotlink types (default both) |
| `includeInstances` | boolean |  | List the placed instances of each node (default true) |
| `includeElementCounts` | boolean |  | Count the elements inside each instance (default false; slower) |

### `place_hotlink`

Places hotlinked modules (one undo step): from a file ('source': .pln / .pla / .mod — a hotlink node is created, or an existing node with the same file and story settings is reused) or another instance of an existing node ('node' from get_hotlinks). Position/angle/mirror set where the source's origin lands; storyIndex is the home story. The module stays linked: refresh it with update_hotlinks, break it into own elements with delete_hotlinks {keepElements: true} (or use merge_file). Returns [{guid, nodeGuid, nodeCreated, instance, node} | {error}]. XRef (DWG) attachment is not available through the Archicad 26 API.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hotlinks` | object[] | yes | Hotlink instances to place |
| `hotlinks[].source` | string |  | Absolute path of an Archicad project (.pln), archive (.pla) or module (.mod, see export_module) file |
| `hotlinks[].node` | string |  | Existing hotlink node guid (get_hotlinks) — places another instance of it |
| `hotlinks[].name` | string |  | Name of a newly created hotlink node (default: the file name) |
| `hotlinks[].reuseNode` | boolean |  | Reuse an existing node of the same source file and settings (default true) |
| `hotlinks[].storyRange` | "AllStories" \\| "SingleStory" |  | Hotlink all stories of the source (default) or a single one (then give sourceStory) |
| `hotlinks[].sourceStory` | integer |  | Story index IN THE SOURCE FILE for a single-story hotlink (implies storyRange 'SingleStory') |
| `hotlinks[].position` | object |  | Where the source file's origin (0,0,0) lands in this project, meters (default 0,0,0; z = vertical offset) |
| `hotlinks[].angle` | number |  | Rotation around the insertion point in degrees, counter-clockwise (default 0) |
| `hotlinks[].mirrored` | boolean |  | Mirror the module (about its local Y axis) before rotating |
| `hotlinks[].storyIndex` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Home story of the placed module in this project (default: the current story) |
| `hotlinks[].storyIndex.name` | string | yes |  |
| `hotlinks[].floorDifference` | integer |  | Advanced: story offset between the source's and this project's stories (default 0 / tool default) |
| `hotlinks[].skipNested` | boolean |  | Skip modules nested inside the source file |
| `hotlinks[].ignoreTopFloorLinks` | boolean |  | Ignore top links of walls/columns on the top story of the module |
| `hotlinks[].relinkWallOpenings` | boolean |  | Re-link wall openings of the module |
| `hotlinks[].adjustLevelDiffs` | boolean |  | Adjust elevations of elements with relative level linking |
| `hotlinks[].suspendFixAngle` | boolean |  | Adjust fixed-angle elements (e.g. texts) to the module rotation |
| `hotlinks[].layer` | integer \\| string \\| object |  | Layer of the hotlink instance |
| `hotlinks[].layer.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step |

### `update_hotlinks`

Refreshes hotlinked modules / XRefs from their source files ('nodes' or all: true — like Hotlink Manager > Update) and/or relinks nodes to another file or changes their name / story range ('relink': [{node, source?, name?, storyRange?, sourceStory?}], relinked nodes are refreshed too). Missing sources are reported with a relink hint. In Teamwork reserve the hotlink cache first (reserve_elements {objectSets: ['HotlinkCacheManagement']}). Returns per node {nodeGuid, updated|relinked, node} | {error}, updatedCount.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `nodes` | string[] |  | Hotlink node guids to refresh (get_hotlinks; an instance guid is accepted too) |
| `all` | boolean |  | Refresh every top-level hotlink |
| `relink` | object[] |  | Nodes to relink / reconfigure (one undo step), then refreshed |
| `relink[].node` | string | yes | Hotlink node guid (or an instance guid) |
| `relink[].source` | string |  | New source file (absolute .pln / .pla / .mod path) |
| `relink[].name` | string |  | New node name |
| `relink[].storyRange` | "AllStories" \\| "SingleStory" |  | Hotlink all stories of the source (default) or a single one (then give sourceStory) |
| `relink[].sourceStory` | integer |  | Story index IN THE SOURCE FILE for a single-story hotlink (implies storyRange 'SingleStory') |

### `delete_hotlinks`

Removes hotlink nodes with ALL their placed instances (one undo step). keepElements: true breaks the link instead: the elements stay in the project as own, editable elements. Nested hotlinks go with their parent. Returns [{nodeGuid, name, deleted|broken, instanceCount} | {error}].

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `nodes` | string[] | yes | Hotlink node guids (get_hotlinks; an instance guid selects its node) |
| `keepElements` | boolean |  | true = break (keep the elements), false = delete them with the hotlink (default false) |


## properties

### `get_property_definitions`

Lists property definitions — built-in (ID, areas, volumes, heights, ...) and user-defined (custom) — so you can address them in get_property_values / set_property_values. Summary per definition: {guid, name, group, type (string|integer|number|length|area|volume|angle|boolean|<type>List|singleEnum|multiEnum), kind (Custom|BuiltIn|DynamicBuiltIn), editable, description?, expressionBased?, enumValues? (options of option sets), availabilityCount? (custom), builtInName? (language-independent name of built-ins, e.g. General_ElementID)}. detail 'full' adds groupGuid, collectionType/valueType/measureType, defaultValue or defaultExpressions, enumOptions with keys, expressionReference (for expressions) and availability (classification items). Names are LOCALIZED (Russian Archicad: Russian group and property names) — use search with a Russian word, or builtInName for built-ins. Filter with search / groups / kind, or with elements / elementTypes to get only what is available for them. Returns {definitions, total, offset, returned, hasMore, groups?}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `search` | string |  | Case-insensitive substring of 'Group/Name' or the description, e.g. 'огнест' or 'Площадь' |
| `groups` | string \\| object[] |  | Only definitions in these groups |
| `properties` | string \\| object[] |  | Only these definitions (resolve references to full details) |
| `kind` | "All" \\| "Custom" \\| "BuiltIn" |  | All (default), Custom (user-defined) or BuiltIn |
| `elements` | any \\| object[] |  | Only definitions available for these elements (see elementMatch) |
| `elementTypes` | string[] |  | Only definitions available for the tool defaults of these element types |
| `elementMatch` | "all" \\| "any" |  | With elements/elementTypes: available for all of them (default) or any |
| `detail` | "summary" \\| "full" |  | summary (default) or full |
| `includeAvailability` | boolean |  | Resolve availability to classification items {guid, id, name?, system} (default: true with detail full) |
| `includeGroups` | boolean |  | Also return all property groups {guid, name, kind, description?, definitionCount} |
| `includeBuiltInNames` | boolean |  | Add builtInName to built-in definitions (default true; 2 extra official API calls, cached) |
| `offset` | integer |  | Skip this many definitions |
| `limit` | integer |  | Return at most this many (default 500) |

### `create_property_groups`

Creates user-defined property groups (the folders of the Property Manager) in one undo step. An existing custom group with the same name is returned with alreadyExisted: true (idempotent). create_property_definitions also creates missing groups itself. Returns {results: [{guid, name, alreadyExisted?} | {error}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `groups` | object[] | yes |  |
| `groups[].name` | string | yes | Group name |
| `groups[].description` | string |  |  |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `modify_property_groups`

Renames user-defined property groups and/or changes their description (built-in groups are read-only). One undo step.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `groups` | object[] | yes |  |
| `groups[].group` | string \\| object | yes | Group to change (current name or GUID) |
| `groups[].group.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `groups[].name` | string |  | New name |
| `groups[].description` | string |  | New description |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `delete_property_groups`

Deletes user-defined property groups in one undo step (undoable with undo). A group that still contains definitions is refused unless deleteDefinitions is true — then its definitions and all their element values are deleted too. Built-in groups cannot be deleted.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `groups` | string \\| object[] | yes |  |
| `deleteDefinitions` | boolean |  | Also delete the definitions inside (default false = refuse non-empty groups) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `create_property_definitions`

Creates user-defined (custom) properties in one undo step — like the Property Manager: any value type, option sets (singleEnum/multiEnum with enumValues), default values or expression-based defaults, and availability per classification. IMPORTANT: custom properties appear only on elements whose classification is in the availability — the default 'all' makes it available for every classification item that exists now (elements with no classification never show custom properties). Missing groups are created (createMissingGroups, default true). Then set values with set_property_values. Returns {results: [{guid, name, group, type, availabilityCount, warnings?} | {error}]} in input order.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `definitions` | object[] | yes |  |
| `definitions[].group` | string \\| object | yes | Custom group (name or GUID); a new name creates the group |
| `definitions[].group.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `definitions[].name` | string | yes | Property name (unique within the group) |
| `definitions[].type` | "string" \\| "integer" \\| "number" \\| "length" \\| "area" \\| "volume" \\| "angle" \\| "boolean" \\| "stringList" \\| "integerList" \\| "numberList" \\| "lengthList" \\| "areaList" \\| "volumeList" \\| "angleList" \\| "booleanList" \\| "singleEnum" \\| "multiEnum" | yes | Value type: string \| integer \| number (plain real) \| length (m) \| area (m²) \| volume (m³) \| angle (degrees) \| boolean; <type>List = list of values (e.g. stringList); singleEnum = option set with one choice; multiEnum = option set with multiple choices (options in enumValues). The type cannot be changed later. |
| `definitions[].description` | string |  | Description shown in the Property Manager / tooltips |
| `definitions[].enumValues` | string \\| number \\| object[] |  | Options — REQUIRED for singleEnum/multiEnum, not allowed otherwise |
| `definitions[].enumValueType` | "string" \\| "integer" \\| "number" \\| "length" \\| "area" \\| "volume" \\| "angle" \\| "boolean" |  | Value type of the options of a singleEnum/multiEnum (default string; Archicad's UI option sets are strings) |
| `definitions[].defaultValue` | string\|number\|boolean \\| any[] |  | Default value shown on every element that has no own value. Units: lengths m, areas m², volumes m³, angles DEGREES; option sets by display value. Default when omitted on create: Undefined. |
| `definitions[].defaultExpressions` | string[] |  | Makes the property EXPRESSION-BASED (calculated, read-only on elements): Archicad property expressions (operators + - * / and functions such as CONCAT, ROUND, IF, ...). Reference other properties with placeholders that are converted to Archicad's reference syntax: {builtIn:General_Area} (built-ins, language independent), {ref:Group/Name} or {ref:GUID} (any property), e.g. ["{builtIn:General_Area} * 1.1"] or ["CONCAT ( {ref:CC-Group/Code}, \"-\", {builtIn:General_ElementID} )"]. Archicad's own syntax (expressionReference from get_property_definitions {detail: 'full'}) also works. The property type must match the expression result (area expression -> type area). Several expressions = alternatives evaluated in order (first applicable wins). |
| `definitions[].availability` | "all" \\| "none" \\| string \\| object[] |  | Classifications the property is available for: 'all' (default when neither availability, addAvailability nor availableForElements is given), 'none', or item refs / {system} |
| `definitions[].addAvailability` | any[] |  | Add these classification items (or {system} = all its items) to the availability |
| `definitions[].availableForElements` | any \\| object[] |  | Make the property available for these elements: adds their current classification items to the availability (elements without classification produce a warning — classify them first) |
| `createMissingGroups` | boolean |  | Create groups that do not exist yet (default true) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `modify_property_definitions`

Changes user-defined properties in one undo step: rename, move to another group, description, option set options (replace the list — unchanged options keep their identity so element values survive — or add/remove/rename options), default value or expressions, and availability (replace, add, remove, or add the classifications of given elements). Built-in properties and the value type cannot be changed (delete and recreate instead). Returns {results: [{guid, name, group, type, availabilityCount, warnings?} | {error}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `definitions` | object[] | yes |  |
| `definitions[].property` | string \\| object | yes | User-defined property to change |
| `definitions[].property.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `definitions[].name` | string |  | New name |
| `definitions[].description` | string |  | New description |
| `definitions[].group` | string \\| object |  | Move into this custom group |
| `definitions[].group.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `definitions[].enumValues` | string \\| number \\| object[] |  | Replace the options (options with the same display value keep their key) |
| `definitions[].addEnumValues` | any[] |  | Append options |
| `definitions[].removeEnumValues` | string\|number\|boolean[] |  | Remove options (by display value) |
| `definitions[].renameEnumValues` | object[] |  | Rename options, keeping element values: [{from: 'old', to: 'new'}] |
| `definitions[].renameEnumValues[].from` | string\|number | yes |  |
| `definitions[].renameEnumValues[].to` | string\|number | yes |  |
| `definitions[].defaultValue` | string\|number\|boolean \\| any[] |  | Default value shown on every element that has no own value. Units: lengths m, areas m², volumes m³, angles DEGREES; option sets by display value. Default when omitted on create: Undefined. |
| `definitions[].defaultExpressions` | string[] |  | Makes the property EXPRESSION-BASED (calculated, read-only on elements): Archicad property expressions (operators + - * / and functions such as CONCAT, ROUND, IF, ...). Reference other properties with placeholders that are converted to Archicad's reference syntax: {builtIn:General_Area} (built-ins, language independent), {ref:Group/Name} or {ref:GUID} (any property), e.g. ["{builtIn:General_Area} * 1.1"] or ["CONCAT ( {ref:CC-Group/Code}, \"-\", {builtIn:General_ElementID} )"]. Archicad's own syntax (expressionReference from get_property_definitions {detail: 'full'}) also works. The property type must match the expression result (area expression -> type area). Several expressions = alternatives evaluated in order (first applicable wins). |
| `definitions[].defaultUndefined` | boolean |  | true = reset the default to Undefined (and remove expressions) |
| `definitions[].availability` | "all" \\| "none" \\| string \\| object[] |  | Replace the availability: 'all', 'none' or item refs / {system} |
| `definitions[].removeAvailability` | string \\| any[] |  | Remove these items from the availability ('all' = clear) |
| `definitions[].addAvailability` | any[] |  | Add these classification items (or {system} = all its items) to the availability |
| `definitions[].availableForElements` | any \\| object[] |  | Make the property available for these elements: adds their current classification items to the availability (elements without classification produce a warning — classify them first) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `delete_property_definitions`

Deletes user-defined property definitions in one undo step — their values on ALL elements are lost (undo restores them). Built-in properties cannot be deleted. Returns {results: [{guid, name, group, type, deleted} | {error}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `properties` | string \\| object[] | yes |  |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `import_property_definitions_xml`

Imports property groups and definitions from an Archicad property XML file (the Property Manager's Export format, <BuildingInformation><PropertyDefinitionGroups>...). Give the XML text or a local file path. One undo step. Returns {created: [{guid, name, group, type}], groupsCreated, definitionCount}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `xml` | string |  | XML content |
| `filePath` | string |  | Absolute path of an .xml file on this computer (alternative to xml) |
| `conflictPolicy` | "Skip" \\| "Replace" \\| "Append" |  | When a definition with the same name exists: Skip (default, keep existing), Replace (overwrite), Append (add alongside) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `get_property_values`

Reads property values (built-in and user-defined) of many elements — and/or of element TOOL DEFAULTS — as one table: {properties: [{guid, name, group, type}], results: [{guid | elementType, values: [cell per property, same order]} | {guid, error}]}. Each cell is {value, display?, isDefault?} (display = Archicad's formatted text incl. units, only when it differs from value; isDefault only for user-defined properties: true = the element has no own value and shows the default/expression) or {status: 'NotAvailable' (property not available for this element/classification) | 'NotEvaluated' | 'Undefined' (value set to Undefined) | 'Empty'}. Units: lengths m, areas m², volumes m³, angles DEGREES; option sets by display value. Without properties: every property available for the targets (scope UserDefined by default; BuiltIn/All can be hundreds). Address built-ins language-independently with {builtIn: 'General_ElementID'} etc.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Elements to read |
| `elementDefaults` | string[] |  | Read the tool default settings of these element types |
| `properties` | string \\| object[] |  | Properties to read (columns). Omit = all available (see scope) |
| `scope` | "UserDefined" \\| "BuiltIn" \\| "All" |  | Used only without properties: which properties to return (default UserDefined) |
| `includeDisplay` | boolean |  | Add Archicad's formatted display text (default true) |

### `set_property_values`

Sets property values on many elements (and/or element tool defaults) in ONE undo step. Each entry sets one property on a list of elements: {elements, property, value} — or reset: true (back to the default/expression) or setUndefined: true. Works for user-defined properties and editable built-ins (e.g. {builtIn: 'General_ElementID'} = Element ID). Units: lengths m, areas m², volumes m³, angles DEGREES; option sets by display value. Fails per element (reported, others continue) when the property is not available for the element's classification (fix: modify_property_definitions {availableForElements}) or read-only (calculated / expression-based). Returns {results: [{property, guid, succeeded, failed?: [{guid|elementType, error}]} | {error}], succeeded, failed}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `values` | object[] | yes |  |
| `values[].elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Elements to change |
| `values[].elementDefaults` | string[] |  | Also/instead change the tool defaults of these element types |
| `values[].property` | string \\| object | yes | Property: definition GUID, localized "Group/Name" (e.g. "ИНФОРМАЦИЯ О ПРОДУКТЕ/Модель"), a unique name alone, {guid}, {group?, name}, or {builtIn: 'General_ElementID'} (built-in properties, language independent — preferred for built-ins). Matching is exact, then case-insensitive; ambiguous names fail with the candidates. Find names with get_property_definitions {search}. |
| `values[].property.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `values[].value` | string\|number\|boolean \\| any[] |  | New value. Units: lengths m, areas m², volumes m³, angles DEGREES; option sets by display value. |
| `values[].reset` | boolean |  | true = remove the element's own value so it shows the default / expression again (user-defined properties) |
| `values[].setUndefined` | boolean |  | true = set the value to Undefined (empty) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `get_attribute_property_values`

Reads property values of ATTRIBUTES — mainly building materials (properties/classifications of building materials, e.g. thermal or product data) — as a table like get_property_values: {properties: [...], results: [{attribute: {type, index, name, guid}, values: [...]}]}. Each cell is {value, display?, isDefault?} (display = Archicad's formatted text incl. units, only when it differs from value; isDefault only for user-defined properties: true = the element has no own value and shows the default/expression) or {status: 'NotAvailable' (property not available for this element/classification) | 'NotEvaluated' | 'Undefined' (value set to Undefined) | 'Empty'}. Without properties: all available ones (scope UserDefined default).

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `attributes` | object[] | yes | Attributes, e.g. [{type: 'BuildingMaterial', attribute: 'Бетон - Конструкционный'}] |
| `attributes[].type` | string |  | Attribute type: BuildingMaterial (default), Composite, Surface, Profile, Layer, ... (properties are mainly used on building materials) |
| `attributes[].attribute` | integer \\| string \\| object | yes | Attribute by name (exact, localized — list with get_attributes), index, or {guid} |
| `attributes[].attribute.index` | integer | yes |  |
| `properties` | string \\| object[] |  |  |
| `scope` | "UserDefined" \\| "BuiltIn" \\| "All" |  | Used only without properties (default UserDefined) |
| `includeDisplay` | boolean |  | Add formatted display text (default true) |

### `set_attribute_property_values`

Sets property values of attributes (building materials, ...) in one undo step. Each entry: {attributes: [{type, attribute}], property, value | reset: true | setUndefined: true}. The property must be available for the attribute's classification. Units: lengths m, areas m², volumes m³, angles DEGREES; option sets by display value.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `values` | object[] | yes |  |
| `values[].attributes` | object[] | yes |  |
| `values[].attributes[].type` | string |  | Attribute type: BuildingMaterial (default), Composite, Surface, Profile, Layer, ... (properties are mainly used on building materials) |
| `values[].attributes[].attribute` | integer \\| string \\| object | yes | Attribute by name (exact, localized — list with get_attributes), index, or {guid} |
| `values[].property` | string \\| object | yes | Property: definition GUID, localized "Group/Name" (e.g. "ИНФОРМАЦИЯ О ПРОДУКТЕ/Модель"), a unique name alone, {guid}, {group?, name}, or {builtIn: 'General_ElementID'} (built-in properties, language independent — preferred for built-ins). Matching is exact, then case-insensitive; ambiguous names fail with the candidates. Find names with get_property_definitions {search}. |
| `values[].property.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `values[].value` | string\|number\|boolean \\| any[] |  | New value. Units: lengths m, areas m², volumes m³, angles DEGREES; option sets by display value. |
| `values[].reset` | boolean |  | true = remove the element's own value so it shows the default / expression again (user-defined properties) |
| `values[].setUndefined` | boolean |  | true = set the value to Undefined (empty) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `get_ifc_data`

Returns IFC data of elements: ifcGlobalId (22-char IFC GUID used on export), archicadIfcId, externalIfcGlobalId (elements imported from IFC), ifcType (e.g. IfcWall) and typeObjectIfcType, IFC properties grouped by property set ({propertySet, name, type Single|List|Bounded|Enumerated|Table, value/values/lower/upper/options, valueType, readOnly?}), IFC attributes (Name, Description, ObjectType, Tag, ...) and IFC classification references. Can also FIND elements by IFC GlobalId (ifcGlobalIds). Returns {elements: [{guid, ...} | {guid, error}], lookup?: [{ifcGlobalId, elements}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  |  |
| `ifcGlobalIds` | string[] |  | Find elements by IFC GlobalId (22 characters) and include them |
| `include` | "identity" \\| "type" \\| "properties" \\| "attributes" \\| "classificationReferences"[] |  | Parts to return (default all) |
| `propertySets` | string[] |  | Only IFC properties of these property sets, e.g. ['Pset_WallCommon'] |
| `storedOnly` | boolean |  | true = only data stored on the element; false (default) = also properties/attributes the IFC translator would export |

### `set_ifc_properties`

Adds or changes IFC properties stored on elements (custom psets such as 'CC_Pset' or standard ones like Pset_WallCommon), removes stored IFC properties, sets IFC attributes (Name, Description, ObjectType, Tag, LongName, ...) and adds/removes stored IFC classification references (IfcClassificationReference) — one undo step. Property kinds: Single {value}, List {values}, Bounded {lower?, upper?}, Enumerated {values (selected), options}, Table {definingValues, definedValues}. Values are written as given (no unit conversion). Returns {properties?, attributes?, classificationReferences?} with per-entry {succeeded, failed?: [{guid, error}]} | {error}. Read back with get_ifc_data {storedOnly: true}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `properties` | object[] |  |  |
| `properties[].elements` | string \\| object[] | yes |  |
| `properties[].propertySet` | string | yes | Property set name, e.g. 'Pset_WallCommon' or a custom pset |
| `properties[].name` | string | yes | IFC property name, e.g. 'FireRating' |
| `properties[].remove` | boolean |  | true = remove this stored IFC property from the elements (no values needed) |
| `properties[].type` | "Single" \\| "List" \\| "Bounded" \\| "Enumerated" \\| "Table" |  | Default: Single (List when values, Bounded when lower/upper, Enumerated when options) |
| `properties[].valueType` | string |  | IFC value type: IfcLabel, IfcText, IfcIdentifier, IfcBoolean, IfcLogical, IfcInteger, IfcReal, IfcCountMeasure, IfcLengthMeasure, IfcPositiveLengthMeasure, IfcAreaMeasure, IfcVolumeMeasure, IfcPlaneAngleMeasure, IfcMassMeasure, IfcThermalTransmittanceMeasure, ... (default inferred: string→IfcLabel, integer→IfcInteger, number→IfcReal, boolean→IfcBoolean) |
| `properties[].value` | string\|number\|boolean \\| object |  | Single value |
| `properties[].value.value` | any | yes |  |
| `properties[].value.valueType` | string |  | IFC type, e.g. IfcLabel |
| `properties[].values` | any[] |  | List values, or the selected values of an Enumerated property |
| `properties[].lower` | any |  | Bounded: lower bound |
| `properties[].upper` | any |  | Bounded: upper bound |
| `properties[].options` | any[] |  | Enumerated: the allowed values (enumeration reference) |
| `properties[].definingValues` | any[] |  | Table: defining values |
| `properties[].definedValues` | any[] |  | Table: defined values (same length) |
| `properties[].description` | string |  |  |
| `attributes` | object[] |  |  |
| `attributes[].elements` | any | yes |  |
| `attributes[].name` | string | yes | IFC attribute name: Name, Description, ObjectType, Tag, LongName, PredefinedType, ... |
| `attributes[].value` | string\|number\|boolean |  | New value (stored as text) |
| `attributes[].clear` | boolean |  | true = remove the value |
| `classificationReferences` | object[] |  | IFC classification references STORED on elements (exported as IfcRelAssociatesClassification). References derived from Archicad classifications are read-only here — classify elements with set_element_classifications instead. |
| `classificationReferences[].elements` | any | yes |  |
| `classificationReferences[].referenceName` | string |  | Key of the reference on the element (default: identification, else name); used to update/remove it |
| `classificationReferences[].identification` | string |  | Item code in the classification, e.g. 'Ss_25_10_30' (IFC Identification/ItemReference) |
| `classificationReferences[].name` | string |  | Item name, e.g. 'Brick walling systems' |
| `classificationReferences[].location` | string |  | URL of the item |
| `classificationReferences[].source` | object |  | The classification system (IfcClassification) — required unless remove |
| `classificationReferences[].source.name` | string | yes | Classification system name, e.g. 'Uniclass' |
| `classificationReferences[].source.source` | string |  | Publisher / source |
| `classificationReferences[].source.edition` | string |  | Edition, e.g. '2015' |
| `classificationReferences[].source.editionDate` | string |  | Edition date text, e.g. '2015-01-01' |
| `classificationReferences[].source.description` | string |  |  |
| `classificationReferences[].source.location` | string |  | URL of the classification system |
| `classificationReferences[].remove` | boolean |  | true = remove the stored reference with this referenceName/identification (source.name narrows the match) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `create_classification_system`

Creates a classification system (like the Classification Manager's 'New'), optionally with its whole item tree, in one undo step. name + editionVersion must be unique. Items: [{id, name?, description?, children?: [...]}] (nested) — or flat with parent: '<id of an item earlier in the list>'. Then classify elements with the official classification tools and use the items in property availability. Returns {system: {guid, name, editionVersion, ...}, items: [{guid, id, path, parent?} | {id, error}], created, failed}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | string | yes | System name, e.g. 'CC-test-System' or 'Uniclass 2015' |
| `editionVersion` | string |  | Edition/version (default '1.0') |
| `description` | string |  |  |
| `source` | string |  | Source, e.g. a URL |
| `editionDate` | string |  | Edition date YYYY-MM-DD (default today) |
| `items` | object[] |  | Items to create (tree) |
| `items[].id` | string | yes | Item ID (unique within the system, shown in the Classification Manager), e.g. 'CC-01' |
| `items[].name` | string |  | Item name |
| `items[].description` | string |  |  |
| `items[].parent` | string \\| object |  | Parent item (GUID or ID in this system, may be an item created earlier in the same call); default: the enclosing item or the root |
| `items[].parent.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `items[].before` | any |  | Insert before this sibling item (default: append at the end) |
| `items[].children` | object[] |  | Child items (same shape, recursively) |
| `items[].children[].id` | any | yes |  |
| `items[].children[].name` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].description` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].parent` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].before` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].children` | object[] |  | Child items (same shape, recursively) |
| `reuseExisting` | boolean |  | If the system already exists, add the items to it instead of failing (default false) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `create_classification_items`

Adds classification items to an existing classification system in one undo step: nested {id, name?, description?, children?} trees, under an optional parent, inserted at the end or before a sibling. Item IDs must be unique within the system. Returns {system, items: [{guid, id, path, parent?} | {id, error}], created, failed}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `system` | string \\| object | yes | Classification system: name (e.g. 'Классификация Archicad'), 'Name version', GUID, or {name, editionVersion} |
| `system.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `parent` | string \\| object |  | Create the items under this item (default: at the root) |
| `parent.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `items` | object[] | yes |  |
| `items[].id` | string | yes | Item ID (unique within the system, shown in the Classification Manager), e.g. 'CC-01' |
| `items[].name` | string |  | Item name |
| `items[].description` | string |  |  |
| `items[].parent` | any |  | Parent item (GUID or ID in this system, may be an item created earlier in the same call); default: the enclosing item or the root |
| `items[].before` | any |  | Insert before this sibling item (default: append at the end) |
| `items[].children` | object[] |  | Child items (same shape, recursively) |
| `items[].children[].id` | any | yes |  |
| `items[].children[].name` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].description` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].parent` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].before` | any |  |  |
| `items[].children[].children` | object[] |  | Child items (same shape, recursively) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `modify_classification_items`

Changes the ID, name and/or description of classification items in one undo step. Returns {results: [{guid, id, name?, description?} | {error}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `system` | string \\| object |  | System in which item IDs are looked up (needed when an ID exists in several systems) |
| `system.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `items` | object[] | yes |  |
| `items[].item` | string \\| object | yes | Item to change |
| `items[].item.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `items[].id` | string |  | New ID |
| `items[].name` | string |  | New name |
| `items[].description` | string |  | New description |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `delete_classification_items`

Deletes classification items together with all their children in one undo step (elements classified with them become unclassified in that system). Returns {results: [{guid, id, deleted, deletedChildren} | {error}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `system` | string \\| object |  | System in which item IDs are looked up |
| `system.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `items` | string \\| object[] | yes |  |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `modify_classification_system`

Changes a classification system's name, editionVersion, description, source or editionDate. Returns {system}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `system` | string \\| object | yes | Classification system: name (e.g. 'Классификация Archicad'), 'Name version', GUID, or {name, editionVersion} |
| `system.guid` | string | yes | Element/attribute GUID |
| `name` | string |  |  |
| `editionVersion` | string |  |  |
| `description` | string |  |  |
| `source` | string |  |  |
| `editionDate` | string |  | Edition date YYYY-MM-DD |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `delete_classification_systems`

Deletes whole classification systems with all their items in one undo step (element classifications in them are removed). Returns {results: [{guid, name, deleted} | {error}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `systems` | string \\| object[] | yes |  |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |

### `import_classifications_xml`

Imports classification systems from an Archicad classification XML (Classification Manager export / downloaded systems such as Uniclass, OmniClass). Give the XML text or a local file path. One undo step. Returns {systems: [{guid, name, editionVersion, new, itemCount, ...}]}.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `xml` | string |  | XML content |
| `filePath` | string |  | Absolute path of the .xml file on this computer (alternative to xml) |
| `systemConflictPolicy` | "Merge" \\| "Replace" \\| "Skip" |  | When a system with the same name/version exists (default Merge) |
| `itemConflictPolicy` | "Replace" \\| "Skip" |  | When an item ID exists (default Replace) |
| `undoName` | string |  | Name of the undo step shown in Archicad |


## collaboration

### `get_teamwork_status`

Teamwork (BIMcloud/BIMserver) status of the open project — call this before reserve_elements / release_elements / teamwork_send / teamwork_receive. Solo project: {isTeamwork: false, message, project: {name, path, untitled}} — then nothing needs to be reserved and the teamwork tools do nothing. Teamwork project: {isTeamwork: true, project, teamwork: {hasConnection, online, serverUrl, teamProjectName, loginName, teamProjectLocation}, currentUser: {userId, name}, members?: [{userId, loginName, fullName, connected}], hotlinkCacheManagementReservedBy?, warning? (server offline), elements?: [{guid, status: Free|ReservedByMe|ReservedByOther|ServerUnavailable|NotExist, reservedBy?: [user names]} | {error}], objectSets?: [{name, status, reservedBy?, canCreate, canDeleteModify}], accessRights?: {RightName: bool}}. Only elements with status ReservedByMe (or Free after reserving) can be modified.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Also return the reservation status of these elements |
| `objectSets` | boolean \\| "LayerSettings" \\| "LineTypes" \\| "FillTypes" \\| "Composites" \\| "PenSets" \\| "Surfaces" \\| "BuildingMaterials" \\| "ZoneCategories" \\| "Profiles" \\| "MEPSystems" \\| "OperationProfiles" \\| "ModelViewOptions" \\| "Favorites" \\| "IssueTags" \\| "ClassificationsAndProperties" \\| "ProjectInfo" \\| "ProjectPreferences" \\| "ProjectLibraryList"[] |  | Also return the reservation status of object sets: true = all of them, or a list of names |
| `includeMembers` | boolean |  | List the team members with their online state (default true) |
| `includeAccessRights` | boolean |  | Return the current user's Teamwork role rights as {RightName: bool} (e.g. LayersCreate, IssuesCreateModify). Default false |

### `teamwork_send`

Teamwork 'Send': uploads your changes to the BIMcloud/BIMserver so team members can receive them (reserved elements stay reserved). Output: {isTeamwork: true, sent: true}. In a solo (non-Teamwork) project this does nothing and returns {isTeamwork: false, message} — that is not an error: everything is editable without reservation.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `comment` | string |  | Comment stored with the sent changes (shown in the project history) |

### `teamwork_receive`

Teamwork 'Receive': downloads the changes that other team members have sent, updating the local model. Output: {isTeamwork: true, received: true}. In a solo (non-Teamwork) project this does nothing and returns {isTeamwork: false, message} — that is not an error: everything is editable without reservation.

### `reserve_elements`

Teamwork: reserves elements and/or object sets so you can edit them (elements reserved by someone else cannot be taken; the result names the owner). Output: {isTeamwork: true, elements?: [{guid, status, reserved, conflictWith?, reservedBy?} | {error}], objectSets?: [{name, status, reserved, apiError?, conflictWith?}], warning?}. Check get_teamwork_status first. In a solo (non-Teamwork) project this does nothing and returns {isTeamwork: false, message} — that is not an error: everything is editable without reservation.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Elements to reserve/release (GUIDs) |
| `objectSets` | "LayerSettings" \\| "LineTypes" \\| "FillTypes" \\| "Composites" \\| "PenSets" \\| "Surfaces" \\| "BuildingMaterials" \\| "ZoneCategories" \\| "Profiles" \\| "MEPSystems" \\| "OperationProfiles" \\| "ModelViewOptions" \\| "Favorites" \\| "IssueTags" \\| "ClassificationsAndProperties" \\| "ProjectInfo" \\| "ProjectPreferences" \\| "ProjectLibraryList"[] |  | Object sets to reserve/release: needed before editing attributes (e.g. 'Composites' before modifying composites, 'LayerSettings' for layers), favorites, project info or project preferences in Teamwork |
| `hotlinkCacheManagement` | boolean |  | Also reserve/release Hotlink Cache Management (needed to update/relink hotlinks) |
| `enableDialogs` | boolean |  | Let Archicad show its own Teamwork dialogs (e.g. request/conflict messages). Default false |

### `release_elements`

Teamwork: releases reserved elements and/or object sets so others can edit them. Archicad sends your pending changes of the released items with the release (call teamwork_send first to send everything with a comment). Output: {isTeamwork: true, elements?: [{guid, status, released} | {error}], objectSets?: [{name, status, released, apiError?}], warning?}. In a solo (non-Teamwork) project this does nothing and returns {isTeamwork: false, message} — that is not an error: everything is editable without reservation.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | string \\| object[] |  | Elements to reserve/release (GUIDs) |
| `objectSets` | "LayerSettings" \\| "LineTypes" \\| "FillTypes" \\| "Composites" \\| "PenSets" \\| "Surfaces" \\| "BuildingMaterials" \\| "ZoneCategories" \\| "Profiles" \\| "MEPSystems" \\| "OperationProfiles" \\| "ModelViewOptions" \\| "Favorites" \\| "IssueTags" \\| "ClassificationsAndProperties" \\| "ProjectInfo" \\| "ProjectPreferences" \\| "ProjectLibraryList"[] |  | Object sets to reserve/release: needed before editing attributes (e.g. 'Composites' before modifying composites, 'LayerSettings' for layers), favorites, project info or project preferences in Teamwork |
| `hotlinkCacheManagement` | boolean |  | Also reserve/release Hotlink Cache Management (needed to update/relink hotlinks) |
| `enableDialogs` | boolean |  | Let Archicad show its own Teamwork dialogs (e.g. request/conflict messages). Default false |

### `get_issues`

Lists the issues of the Issue Manager (Archicad markup entries = BCF topics). Output: {issues: [{guid, name, parentGuid?, parentName?, childIssues?, created, modified (ISO 8601 UTC), tagText, tagTextVisible, tagTextElement?, commentCount, comments?, attachedElementCounts: {creation, highlight, deletion, modification}, attachedElements?: {creation: [guid], ...}}], count, elementAttachment? ('highlighted'|'corrected' when filtered by element), total/offset/hasMore when paginated}. Issue GUIDs from here are accepted by every other issue tool.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `issues` | string \\| object[] |  | Only these issues (GUIDs or exact names); default: all issues |
| `element` | any \\| object |  | Only the issues this element is attached to |
| `element.guid` | any | yes |  |
| `search` | string |  | Case-insensitive substring of the issue name or tag text |
| `includeComments` | boolean |  | Include the full comment list of each issue (default false; commentCount is always returned) |
| `includeElements` | boolean |  | Include the attached element GUIDs by attachment type (default false; counts are always returned) |
| `offset` | integer |  | Skip this many results |
| `limit` | integer |  | Return at most this many results (default 500) |

### `create_issue`

Creates one or more issues in the Issue Manager (one undo step), optionally with comments and attached elements — use it to report problems found in the model (clashes, missing data, review notes) so the user sees them in Archicad and can export them to BCF. Output: {results: [{guid, name, comments: [commentGuid], attached?: {highlight?|creation?|deletion?|modification?: {attached: [guid], missing?, modificationPairs?}}} | {error}]} in input order.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `issues` | object[] | yes | Issues to create |
| `issues[].name` | string | yes | Issue name (title) shown in the Issue Manager |
